Authors: Yihao Wang, Yang Miao, Wenshuai Zhao, Wenyan Yang, Zihan Wang, Joni Pajarinen, Luc Van Gool, Danda Pani Paudel, Juho Kannala, Xi Wang, Arno Solin

VideoWeaver: Multimodal Multi-View Video-to-Video Transfer for Embodied Agents

Abstract (English): Recent progress in video-to-video (V2V) translation has enabled realistic resimulation of embodied AI demonstrations, a capability that allows pretrained robot policies to be transferable to new environments without additional data collection. However, prior works can only operate on a single view at a time, while embodied AI tasks are commonly captured from multiple synchronized cameras to support policy learning. Naively applying single-view models independently to each camera leads to inconsistent appearance across views, and standard transformer architectures do not scale to multi-view settings due to the quadratic cost of cross-view attention. We present VideoWeaver, the first multimodal multi-view V2V translation framework. VideoWeaver is initially trained as a single-view flow-based V2V model. To achieve an extension to the multi-view regime, we propose to ground all views in a shared 4D latent space derived from a feed-forward spatial foundation model, namely, Pi3. This encourages view-consistent appearance even under wide baselines and dynamic camera motion. To scale beyond a fixed number of cameras, we train views at distinct diffusion timesteps, enabling the model to learn both joint and conditional view distributions. This in turn allows autoregressive synthesis of new viewpoints conditioned on existing ones. Experiments show superior or similar performance to the state-of-the-art on the single-view translation benchmarks and, for the first time, physically and stylistically consistent multi-view translations, including challenging egocentric and heterogeneous-camera setups central to world randomization for robot learning.

摘要（中文）: 视频到视频 (V2V) 转换的最新进展使得具身人工智能演示的逼真重模拟成为可能。这种能力使得预训练的机器人策略可以在无需额外数据收集的情况下迁移到新的环境中。然而，先前的工作一次只能处理单个视角，而具身人工智能任务通常是从多个同步摄像机捕获的，以支持策略学习。简单地将单视角模型独立应用于每个摄像机会导致视角间外观不一致，并且由于跨视角注意力的二次代价，标准的 Transformer 架构无法扩展到多视角设置。 我们提出了 VideoWeaver，这是第一个多模态多视角 V2V 转换框架。VideoWeaver 最初被训练成单视角的基于流的 V2V 模型。为了扩展到多视角领域，我们提出将所有视角置于从前馈空间基础模型 Pi3 导出的共享 4D 潜在空间中。这鼓励了即使在宽基线和动态相机运动下，视角间外观的一致性。为了超越固定数量的摄像机，我们在不同的扩散时间步长训练视角，使模型能够学习联合和条件视图分布。反过来，这允许根据现有视点进行新的视点的自回归合成。 实验表明，在单视角转换基准测试上，VideoWeaver 表现出优越或与最先进水平相当的性能，并且首次实现了物理和风格上一致的多视角转换，包括对机器人学习的世界随机化至关重要的具有挑战性的自我中心和异构相机设置。

TLDR: VideoWeaver introduces a multimodal, multi-view video-to-video translation framework for embodied AI, achieving view-consistent and scalable video generation across multiple camera angles and heterogeneous setups, potentially benefiting robot learning through world randomization.

简要说明: VideoWeaver 提出了一个用于具身人工智能的多模态、多视角视频到视频翻译框架，实现了跨多个摄像机角度和异构设置的视角一致且可扩展的视频生成，可能通过世界随机化来促进机器人学习。

Authors: George Eskandar, Fengyi Shen, Mohammad Altillawi, Dong Chen, Yang Bai, Liudi Yang, Ziyuan Liu

SportSkills: Physical Skill Learning from Sports Instructional Videos

Abstract (English): Current large-scale video datasets focus on general human activity, but lack depth of coverage on fine-grained activities needed to address physical skill learning. We introduce SportSkills, the first large-scale sports dataset geared towards physical skill learning with in-the-wild video. SportSkills has more than 360k instructional videos containing more than 630k visual demonstrations paired with instructional narrations explaining the know-how behind the actions from 55 varied sports. Through a suite of experiments, we show that SportSkills unlocks the ability to understand fine-grained differences between physical actions. Our representation achieves gains of up to 4x with the same model trained on traditional activity-centric datasets. Crucially, building on SportSkills, we introduce the first large-scale task formulation of mistake-conditioned instructional video retrieval, bridging representation learning and actionable feedback generation (e.g., "here's my execution of a skill; which video clip should I watch to improve it?"). Formal evaluations by professional coaches show our retrieval approach significantly advances the ability of video models to personalize visual instructions for a user query.

摘要（中文）: 当前的大规模视频数据集侧重于一般的人类活动，但在解决身体技能学习所需的细粒度活动方面缺乏深度覆盖。我们推出了SportSkills，这是首个面向野外视频的身体技能学习的大型体育数据集。SportSkills包含超过36万个教学视频，其中包含超过63万个视觉演示，并配有讲解动作背后知识的指导性叙述，涵盖55种不同的运动项目。通过一系列实验，我们表明SportSkills能够理解物理动作之间细微的差异。相同的模型在SportSkills上训练后，其表征能力比在传统的以活动为中心的数据集上训练后提升高达4倍。关键的是，基于SportSkills，我们首次提出了大规模的错误条件教学视频检索任务，将表征学习与可操作的反馈生成联系起来（例如，“这是我对一项技能的执行；我应该看哪个视频片段来改进它？”）。专业教练进行的正式评估表明，我们的检索方法显著提高了视频模型为用户查询个性化视觉指导的能力。

TLDR: The paper introduces SportSkills, a large-scale sports instructional video dataset for physical skill learning and develops a mistake-conditioned instructional video retrieval task, showing improvements in understanding fine-grained actions and personalized instruction.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一个名为SportSkills的大规模体育教学视频数据集，用于物理技能学习，并开发了一个基于错误条件的教学视频检索任务，展示了在理解细粒度动作和个性化指导方面的改进。

Authors: Kumar Ashutosh, Chi Hsuan Wu, Kristen Grauman

MSRL: Scaling Generative Multimodal Reward Modeling via Multi-Stage Reinforcement Learning

Abstract (English): Recent advances in multimodal reward modeling have been largely driven by a paradigm shift from discriminative to generative approaches. Building on this progress, recent studies have further employed reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) to enhance multimodal reward models (MRMs). Despite their success, RLVR-based training typically relies on labeled multimodal preference data, which are costly and labor-intensive to obtain, making it difficult to scale MRM training. To overcome this limitation, we propose a Multi-Stage Reinforcement Learning (MSRL) approach, which can achieve scalable RL for MRMs with limited multimodal data. MSRL replaces the conventional RLVR-based training paradigm by first learning a generalizable reward reasoning capability from large-scale textual preference data, and then progressively transferring this capability to multimodal tasks through caption-based and fully multimodal reinforcement-learning stages. Furthermore, we introduce a cross-modal knowledge distillation approach to improve preference generalization within MSRL. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MSRL effectively scales the RLVR-based training of generative MRMs and substantially improves their performance across both visual understanding and visual generation tasks (e.g., from 66.6% to 75.9% on VL-RewardBench and from 70.2% to 75.7% on GenAI-Bench), without requiring additional multimodal preference annotations. Our code is available at: https://github.com/wangclnlp/MSRL.

摘要（中文）: 多模态奖励建模的最新进展很大程度上得益于从判别式到生成式方法的范式转变。在此基础上，近期的研究进一步采用了基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）来增强多模态奖励模型（MRM）。尽管它们取得了成功，但基于RLVR的训练通常依赖于带标签的多模态偏好数据，获取这些数据成本高昂且劳动密集，这使得扩展MRM训练变得困难。为了克服这一局限性，我们提出了一种多阶段强化学习（MSRL）方法，该方法可以在有限的多模态数据下实现可扩展的MRM强化学习。MSRL用首先从大规模文本偏好数据中学习可泛化的奖励推理能力，然后通过基于标题的和完全多模态强化学习阶段逐步将此能力转移到多模态任务来取代传统的基于RLVR的训练范式。此外，我们引入了一种跨模态知识蒸馏方法来提高MSRL中的偏好泛化能力。大量实验表明，MSRL有效地扩展了生成式MRM的基于RLVR的训练，并显著提高了其在视觉理解和视觉生成任务中的性能（例如，在VL-RewardBench上从66.6%提高到75.9%，在GenAI-Bench上从70.2%提高到75.7%），而无需额外的多模态偏好标注。我们的代码可在https://github.com/wangclnlp/MSRL获取。

TLDR: The paper proposes Multi-Stage Reinforcement Learning (MSRL) to scale multimodal reward model training by leveraging textual preference data and progressive transfer learning, achieving improved performance on visual understanding and generation tasks without additional multimodal annotations.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种多阶段强化学习(MSRL)方法，通过利用文本偏好数据和渐进式迁移学习来扩展多模态奖励模型训练，在视觉理解和生成任务上取得了更好的性能，而不需要额外的多模态注释。

Authors: Chenglong Wang, Yifu Huo, Yang Gan, Qiaozhi He, Qi Meng, Bei Li, Yan Wang, Junfu Liu, Tianhua Zhou, Jingbo Zhu, Tong Xiao

Visual Attention Drifts,but Anchors Hold:Mitigating Hallucination in Multimodal Large Language Models via Cross-Layer Visual Anchors

Abstract (English): Multimodal Large Language Models often suffer from object hallucination. While existing research utilizes attention enhancement and visual retracing, we find these works lack sufficient interpretability regarding attention drift in final model stages. In this paper, we investigate the layer wise evolution of visual features and discover that hallucination stems from deep layer attention regressing toward initial visual noise from early layers. We observe that output reliability depends on acquiring visual anchors at intermediate layers rather than final layers. Based on these insights, we propose CLVA, which stands for Cross-Layer Visual Anchors, a training free method that reinforces critical mid layer features while suppressing regressive noise. This approach effectively pulls deep layer attention back to correct visual regions by utilizing essential anchors captured from attention dynamics. We evaluate our method across diverse architectures and benchmarks, demonstrating outstanding performance without significant increase in computational time and GPU memory.

摘要（中文）: 多模态大型语言模型常常遭受对象幻觉问题。虽然现有研究利用注意力增强和视觉回溯，但我们发现这些方法在最终模型阶段对于注意力漂移缺乏足够的解释性。在本文中，我们研究了视觉特征的逐层演化，并发现幻觉源于深层注意力向早期图层的初始视觉噪声回归。我们观察到，输出的可靠性取决于在中间层而不是最终层获取视觉锚点。基于这些见解，我们提出了CLVA，即跨层视觉锚点，这是一种无需训练的方法，可以强化关键的中间层特征，同时抑制回归噪声。 该方法通过利用从注意力动态中捕获的关键锚点，有效地将深层注意力拉回到正确的视觉区域。 我们在不同的架构和基准测试中评估了我们的方法，证明了出色的性能，而没有显着增加计算时间和GPU内存。

TLDR: This paper identifies that object hallucination in Multimodal LLMs arises from attention drift towards initial visual noise and proposes a training-free method, CLVA, using cross-layer visual anchors to mitigate this issue by reinforcing mid-layer features.

简要说明: 本文指出多模态LLM中的对象幻觉源于注意力向初始视觉噪声的漂移，并提出了一种无需训练的方法CLVA，利用跨层视觉锚点通过加强中间层特征来缓解这个问题。

Authors: Chengxu Yang, Jingling Yuan, Chuang Hu, Jiawei Jiang

Bridging Perception and Reasoning: Token Reweighting for RLVR in Multimodal LLMs

Abstract (English): Extending Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) to multimodal large language models (MLLMs) faces a fundamental challenge: their responses inherently interleave perception-related tokens, which ground visual content, with reasoning-related tokens, which construct reasoning chains. These token types instantiate distinct yet interdependent capacities -- visual grounding and symbolic reasoning -- making isolated optimization insufficient. Through token-level empirical analysis, we demonstrate that optimizing either perception- or reasoning-only tokens consistently underperforms full optimization, underscoring their inherent coupling. To address this, we propose a plug-and-play Token-Reweighting (ToR) strategy that explicitly models this interdependence by identifying critical tokens of both types and dynamically reweighting them during RLVR training. Applied on top of existing methods (e.g., GRPO and DAPO), ToR delivers consistent performance gains across multiple multi-modal reasoning benchmarks, achieving state-of-the-art performance with both accurate visual grounding and coherent reasoning.

摘要（中文）: 将可验证回报的强化学习(RLVR)扩展到多模态大型语言模型(MLLM)面临着一个根本性的挑战：它们的响应天生就交织着感知相关的token（用于锚定视觉内容）和推理相关的token（用于构建推理链）。这些token类型实例化了不同但又相互依赖的能力——视觉锚定和符号推理——这使得孤立的优化是不充分的。通过token级别的实证分析，我们证明了仅优化感知或推理相关的token始终不如全面优化，这突显了它们固有的耦合性。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种即插即用的Token重加权(ToR)策略，该策略通过识别两种类型的关键token并在RLVR训练期间动态地重新加权来显式地建模这种相互依赖性。应用于现有方法（例如，GRPO和DAPO）之上，ToR在多个多模态推理基准测试中提供了持续的性能提升，通过准确的视觉锚定和连贯的推理实现了最先进的性能。

TLDR: This paper introduces a Token-Reweighting (ToR) strategy for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) in multimodal LLMs, addressing the interdependence of perception and reasoning tokens to improve performance on multimodal reasoning tasks.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种针对多模态LLM中可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）的Token-Reweighting（ToR）策略，通过解决感知和推理token的相互依赖性，从而提升多模态推理任务的性能。

Authors: Jinda Lu, Junkang Wu, Jinghan Li, Kexin Huang, Shuo Yang, Guoyin Wang, Jiancan Wu, Xiang Wang, Xiangnan He

VideoTIR: Accurate Understanding for Long Videos with Efficient Tool-Integrated Reasoning

Abstract (English): Existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often suffer from hallucinations in long video understanding (LVU), primarily due to the imbalance between textual and visual tokens. Observing that MLLMs handle short visual inputs well, recent LVU works alleviate hallucinations by automatically parsing the vast visual data into manageable segments that can be effectively processed by MLLMs. SFT-based tool-calling methods can serve this purpose, but they typically require vast amounts of fine-grained, high-quality data and suffer from constrained tool-calling trajectories. We propose a novel VideoTIR that leverages Reinforcement Learning (RL) to encourage proper usage of comprehensive multi-level toolkits for efficient long video understanding. VideoTIR explores both Zero-RL and SFT cold-starting to enable MLLMs to retrieve and focus on meaningful video segments/images/regions, enhancing long video understanding both accurately and efficiently. To reduce redundant tool-calling, we propose Toolkit Action Grouped Policy Optimization (TAGPO), which enhances the efficiency of the calling process through stepwise reward assignment and reuse of failed rollouts. Additionally, we develop a sandbox-based trajectory synthesis framework to generate high-quality trajectories data. Extensive experiments on three long-video QA benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of our method.

摘要（中文）: 现有的多模态大型语言模型(MLLM)在长视频理解(LVU)中经常出现幻觉，这主要是由于文本和视觉token之间的不平衡造成的。 观察到MLLM能够很好地处理短视频输入，最近的LVU工作通过自动将海量视觉数据解析为易于管理的片段来缓解幻觉，从而使MLLM能够有效地处理这些片段。 基于SFT的工具调用方法可以实现这个目标，但它们通常需要大量的细粒度、高质量数据，并且受到约束的工具调用轨迹的限制。 我们提出了一种新颖的VideoTIR，它利用强化学习(RL)来鼓励对全面的多层级工具包进行适当的使用，从而实现高效的长视频理解。 VideoTIR探索了Zero-RL和基于SFT的冷启动，使MLLM能够检索并专注于有意义的视频片段/图像/区域，从而准确有效地增强长视频理解。 为了减少冗余的工具调用，我们提出了工具包行动分组策略优化(TAGPO)，它通过逐步奖励分配和失败轨迹的重用来提高调用过程的效率。 此外，我们开发了一种基于沙箱的轨迹合成框架来生成高质量的轨迹数据。 在三个长视频问答基准上的大量实验证明了我们方法的有效性和效率。

TLDR: VideoTIR utilizes reinforcement learning and tool-integrated reasoning to improve the accuracy and efficiency of long video understanding in Multimodal Large Language Models, addressing the hallucination problem by intelligently parsing and focusing on relevant video segments.

简要说明: VideoTIR 利用强化学习和工具集成推理来提高多模态大型语言模型中长视频理解的准确性和效率。它通过智能地解析和关注相关的视频片段，解决了幻觉问题。

Authors: Zhe Gao, Shiyu Shen, Taifeng Chai, Weinong Wang, Haotian Xu, Xing W, Wenbin Li, Qi Fan, Yang Gao, Dacheng Tao

Beyond Attention Magnitude: Leveraging Inter-layer Rank Consistency for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models

Abstract (English): Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel in robotic manipulation but suffer from significant inference latency due to processing dense visual tokens. Existing token reduction methods predominantly rely on attention magnitude as a static selection. In this work, we challenge this assumption, revealing that high-attention tokens are task-dependent and can even degrade policy performance. To address this, we introduce \textbf{TIES} (\textbf{T}au-guided \textbf{I}nter-layer \textbf{E}fficient \textbf{S}election), a dynamic framework guided by inter-layer token ranking consistency. By adaptively balancing attention magnitude with ranking consistency, TIES ensures robust token selection without requiring additional training. On the CogACT + SIMPLER benchmark, TIES improves average success rates by 6\% while reducing token usage by 78\%, and demonstrate strong generalization across diverse decoders and benchmarks.

摘要（中文）: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人操作方面表现出色，但由于处理密集视觉标记而导致推理延迟显著。现有的标记缩减方法主要依赖于注意力大小作为静态选择依据。在这项工作中，我们对这一假设提出了挑战，揭示了高注意力标记是任务相关的，甚至可能会降低策略性能。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了 \textbf{TIES} (\textbf{T}au引导的\textbf{I}层间\textbf{E}高效\textbf{S}选择)，一个由层间标记排序一致性指导的动态框架。通过自适应地平衡注意力大小与排序一致性，TIES 确保了鲁棒的标记选择，且无需额外的训练。在 CogACT + SIMPLER 基准上，TIES 将平均成功率提高了 6%，同时将标记使用量减少了 78%，并展示了在各种解码器和基准测试中强大的泛化能力。

TLDR: This paper introduces TIES, a novel token selection framework for Vision-Language-Action models that leverages inter-layer rank consistency to improve efficiency and performance by adaptively balancing attention magnitude, achieving significant token reduction and success rate improvements without additional training.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为TIES的新型视觉-语言-动作模型的令牌选择框架，该框架利用层间排序一致性，通过自适应平衡注意力大小来提高效率和性能，无需额外训练即可显著减少令牌使用并提高成功率。

Authors: Peiju Liu, Jinming Liu, Xipeng Qiu, Xuanjing Huang

DCARL: A Divide-and-Conquer Framework for Autoregressive Long-Trajectory Video Generation

Abstract (English): Long-trajectory video generation is a crucial yet challenging task for world modeling primarily due to the limited scalability of existing video diffusion models (VDMs). Autoregressive models, while offering infinite rollout, suffer from visual drift and poor controllability. To address these issues, we propose DCARL, a novel divide-and-conquer, autoregressive framework that effectively combines the structural stability of the divide-and-conquer scheme with the high-fidelity generation of VDMs. Our approach first employs a dedicated Keyframe Generator trained without temporal compression to establish long-range, globally consistent structural anchors. Subsequently, an Interpolation Generator synthesizes the dense frames in an autoregressive manner with overlapping segments, utilizing the keyframes for global context and a single clean preceding frame for local coherence. Trained on a large-scale internet long trajectory video dataset, our method achieves superior performance in both visual quality (lower FID and FVD) and camera adherence (lower ATE and ARE) compared to state-of-the-art autoregressive and divide-and-conquer baselines, demonstrating stable and high-fidelity generation for long trajectory videos up to 32 seconds in length.

摘要（中文）: 长时程视频生成是世界建模领域一项关键但极具挑战的任务，这主要是由于现有视频扩散模型(VDMs)的可扩展性受限。自回归模型虽然能够无限延伸，但存在视觉漂移和可控性差的问题。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种新的分而治之的自回归框架DCARL，它有效地结合了分而治之方案的结构稳定性与VDMs的高保真生成能力。我们的方法首先采用一个专门的关键帧生成器，该生成器在没有时间压缩的情况下进行训练，以建立长程、全局一致的结构锚点。随后，一个插帧生成器通过重叠片段以自回归的方式合成密集帧，利用关键帧来提供全局上下文，并利用单个干净的前置帧来保证局部连贯性。我们的方法在一个大规模互联网长时程视频数据集上进行训练，与最先进的自回归和分而治之基线模型相比，在视觉质量（更低的FID和FVD）和相机一致性（更低的ATE和ARE）方面均取得了更优的性能，证明了其能够对长达32秒的长时程视频进行稳定和高保真的生成。

TLDR: The paper introduces DCARL, a divide-and-conquer autoregressive framework for generating high-fidelity, long-trajectory videos by combining keyframe generation with autoregressive interpolation.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为DCARL的分割征服自回归框架，通过结合关键帧生成和自回归插值，生成高保真、长轨迹视频。

Authors: Junyi Ouyang, Wenbin Teng, Gonglin Chen, Yajie Zhao, Haiwei Chen

LLaVA-LE: Large Language-and-Vision Assistant for Lunar Exploration

Abstract (English): Recent advances in multimodal vision-language models (VLMs) have enabled joint reasoning over visual and textual information, yet their application to planetary science remains largely unexplored. A key hindrance is the absence of large-scale datasets that pair real planetary imagery with detailed scientific descriptions. In this work, we introduce LLaVA-LE (Large Language-and-Vision Assistant for Lunar Exploration), a vision-language model specialized for lunar surface and subsurface characterization. To enable this capability, we curate a new large-scale multimodal lunar dataset, LUCID (LUnar Caption Image Dataset) consisting of 96k high-resolution panchromatic images paired with detailed captions describing lunar terrain characteristics, and 81k question-answer (QA) pairs derived from approximately 20k images in the LUCID dataset. Leveraging this dataset, we fine-tune LLaVA using a two-stage training curriculum: (1) concept alignment for domain-specific terrain description, and (2) instruction-tuned visual question answering. We further design evaluation benchmarks spanning multiple levels of reasoning complexity relevant to lunar terrain analysis. Evaluated against GPT and Gemini judges, LLaVA-LE achieves a 3.3x overall performance gain over Base LLaVA and 2.1x over our Stage 1 model, with a reasoning score of 1.070, exceeding the judge's own reference score, highlighting the effectiveness of domain-specific multimodal data and instruction tuning to advance VLMs in planetary exploration. Code is available at https://github.com/OSUPCVLab/LLaVA-LE.

摘要（中文）: 多模态视觉语言模型(VLMs)的最新进展使得能够对视觉和文本信息进行联合推理，但它们在行星科学中的应用在很大程度上仍未被探索。一个关键的阻碍因素是缺乏将真实的行星图像与详细科学描述配对的大规模数据集。在这项工作中，我们推出了LLaVA-LE（月球勘探的大型语言和视觉助手），一种专门用于月球表面和次表面特征分析的视觉语言模型。为了实现这一能力，我们整理了一个新的大规模多模态月球数据集LUCID（月球图像字幕数据集），该数据集包含96k张与描述月球地形特征的详细字幕配对的高分辨率全色图像，以及从LUCID数据集中大约20k张图像衍生的81k个问答（QA）对。利用该数据集，我们使用两阶段训练课程对LLaVA进行微调：（1）用于领域特定地形描述的概念对齐，以及（2）指令调整的视觉问答。我们进一步设计了涵盖与月球地形分析相关的多个推理复杂程度的评估基准。针对GPT和Gemini评判者进行评估，LLaVA-LE在Base LLaVA的基础上实现了3.3倍的总体性能提升，在我们第一阶段模型的基础上实现了2.1倍的提升，推理得分为1.070，超过了评判者自身的参考得分，突出了领域特定的多模态数据和指令调整在推动行星勘探中VLMs发展方面的有效性。代码可在https://github.com/OSUPCVLab/LLaVA-LE获取。

TLDR: The paper introduces LLaVA-LE, a vision-language model specialized for lunar exploration, fine-tuned on a new large-scale lunar image dataset (LUCID). It demonstrates significant performance gains over baseline LLaVA and even exceeds human judge performance in lunar terrain analysis.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了LLaVA-LE，一个专门用于月球探测的视觉语言模型，它在一个新的大规模月球图像数据集(LUCID)上进行了微调。 结果表明，该模型在月球地形分析方面的性能比基线LLaVA有了显著提高，甚至超过了人类评判者的表现。

Authors: Gokce Inal, Pouyan Navard, Alper Yilmaz

KitchenTwin: Semantically and Geometrically Grounded 3D Kitchen Digital Twins

Abstract (English): Embodied AI training and evaluation require object-centric digital twin environments with accurate metric geometry and semantic grounding. Recent transformer-based feedforward reconstruction methods can efficiently predict global point clouds from sparse monocular videos, yet these geometries suffer from inherent scale ambiguity and inconsistent coordinate conventions. This mismatch prevents the reliable fusion of these dimensionless point cloud predictions with locally reconstructed object meshes. We propose a novel scale-aware 3D fusion framework that registers visually grounded object meshes with transformer-predicted global point clouds to construct metrically consistent digital twins. Our method introduces a Vision-Language Model (VLM)-guided geometric anchor mechanism that resolves this fundamental coordinate mismatch by recovering an accurate real-world metric scale. To fuse these networks, we propose a geometry-aware registration pipeline that explicitly enforces physical plausibility through gravity-aligned vertical estimation, Manhattan-world structural constraints, and collision-free local refinement. Experiments on real indoor kitchen environments demonstrate improved cross-network object alignment and geometric consistency for downstream tasks, including multi-primitive fitting and metric measurement. We additionally introduce an open-source indoor digital twin dataset with metrically scaled scenes and semantically grounded and registered object-centric mesh annotations.

摘要（中文）: 具身人工智能的训练和评估需要以对象为中心的数字孪生环境，这些环境具有精确的度量几何和语义基础。 近期基于Transformer的前馈重建方法可以有效地从稀疏单目视频预测全局点云，但这些几何体存在固有的尺度歧义和不一致的坐标约定。这种不匹配阻碍了将这些无量纲的点云预测与局部重建的对象网格进行可靠融合。 我们提出了一种新颖的尺度感知三维融合框架，将视觉接地的对象网格与Transformer预测的全局点云进行配准，以构建度量一致的数字孪生体。 我们的方法引入了一种视觉-语言模型（VLM）引导的几何锚定机制，通过恢复精确的真实世界度量尺度来解决这种根本的坐标不匹配问题。 为了融合这些网络，我们提出了一种几何感知配准流程，通过重力对齐的垂直方向估计、曼哈顿世界结构约束和无碰撞局部细化，明确地强化物理合理性。 在真实室内厨房环境上的实验表明，下游任务（包括多基元拟合和度量测量）的对象对齐和几何一致性得到了改善。 此外，我们还引入了一个开源的室内数字孪生数据集，该数据集具有度量缩放的场景和语义基础且已配准的以对象为中心的网格标注。

TLDR: The paper presents a novel framework, KitchenTwin, that fuses transformer-predicted point clouds and visually grounded object meshes to create metrically consistent 3D kitchen digital twins, addressing scale ambiguity and coordinate convention inconsistencies using VLM-guided geometric anchors and geometry-aware registration.

简要说明: 本文提出了一个名为KitchenTwin的新框架，它融合了基于 Transformer 预测的点云和视觉接地的物体网格，创建了度量一致的 3D 厨房数字孪生模型。该框架利用 VLM 引导的几何锚点和几何感知注册，解决了尺度模糊和坐标系不一致的问题。

Authors: Quanyun Wu, Kyle Gao, Daniel Long, David A. Clausi, Jonathan Li, Yuhao Chen

ReDiPrune: Relevance-Diversity Pre-Projection Token Pruning for Efficient Multimodal LLMs

Abstract (English): Recent multimodal large language models are computationally expensive because Transformers must process a large number of visual tokens. We present \textbf{ReDiPrune}, a training-free token pruning method applied before the vision-language projector, where visual features remain rich and discriminative. Unlike post-projection pruning methods that operate on compressed representations, ReDiPrune selects informative tokens directly from vision encoder outputs, preserving fine-grained spatial and semantic cues. Each token is scored by a lightweight rule that jointly consider text-conditioned relevance and max-min diversity, ensuring the selected tokens are both query-relevant and non-redundant. ReDiPrune is fully plug-and-play, requiring no retraining or architectural modifications, and can be seamlessly inserted between the encoder and projector. Across four video and five image benchmarks, it consistently improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off. For example, on EgoSchema with LLaVA-NeXT-Video-7B, retaining only 15\% of visual tokens yields a +2.0\% absolute accuracy gain while reducing computation by more than $6\times$ in TFLOPs. Code is available at https://github.com/UA-CVML/ReDiPrune.

摘要（中文）: 近期的多模态大型语言模型计算成本高昂，因为Transformer需要处理大量的视觉token。我们提出了\textbf{ReDiPrune}，一种无须训练的token剪枝方法，应用于视觉-语言投影器之前，此时视觉特征仍然丰富且具有判别性。与在压缩表征上操作的投影后剪枝方法不同，ReDiPrune直接从视觉编码器输出中选择信息量丰富的token，从而保留了细粒度的空间和语义线索。每个token通过一个轻量级的规则进行评分，该规则联合考虑了文本条件下的相关性和最大-最小多样性，从而确保所选token既与查询相关又非冗余。ReDiPrune是完全即插即用的，无需重新训练或架构修改，可以无缝地插入编码器和投影器之间。在四个视频和五个图像基准测试中，它始终提高了准确率-效率的权衡。例如，在EgoSchema上使用LLaVA-NeXT-Video-7B，仅保留15%的视觉token即可获得+2.0%的绝对准确率提升，同时将TFLOPs中的计算量减少6倍以上。代码可在https://github.com/UA-CVML/ReDiPrune获取。

TLDR: ReDiPrune is a training-free token pruning method for efficient multimodal LLMs which operates before the vision-language projector, improving accuracy and reducing computation costs by selectively retaining informative visual tokens based on relevance and diversity.

简要说明: ReDiPrune 是一种用于高效多模态 LLM 的免训练 token 剪枝方法，它在视觉-语言投影器之前运行，通过选择性地保留基于相关性和多样性的信息丰富的视觉 token，从而提高准确性并降低计算成本。

Authors: An Yu, Ting Yu Tsai, Zhenfei Zhang, Weiheng Lu, Felix X. -F. Ye, Ming-Ching Chang

Offline Decision Transformers for Neural Combinatorial Optimization: Surpassing Heuristics on the Traveling Salesman Problem

Abstract (English): Combinatorial optimization problems like the Traveling Salesman Problem are critical in industry yet NP-hard. Neural Combinatorial Optimization has shown promise, but its reliance on online reinforcement learning (RL) hampers deployment and underutilizes decades of algorithmic knowledge. We address these limitations by applying the offline RL framework, Decision Transformer, to learn superior strategies directly from datasets of heuristic solutions; it aims to not only to imitate but to synthesize and outperform them. Concretely, we (i) integrate a Pointer Network to handle the instance-dependent, variable action space of node selection, and (ii) employ expectile regression for optimistic conditioning of Return-to-Go, which is crucial for instances with widely varying optimal values. Experiments show that our method consistently produces higher-quality tours than the four classical heuristics it is trained on, demonstrating the potential of offline RL to unlock and exceed the performance embedded in existing domain knowledge.

摘要（中文）: 摘要： 旅行商问题等组合优化问题在工业界至关重要，但属于NP难问题。神经组合优化已显示出潜力，但其对在线强化学习（RL）的依赖阻碍了部署，并且未能充分利用数十年的算法知识。我们通过应用离线RL框架——决策Transformer，直接从启发式解决方案的数据集中学习更优策略来解决这些限制；其目标不仅是模仿，而是综合并超越这些启发式方法。具体而言，我们（i）集成了一个指针网络来处理节点选择的实例相关、可变动作空间，以及（ii）采用分位数回归对剩余回报（Return-to-Go）进行乐观调节，这对于具有广泛变化的最佳值的实例至关重要。实验表明，我们的方法始终比其训练所用的四种经典启发式方法产生更高质量的路线，展示了离线RL在解锁和超越嵌入在现有领域知识中的性能方面的潜力。

TLDR: The paper applies offline reinforcement learning (Decision Transformer) to the Traveling Salesman Problem, learning from heuristic solutions to outperform them, demonstrating the potential of offline RL for combinatorial optimization.

简要说明: 该论文将离线强化学习（Decision Transformer）应用于旅行商问题，通过学习启发式解法来超越它们，展示了离线强化学习在组合优化中的潜力。

Authors: Hironori Ohigashi, Shinichiro Hamada

Transformers in the Dark: Navigating Unknown Search Spaces via Bandit Feedback

Abstract (English): Effective problem solving with Large Language Models (LLMs) can be enhanced when they are paired with external search algorithms. By viewing the space of diverse ideas and their follow-up possibilities as a tree structure, the search algorithm can navigate such a search space and guide the LLM toward better solutions more efficiently. While the search algorithm enables an effective balance between exploitation and exploration of a tree-structured space, the need for an external component can complicate the overall problem-solving process. We therefore pose the following question: Can LLMs or their underlying Transformer architectures approximate a search algorithm? To answer this question, we first introduce a simplified framework in which tree extensions and feedback signals are externally specified, allowing for controlled evaluation of search capabilities. We call this setting unknown tree search with bandit feedback. Within this setting, we show that Transformers are theoretically expressive enough to implement distinct search strategies and can be trained from scratch to approximate those strategies. Our Transformer models exhibit the possibility of generalizing to unseen conditions such as longer horizons or deeper trees. Furthermore, we demonstrate that continued task-focused training unlocks the complete capabilities of a pretrained LLM, by fine-tuning the LLM on search trajectories.

摘要（中文）: 大型语言模型（LLM）与外部搜索算法结合使用时，可以增强解决问题的效率。 通过将各种想法及其后续可能性对应的空间视为树结构，搜索算法可以导航此类搜索空间，并更高效地引导 LLM 找到更优的解决方案。 虽然搜索算法能够在树状结构的探索和利用之间实现有效的平衡，但对外部组件的需求可能会使整个问题解决过程复杂化。 因此，我们提出以下问题：LLM 或其底层的 Transformer 架构能否近似搜索算法？ 为了回答这个问题，我们首先引入一个简化的框架，其中树扩展和反馈信号是从外部指定的，从而可以对搜索能力进行受控评估。 我们将此设置称为具有bandit反馈的未知树搜索。 在此设置中，我们表明 Transformer 在理论上具有足够的表达能力来实现不同的搜索策略，并且可以从头开始训练以近似这些策略。 我们的 Transformer 模型展示了泛化到未见条件（如更长的视野或更深的树）的可能性。 此外，我们证明了通过在搜索轨迹上微调 LLM，持续的以任务为中心的训练可以释放预训练 LLM 的全部能力。

TLDR: This paper investigates whether Transformers can approximate search algorithms in tree-structured spaces with bandit feedback, finding that they are theoretically expressive enough and trainable to do so, with generalization capabilities.

简要说明: 本文研究了Transformer是否可以在具有bandit反馈的树状结构空间中近似搜索算法，发现它们在理论上具有足够的表达能力，可以通过训练来实现，并具有泛化能力。

Authors: Jungtaek Kim, Thomas Zeng, Ziqian Lin, Minjae Lee, Chungpa Lee, Jy-yong Sohn, Hyung Il Koo, Kangwook Lee

Training LLMs for Multi-Step Tool Orchestration with Constrained Data Synthesis and Graduated Rewards

Abstract (English): Multi-step tool orchestration, where LLMs must invoke multiple dependent APIs in the correct order while propagating intermediate outputs, remains challenging. State-of-the-art models frequently fail on full sequence execution, with parameter value errors accounting for a significant portion of failures. Training models to handle such workflows faces two obstacles: existing environments focus on simple per-turn function calls with simulated data, and binary rewards provide no signal for partial correctness. We present a framework addressing both challenges. First, we construct a reinforcement learning environment backed by a large-scale cache of real API responses, enabling a data synthesis pipeline that samples valid multi-step orchestration traces with controllable complexity and significantly higher generation efficiency than unconstrained methods. Second, we propose a graduated reward design that decomposes correctness into atomic validity (individual function call correctness at increasing granularity) and orchestration (correct tool sequencing with dependency respect). On ComplexFuncBench, our approach demonstrates substantial improvements in turn accuracy. Ablation studies confirm both reward components are essential: using either alone significantly degrades performance.

摘要（中文）: 多步骤工具编排，其中大型语言模型必须以正确的顺序调用多个依赖性API并传递中间输出，仍然具有挑战性。当前最先进的模型经常在完整序列执行中失败，其中参数值错误占了很大比例的失败。训练模型来处理此类工作流程面临两个障碍：现有环境侧重于使用模拟数据的简单单轮函数调用，而二元奖励无法提供部分正确性的信号。 我们提出了一个框架来应对这两个挑战。首先，我们构建了一个由大型真实API响应缓存支持的强化学习环境，从而实现了一个数据合成流水线，该流水线可以对具有可控复杂度的有效多步骤编排轨迹进行采样，并且生成效率比无约束方法显著提高。其次，我们提出了一种分级奖励设计，该设计将正确性分解为原子有效性（以递增粒度为单位的单个函数调用正确性）和编排（遵循依赖关系的正确工具排序）。在ComplexFuncBench上，我们的方法在单轮准确率方面表现出显著提升。消融研究证实，两种奖励成分都至关重要：单独使用任何一种都会显著降低性能。

TLDR: This paper introduces a framework for training LLMs to perform multi-step tool orchestration using constrained data synthesis and graduated rewards, achieving significant improvement on the ComplexFuncBench benchmark.

简要说明: 本文提出了一个框架，通过约束数据合成和分级奖励来训练 LLM 执行多步骤工具编排，并在 ComplexFuncBench 基准测试中取得了显著改进。

Authors: Cheng Jiayang, Xin Liu, Zhihan Zhang, Haoyang Wen, Zixuan Zhang, Qingyu Yin, Shiyang Li, Priyanka Nigam, Bing Yin, Chao Zhang, Yangqiu Song

Intelligent Navigation and Obstacle-Aware Fabrication for Mobile Additive Manufacturing Systems

Abstract (English): As the demand for mass customization increases, manufacturing systems must become more flexible and adaptable to produce personalized products efficiently. Additive manufacturing (AM) enhances production adaptability by enabling on-demand fabrication of customized components directly from digital models, but its flexibility remains constrained by fixed equipment layouts. Integrating mobile robots addresses this limitation by allowing manufacturing resources to move and adapt to changing production requirements. Mobile AM Robots (MAMbots) combine AM with mobile robotics to produce and transport components within dynamic manufacturing environments. However, the dynamic manufacturing environments introduce challenges for MAMbots. Disturbances such as obstacles and uneven terrain can disrupt navigation stability, which in turn affects printing accuracy and surface quality. This work proposes a universal mobile printing-and-delivery platform that couples navigation and material deposition, addressing the limitations of earlier frameworks that treated these processes separately. A real-time control framework is developed to plan and control the robot's navigation, ensuring safe motion, obstacle avoidance, and path stability while maintaining print quality. The closed-loop integration of sensing, mobility, and manufacturing provides real-time feedback for motion and process control, enabling MAMbots to make autonomous decisions in dynamic environments. The framework is validated through simulations and real-world experiments that test its adaptability to trajectory variations and external disturbances. Coupled navigation and printing together enable MAMbots to plan safe, adaptive trajectories, improving flexibility and adaptability in manufacturing.

摘要（中文）: 随着大规模定制需求的增长，制造系统必须变得更加灵活和适应性更强，才能高效地生产个性化产品。增材制造（AM）通过直接从数字模型按需制造定制组件，增强了生产的适应性，但其灵活性仍然受到固定设备布局的限制。集成移动机器人通过允许制造资源移动和适应不断变化的生产需求，解决了这一限制。移动增材制造机器人（MAMbots）将增材制造与移动机器人技术相结合，以在动态制造环境中生产和运输组件。然而，动态制造环境给MAMbots带来了挑战。诸如障碍物和不平坦地形等干扰会扰乱导航稳定性，进而影响打印精度和表面质量。本研究提出了一种通用的移动打印和交付平台，该平台将导航和材料沉积相结合，解决了早期框架将这些过程分开处理的局限性。开发了一种实时控制框架，用于规划和控制机器人的导航，确保安全运动、避障和路径稳定性，同时保持打印质量。传感、移动性和制造的闭环集成为运动和过程控制提供了实时反馈，使MAMbots能够在动态环境中做出自主决策。该框架通过仿真和实际实验进行了验证，测试了其对轨迹变化和外部干扰的适应性。导航和打印的耦合使MAMbots能够规划安全的自适应轨迹，从而提高制造的灵活性和适应性。

TLDR: This paper presents a real-time control framework for mobile additive manufacturing robots (MAMbots) that integrates navigation and material deposition to enable autonomous operation in dynamic environments, validated through simulations and real-world experiments.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种用于移动增材制造机器人 (MAMbot) 的实时控制框架，该框架集成了导航和材料沉积，从而能够在动态环境中自主运行，并通过仿真和实际实验进行了验证。

Authors: Yifei Li, Ruizhe Fu, Huihang Liu, Guha Manogharan, Feng Ju, Ilya Kovalenko

Can Users Specify Driving Speed? Bench2Drive-Speed: Benchmark and Baselines for Desired-Speed Conditioned Autonomous Driving

Abstract (English): End-to-end autonomous driving (E2E-AD) has achieved remarkable progress. However, one practical and useful function has been long overlooked: users may wish to customize the desired speed of the policy or specify whether to allow the autonomous vehicle to overtake. To bridge this gap, we present Bench2Drive-Speed, a benchmark with metrics, dataset, and baselines for desired-speed conditioned autonomous driving. We introduce explicit inputs of users' desired target-speed and overtake/follow instructions to driving policy models. We design quantitative metrics, including Speed-Adherence Score and Overtake Score, to measure how faithfully policies follow user specifications, while remaining compatible with standard autonomous driving metrics. To enable training of speed-conditioned policies, one approach is to collect expert demonstrations that strictly follow speed requirements, an expensive and unscalable process in the real world. An alternative is to adapt existing regular driving data by treating the speed observed in future frames as the target speed for training. To investigate this, we construct CustomizedSpeedDataset, composed of 2,100 clips annotated with experts demonstrations, enabling systematic investigation of supervision strategies. Our experiments show that, under proper re-annotation, models trained on regular driving data perform comparably to on expert demonstrations, suggesting that speed supervision can be introduced without additional complex real-world data collection. Furthermore, we find that while target-speed following can be achieved without degrading regular driving performance, executing overtaking commands remains challenging due to the inherent difficulty of interactive behaviors. All code, datasets and baselines are available at https://github.com/Thinklab-SJTU/Bench2Drive-Speed

摘要（中文）: 端到端自动驾驶（E2E-AD）已取得了显著进展。然而，一项实用且有用的功能长期以来一直被忽视：用户可能希望自定义策略所需的车速，或指定是否允许自动驾驶车辆超车。为了弥补这一差距，我们提出了Bench2Drive-Speed，一个包含指标、数据集和基准线的期望速度条件下的自动驾驶基准。我们引入了用户期望目标速度和超车/跟随指令的显式输入到驾驶策略模型中。我们设计了包括速度一致性得分和超车得分在内的定量指标，以衡量策略对用户规范的忠实程度，同时保持与标准自动驾驶指标的兼容性。为了支持速度条件策略的训练，一种方法是收集严格遵循速度要求的专家演示，但这是现实世界中代价高昂且不可扩展的过程。另一种方法是通过将未来帧中观察到的速度视为训练目标速度来调整现有的常规驾驶数据。为了研究这一点，我们构建了CustomizedSpeedDataset，它由2100个带有专家演示注释的片段组成，从而可以系统地研究监督策略。我们的实验表明，在适当的重新标注下，在常规驾驶数据上训练的模型性能与专家演示模型相当，这表明可以在无需额外复杂现实世界数据收集的情况下引入速度监督。此外，我们发现，虽然在不降低常规驾驶性能的情况下可以实现目标速度跟随，但由于交互行为的固有困难，执行超车命令仍然具有挑战性。所有代码、数据集和基准线都可在https://github.com/Thinklab-SJTU/Bench2Drive-Speed获取。

TLDR: The paper introduces Bench2Drive-Speed, a benchmark for desired-speed conditioned autonomous driving, which allows users to specify target speed and overtaking behavior, and shows that re-annotated standard driving data can be used to train models effectively.

简要说明: 本文介绍了Bench2Drive-Speed，一个用于期望速度条件下的自动驾驶的基准，允许用户指定目标速度和超车行为，并表明重新标注的标准驾驶数据可以有效地用于训练模型。

Authors: Yuqian Shao, Xiaosong Jia, Langechuan Liu, Junchi Yan

Fast-dVLA: Accelerating Discrete Diffusion VLA to Real-Time Performance

Abstract (English): This paper proposes a novel approach to address the challenge that pretrained VLA models often fail to effectively improve performance and reduce adaptation costs during standard supervised finetuning (SFT). Some advanced finetuning methods with auxiliary training objectives can improve performance and reduce the number of convergence steps. However, they typically incur significant computational overhead due to the additional losses from auxiliary tasks. To simultaneously achieve the enhanced capabilities of auxiliary training with the simplicity of standard SFT, we decouple the two objectives of auxiliary task training within the parameter space, namely, enhancing general capabilities and fitting task-specific action distributions. To deliver this goal, we only need to train the model to converge on a small-scale task set using two distinct training strategies. The difference between the resulting model parameters can then be interpreted as capability vectors provided by auxiliary tasks. These vectors are then merged with pretrained parameters to form a capability-enhanced meta model. Moreover, when standard SFT is augmented with a lightweight orthogonal regularization loss, the merged model attains performance comparable to auxiliary finetuned baselines with reduced computational overhead. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach is highly effective across diverse robot tasks. Project page: https://chris1220313648.github.io/Fast-dVLA/

摘要（中文）: 本文提出了一种新颖的方法，旨在解决预训练VLA模型在标准监督微调（SFT）期间，常常无法有效提升性能并降低适配成本的挑战。一些具有辅助训练目标的先进微调方法可以提高性能并减少收敛步数。然而，由于辅助任务带来的额外损失，它们通常会导致显著的计算开销。为了在实现辅助训练增强能力的同时，保持标准SFT的简便性，我们在参数空间中解耦了辅助任务训练的两个目标，即增强通用能力和拟合特定任务的动作分布。为了实现这一目标，我们只需要使用两种不同的训练策略，训练模型在一小规模的任务集上收敛。由此产生的模型参数之间的差异可以被解释为辅助任务提供的能力向量。然后将这些向量与预训练参数合并，形成一个能力增强的元模型。此外，当标准SFT辅以轻量级的正交正则化损失时，合并后的模型在降低计算开销的同时，获得了与辅助微调基线相当的性能。实验结果表明，这种方法在各种机器人任务中都非常有效。项目主页：https://chris1220313648.github.io/Fast-dVLA/

TLDR: The paper introduces Fast-dVLA, a technique to improve the performance and reduce the computational cost of adapting pretrained VLA models for robotic tasks by decoupling auxiliary training objectives and using capability vectors for enhanced finetuning.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了Fast-dVLA，一种通过分离辅助训练目标，并使用能力向量进行增强微调，从而改进VLA预训练模型在机器人任务中的性能并降低计算成本的技术。

A Mentalistic Interface for Probing Folk-Psychological Attribution to Non-Humanoid Robots

Abstract (English): This paper presents an experimental platform for studying intentional-state attribution toward a non-humanoid robot. Authors: Giulio Pisaneschi, Pierpaolo Serio, Estelle Gerbier, Andrea Dan Ryals, Lorenzo Pollini, Mario G. C. A. Cimino

Visualizing Impedance Control in Augmented Reality for Teleoperation: Design and User Evaluation

Abstract (English): Teleoperation for contact-rich manipulation remains challenging, especially when using low-cost, motion-only interfaces that provide no haptic feedback. Virtual reality controllers enable intuitive motion control but do not allow operators to directly perceive or regulate contact forces, limiting task performance. To address this, we propose an augmented reality (AR) visualization of the impedance controller's target pose and its displacement from each robot end effector. This visualization conveys the forces generated by the controller, providing operators with intuitive, real-time feedback without expensive haptic hardware. We evaluate the design in a dual-arm manipulation study with 17 participants who repeatedly reposition a box with and without the AR visualization. Results show that AR visualization reduces completion time by 24% for force-critical lifting tasks, with no significant effect on sliding tasks where precise force control is less critical. These findings indicate that making the impedance target visible through AR is a viable approach to improve human-robot interaction for contact-rich teleoperation.

摘要（中文）: 接触丰富的远端操控仍然具有挑战性，尤其是在使用仅提供运动控制而无触觉反馈的低成本界面时。虚拟现实控制器虽然能实现直观的运动控制，但无法让操作者直接感知或调节接触力，从而限制了任务表现。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种增强现实（AR）可视化方法来呈现阻抗控制器的目标姿态以及其与每个机器人末端执行器的位移。这种可视化能够传递控制器产生的力，从而在无需昂贵的触觉硬件的情况下，为操作者提供直观的实时反馈。我们通过一项双臂操控研究评估了该设计，其中17名参与者在有或没有AR可视化的情况下反复重新定位一个盒子。结果表明，对于力关键的抬升任务，AR可视化将完成时间缩短了24%，而对于需要精确力控制程度较低的滑动任务，则没有显著影响。这些发现表明，通过AR使阻抗目标可见是一种可行的改进接触丰富远端操控人机交互的方法。

TLDR: The paper presents an augmented reality visualization of impedance control in teleoperation, showing improved performance in force-critical tasks by providing real-time feedback on contact forces without haptic hardware.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种远程操作中阻抗控制的增强现实可视化方法，通过提供接触力的实时反馈，在对力敏感的任务中提高了性能，而无需触觉硬件。

Authors: Gijs van den Brandt, Femke van Beek, Elena Torta

System Design for Maintaining Internal State Consistency in Long-Horizon Robotic Tabletop Games

Abstract (English): Long-horizon tabletop games pose a distinct systems challenge for robotics: small perceptual or execution errors can invalidate accumulated task state, propagate across decision-making modules, and ultimately derail interaction. This paper studies how to maintain internal state consistency in turn-based, multi-human robotic tabletop games through deliberate system design rather than isolated component improvement. Using Mahjong as a representative long-horizon setting, we present an integrated architecture that explicitly maintains perceptual, execution, and interaction state, partitions high-level semantic reasoning from time-critical perception and control, and incorporates verified action primitives with tactile-triggered recovery to prevent premature state corruption. We further introduce interaction-level monitoring mechanisms to detect turn violations and hidden-information breaches that threaten execution assumptions. Beyond demonstrating complete-game operation, we provide an empirical characterization of failure modes, recovery effectiveness, cross-module error propagation, and hardware-algorithm trade-offs observed during deployment. Our results show that explicit partitioning, monitored state transitions, and recovery mechanisms are critical for sustaining executable consistency over extended play, whereas monolithic or unverified pipelines lead to measurable degradation in end-to-end reliability. The proposed system serves as an empirical platform for studying system-level design principles in long-horizon, turn-based interaction.

摘要（中文）: 长时程桌面游戏为机器人技术带来独特的系统挑战：微小的感知或执行错误可能导致累积的任务状态失效，并在决策模块间传播，最终破坏交互。本文研究如何在回合制、多人参与的机器人桌面游戏中，通过审慎的系统设计而非孤立的组件改进来维持内部状态一致性。以麻将作为代表性的长时程环境，我们提出了一种集成架构，该架构显式地维护了感知状态、执行状态和交互状态，将高层语义推理与时间关键的感知和控制相分离，并结合了经过验证的动作原语与触觉触发的恢复机制，以防止状态过早损坏。我们进一步引入交互层级的监控机制，以检测违反回合规则和隐藏信息泄露的情况，从而避免威胁执行假设。除了演示完整的游戏操作，我们还提供了部署过程中观察到的失效模式、恢复有效性、跨模块误差传播以及硬件-算法权衡的实证表征。我们的结果表明，显式分区、受监控的状态转换和恢复机制对于在长时间游戏中维持可执行的一致性至关重要，而单片或未经验证的流水线会导致端到端可靠性的显著下降。所提出的系统为研究长时程、回合制交互中的系统级设计原则提供了一个实证平台。

TLDR: The paper presents a system design for robotic tabletop games, particularly Mahjong, focusing on maintaining internal state consistency through explicit partitioning, monitored state transitions, and recovery mechanisms to address the challenges of long-horizon tasks with potential perceptual and execution errors.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种用于机器人桌面游戏（特别是麻将）的系统设计，重点是通过显式划分、监控状态转换和恢复机制来维护内部状态一致性，从而解决具有潜在感知和执行错误的长期任务的挑战。

Authors: Guangyu Zhao, Ceyao Zhang, Chengdong Ma, Tao Wu, Yiyang Song, Haoxuan Ru, Yifan Zhong, Ruilin Yan, Lingfeng Li, Ruochong Li, Yu Li, Xuyuan Han, Yun Ding, Ruizhang Jiang, Xiaochuan Zhang, Yichao Li, Yuanpei Chen, Yaodong Yang, Yitao Liang

UMBRELLA: Uncertainty-aware Multi-robot Reactive Coordination under Dynamic Temporal Logic Tasks

Abstract (English): Multi-robot systems can be extremely efficient for accomplishing team-wise tasks by acting concurrently and collaboratively. However, most existing methods either assume static task features or simply replan when environmental changes occur. This paper addresses the challenging problem of coordinating multi-robot systems for collaborative tasks involving dynamic and moving targets. We explicitly model the uncertainty in target motion prediction via Conformal Prediction(CP), while respecting the spatial-temporal constraints specified by Linear Temporal Logic (LTL). The proposed framework (UMBRELLA) combines the Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) over partial plans with uncertainty-aware rollouts, and introduces a CP-based metric to guide and accelerate the search. The objective is to minimize the Conditional Value at Risk (CVaR) of the average makespan. For tasks released online, a receding-horizon planning scheme dynamically adjusts the assignments based on updated task specifications and motion predictions. Spatial and temporal constraints among the tasks are always ensured, and only partial synchronization is required for the collaborative tasks during online execution. Extensive large-scale simulations and hardware experiments demonstrate substantial reductions in both the average makespan and its variance by 23% and 71%, compared with static baselines.

摘要（中文）: 多机器人系统通过并发和协作执行团队任务，可以展现出极高的效率。然而，现有的大多数方法要么假设静态的任务特征，要么仅在环境变化时进行重新规划。本文旨在解决多机器人系统在协作任务中协调动态和移动目标的难题。我们通过符合预测(CP)显式地对目标运动预测中的不确定性进行建模，同时遵守线性时序逻辑(LTL)指定的时空约束。所提出的框架(UMBRELLA)结合了基于蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)的部分计划与不确定性感知的在线推演，并引入基于CP的度量来指导和加速搜索。目标是最小化平均最大完工时间的条件风险价值(CVaR)。对于在线发布的任务，滚动时域规划方案会根据更新的任务规范和运动预测动态调整分配。任务之间的空间和时间约束始终得到保证，并且在线执行期间协作任务仅需要部分同步。大量的规模化仿真和硬件实验表明，与静态基线相比，平均最大完工时间和其方差分别显著降低了23%和71%。

TLDR: This paper presents UMBRELLA, a framework for multi-robot coordination under dynamic LTL tasks that incorporates uncertainty in target motion prediction using Conformal Prediction and minimizes the Conditional Value at Risk (CVaR) of the makespan.

简要说明: 本文提出了UMBRELLA，一个在动态LTL任务下进行多机器人协同的框架，它利用Conformal Prediction来处理目标运动预测的不确定性，并最小化平均完工时间的条件风险价值(CVaR)。

Authors: Qisheng Zhao, Meng Guo, Hengxuan Du, Lars Lindemann, Zhongkui Li

IntentReact: Guiding Reactive Object-Centric Navigation via Topological Intent

Abstract (English): Object-goal visual navigation requires robots to reason over semantic structure and act effectively under partial observability. Recent approaches based on object-level topological maps enable long-horizon navigation without dense geometric reconstruction, but their execution remains limited by the gap between global topological guidance and local perception-driven control. In particular, local decisions are made solely from the current egocentric observation, without access to information beyond the robot's field of view. As a result, the robot may persist along its current heading even when initially oriented away from the goal, moving toward directions that do not decrease the global topological distance. In this work, we propose IntentReact, an intent-conditioned object-centric navigation framework that introduces a compact interface between global topological planning and reactive object-centric control. Our approach encodes global topological guidance as a low-dimensional directional signal, termed intent, which conditions a learned waypoint prediction policy to bias navigation toward topologically consistent progression. This design enables the robot to promptly reorient when local observations are misleading, guiding motion toward directions that decrease global topological distance while preserving the reactivity and robustness of object-centric control. We evaluate the proposed framework through extensive experiments, demonstrating improved navigation success and execution quality compared to prior object-centric navigation methods.

摘要（中文）: 基于物体目标的视觉导航要求机器人能够理解语义结构，并在部分可观测性下有效行动。最近基于物体级拓扑地图的方法能够实现长程导航，而无需进行密集的几何重建，但其执行仍受到全局拓扑引导和局部感知驱动控制之间的差距的限制。特别是，局部决策仅根据当前的自我中心观测做出，无法获取机器人视野之外的信息。因此，即使机器人最初偏离目标方向，也可能坚持当前航向，朝着不会缩短全局拓扑距离的方向移动。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种意图条件物体中心导航框架IntentReact，该框架在全局拓扑规划和反应式物体中心控制之间引入了一个紧凑的接口。我们的方法将全局拓扑引导编码为一种低维度的方向信号，称为意图，该意图调节一个学习到的航点预测策略，从而使导航偏向拓扑一致的前进。这种设计使机器人在局部观测具有误导性时能够迅速重新定向，引导运动朝着缩短全局拓扑距离的方向前进，同时保持物体中心控制的反应性和鲁棒性。我们通过广泛的实验评估了所提出的框架，结果表明，与之前的物体中心导航方法相比，导航成功率和执行质量均有所提高。

TLDR: The paper introduces IntentReact, a new object-centric navigation framework that uses a low-dimensional 'intent' signal derived from global topological planning to guide local reactive control, improving navigation success and robustness under partial observability.

简要说明: 本文介绍了IntentReact，一种新的以对象为中心的导航框架，它使用来自全局拓扑规划的低维“意图”信号来指导局部反应控制，从而提高在部分可观测情况下的导航成功率和鲁棒性。

Authors: Yanmei Jiao, Anpeng Lu, Wenhan Hu, Rong Xiong, Yue Wang, Huajin Tang, Wen-an Zhang

Connectivity-Aware Representations for Constrained Motion Planning via Multi-Scale Contrastive Learning

Abstract (English): The objective of constrained motion planning is to connect start and goal configurations while satisfying task-specific constraints. Motion planning becomes inefficient or infeasible when the configurations lie in disconnected regions, known as essentially mutually disconnected (EMD) components. Constraints further restrict feasible space to a lower-dimensional submanifold, while redundancy introduces additional complexity because a single end-effector pose admits infinitely many inverse kinematic solutions that may form discrete self-motion manifolds. This paper addresses these challenges by learning a connectivity-aware representation for selecting start and goal configurations prior to planning. Joint configurations are embedded into a latent space through multi-scale manifold learning across neighborhood ranges from local to global, and clustering generates pseudo-labels that supervise a contrastive learning framework. The proposed framework provides a connectivity-aware measure that biases the selection of start and goal configurations in connected regions, avoiding EMDs and yielding higher success rates with reduced planning time. Experiments on various manipulation tasks showed that our method achieves 1.9 times higher success rates and reduces the planning time by a factor of 0.43 compared to baselines.

摘要（中文）: 受约束的运动规划的目标是在满足特定任务约束的同时，连接起始和目标构型。当构型位于不连通的区域时，运动规划会变得低效或不可行，这些区域被称为本质上互不连通（EMD）的分量。约束进一步将可行空间限制到低维子流形，而冗余引入了额外的复杂性，因为单个末端执行器姿态允许无限多个逆运动学解，这些解可能形成离散的自运动流形。本文通过学习一种具有连通性感知的表示，用于在规划之前选择起始和目标构型，从而解决这些挑战。关节构型通过多尺度流形学习嵌入到潜在空间中，学习范围涵盖从局部到全局的邻域范围，聚类生成伪标签，用于监督对比学习框架。所提出的框架提供了一种具有连通性感知的度量，它偏向于在连通区域中选择起始和目标构型，避免EMD，并以更短的规划时间获得更高的成功率。在各种操作任务上的实验表明，与基线方法相比，我们的方法成功率提高了 1.9 倍，并将规划时间缩短了 0.43 倍。

TLDR: This paper introduces a multi-scale contrastive learning approach to learn connectivity-aware representations for motion planning, improving success rates and reducing planning time by selecting start and goal configurations in connected regions.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种多尺度对比学习方法，用于学习运动规划的连通性感知表示，通过选择连通区域中的起始和目标配置，提高成功率并减少规划时间。

Authors: Suhyun Jeon, Yumin Lim, Woo-Jeong Baek, Hyeonseo Kim, Suhan Park, Jaeheung Park

CTS-PLL: A Robust and Anytime Framework for Collaborative Task Sequencing and Multi-Agent Path Finding

Abstract (English): The Collaborative Task Sequencing and Multi-Agent Path Finding (CTS-MAPF) problem requires agents to accomplish sequences of tasks while avoiding collisions, posing significant challenges due to its combinatorial complexity. This work introduces CTS-PLL, a hierarchical framework that extends the configuration-based CTS-MAPF planning paradigm with two key enhancements: a lock agents detection and release mechanism leveraging a complete planning method for local re-planning, and an anytime refinement procedure based on Large Neighborhood Search (LNS). These additions ensure robustness in dense environments and enable continuous improvement of solution quality. Extensive evaluations across sparse and dense benchmarks demonstrate that CTS-PLL achieves higher success rates and solution quality compared with existing methods, while maintaining competitive runtime efficiency. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate the feasibility of the approach in practice.

摘要（中文）: 协作任务排序与多智能体路径规划（CTS-MAPF）问题要求智能体完成一系列任务，同时避免碰撞，其组合复杂性带来了巨大的挑战。 本文提出了一种分层框架CTS-PLL，它通过两项关键改进扩展了基于配置的CTS-MAPF规划范式：一种利用完整规划方法进行局部重新规划的锁定智能体检测与释放机制，以及一种基于大规模邻域搜索（LNS）的随时改进过程。 这些补充确保了在密集环境中的鲁棒性，并能够持续提高解决方案的质量。 在稀疏和密集的 benchmarks 上进行的广泛评估表明，与现有方法相比，CTS-PLL 实现了更高的成功率和解决方案质量，同时保持了具有竞争力的运行时效率。 真实的机器人实验进一步证明了该方法在实践中的可行性。

TLDR: The paper introduces CTS-PLL, a novel framework for Collaborative Task Sequencing and Multi-Agent Path Finding (CTS-MAPF) that leverages lock detection/release and anytime refinement to achieve higher success rates, solution quality, and runtime efficiency in both simulated and real-world settings.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为CTS-PLL的新型框架，用于协作任务排序和多智能体路径规划（CTS-MAPF）。该框架利用锁检测/释放机制和随时优化策略，在模拟和真实环境中实现了更高的成功率、解决方案质量和运行效率。

Authors: Junkai Jiang, Yitao Xu, Ruochen Li, Shaobing Xu, Jianqiang Wang

Learning Rollout from Sampling:An R1-Style Tokenized Traffic Simulation Model

Abstract (English): Learning diverse and high-fidelity traffic simulations from human driving demonstrations is crucial for autonomous driving evaluation. The recent next-token prediction (NTP) paradigm, widely adopted in large language models (LLMs), has been applied to traffic simulation and achieves iterative improvements via supervised fine-tuning (SFT). However, such methods limit active exploration of potentially valuable motion tokens, particularly in suboptimal regions. Entropy patterns provide a promising perspective for enabling exploration driven by motion token uncertainty. Motivated by this insight, we propose a novel tokenized traffic simulation policy, R1Sim, which represents an initial attempt to explore reinforcement learning based on motion token entropy patterns, and systematically analyzes the impact of different motion tokens on simulation outcomes. Specifically, we introduce an entropy-guided adaptive sampling mechanism that focuses on previously overlooked motion tokens with high uncertainty yet high potential. We further optimize motion behaviors using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), guided by a safety-aware reward design. Overall, these components enable a balanced exploration-exploitation trade-off through diverse high-uncertainty sampling and group-wise comparative estimation, resulting in realistic, safe, and diverse multi-agent behaviors. Extensive experiments on the Waymo Sim Agent benchmark demonstrate that R1Sim achieves competitive performance compared to state-of-the-art methods.

摘要（中文）: 从人类驾驶演示中学习多样且高保真的交通模拟对于自动驾驶评估至关重要。最近的下一令牌预测（NTP）范式，被广泛应用于大型语言模型（LLM），已被应用于交通模拟，并通过监督微调（SFT）实现了迭代改进。然而，这些方法限制了对潜在有价值的运动令牌的主动探索，尤其是在次优区域。熵模式为启用由运动令牌不确定性驱动的探索提供了一个有前景的视角。受此启发，我们提出了一种新型的令牌化交通模拟策略R1Sim，这是基于运动令牌熵模式探索强化学习的初步尝试，并系统地分析了不同运动令牌对模拟结果的影响。具体来说，我们引入了一种熵引导的自适应采样机制，该机制侧重于先前被忽视的、具有高不确定性但高潜力的运动令牌。我们进一步利用群体相对策略优化（GRPO）优化运动行为，并以安全感知的奖励设计为指导。总而言之，这些组件通过多样性的高不确定性采样和组间比较估计，实现了平衡的探索-利用权衡，从而产生真实、安全且多样化的多智能体行为。在Waymo Sim Agent基准测试中的大量实验表明，与最先进的方法相比，R1Sim取得了具有竞争力的性能。

TLDR: The paper introduces R1Sim, a novel tokenized traffic simulation policy that uses entropy-guided adaptive sampling and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to improve the realism, safety, and diversity of multi-agent behaviors by exploring previously overlooked motion tokens. Experiments demonstrate competitive performance on the Waymo Sim Agent benchmark.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种新颖的令牌化交通仿真策略R1Sim，它使用熵引导的自适应抽样和组相对策略优化（GRPO）来提高多智能体行为的真实性、安全性和多样性，通过探索以前被忽视的具有高潜力的运动令牌。实验表明，在Waymo Sim Agent基准测试中，取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Authors: Ziyan Wang, Peng Chen, Ding Li, Chiwei Li, Qichao Zhang, Zhongpu Xia, Guizhen Yu

CROSS: A Mixture-of-Experts Reinforcement Learning Framework for Generalizable Large-Scale Traffic Signal Control

Abstract (English): Recent advances in robotics, automation, and artificial intelligence have enabled urban traffic systems to operate with increasing autonomy towards future smart cities, powered in part by the development of adaptive traffic signal control (ATSC), which dynamically optimizes signal phases to mitigate congestion and optimize traffic. However, achieving effective and generalizable large-scale ATSC remains a significant challenge due to the diverse intersection topologies and highly dynamic, complex traffic demand patterns across the network. Existing RL-based methods typically use a single shared policy for all scenarios, whose limited representational capacity makes it difficult to capture diverse traffic dynamics and generalize to unseen environments. To address these challenges, we propose CROSS, a novel Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-based decentralized RL framework for generalizable ATSC. We first introduce a Predictive Contrastive Clustering (PCC) module that forecasts short-term state transitions to identify latent traffic patterns, followed by clustering and contrastive learning to enhance pattern-level representation. We further design a Scenario-Adaptive MoE module that augments a shared policy with multiple experts, thus enabling adaptive specialization and more flexible scenario-specific strategies. We conduct extensive experiments in the SUMO simulator on both synthetic and real-world traffic datasets. Compared with state-of-the-art baselines, CROSS achieves superior performance and generalization through improved representation of diverse traffic scenarios.

摘要（中文）: 摘要： 机器人、自动化和人工智能领域的最新进展使得城市交通系统能够以日益增长的自主性运行，从而迈向未来的智慧城市，这部分得益于自适应交通信号控制（ATSC）的发展，该技术能够动态优化信号相位，以缓解拥堵并优化交通。然而，由于网络中多样化的交叉路口拓扑结构和高度动态、复杂的交通需求模式，实现有效的、可推广的大规模ATSC仍然是一个重大挑战。现有的基于强化学习（RL）的方法通常对所有场景使用单一的共享策略，其有限的表征能力使得难以捕捉多样化的交通动态并推广到未见过的环境。为了应对这些挑战，我们提出CROSS，一种新颖的基于混合专家（MoE）的去中心化RL框架，用于可推广的ATSC。我们首先引入一个预测对比聚类（PCC）模块，该模块预测短期的状态转移，以识别潜在的交通模式，然后进行聚类和对比学习，以增强模式级别的表示。我们进一步设计了一个场景自适应 MoE 模块，该模块使用多个专家来增强共享策略，从而实现自适应专业化和更灵活的特定场景策略。我们在 SUMO 模拟器上，在合成和真实世界交通数据集上进行了广泛的实验。与最先进的基线方法相比，CROSS 通过改进对多样化交通场景的表示，实现了卓越的性能和泛化能力。

TLDR: The paper introduces CROSS, a Mixture-of-Experts Reinforcement Learning framework for adaptive traffic signal control (ATSC) that uses predictive contrastive clustering and scenario-adaptive MoE to improve generalization across diverse traffic scenarios.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种名为CROSS的混合专家强化学习框架，用于自适应交通信号控制（ATSC）。该框架使用预测对比聚类和场景自适应MoE来提高在各种交通场景中的泛化能力。

Authors: Xibei Chen, Yifeng Zhang, Yuxiang Xiao, Mingfeng Fan, Maonan Wang, Guillaume Sartoretti

Integrated Multi-Drone Task Allocation, Sequencing, and Optimal Trajectory Generation in Obstacle-Rich 3D Environments

Abstract (English): Coordinating teams of aerial robots in cluttered three-dimensional (3D) environments requires a principled integration of discrete mission planning-deciding which robot serves which goals and in what order -- with continuous-time trajectory synthesis that enforces collision avoidance and dynamic feasibility. This paper introduces IMD-TAPP (Integrated Multi-Drone Task Allocation and Path Planning), an end-to-end framework that jointly addresses multi-goal allocation, tour sequencing, and safe trajectory generation for quadrotor teams operating in obstacle-rich spaces. IMD--TAPP first discretizes the workspace into a 3D navigation graph and computes obstacle-aware robot-to-goal and goal-to-goal travel costs via graph-search-based pathfinding. These costs are then embedded within an Injected Particle Swarm Optimization (IPSO) scheme, guided by multiple linear assignment, to efficiently explore coupled assignment/ordering alternatives and to minimize mission makespan. Finally, the resulting waypoint tours are transformed into time-parameterized minimum-snap trajectories through a generation-and-optimization routine equipped with iterative validation of obstacle clearance and inter-robot separation, triggering re-planning when safety margins are violated. Extensive MATLAB simulations across cluttered 3D scenarios demonstrate that IMD--TAPP consistently produces dynamically feasible, collision-free trajectories while achieving competitive completion times. In a representative case study with two drones serving multiple goals, the proposed approach attains a minimum mission time of 136~s while maintaining the required safety constraints throughout execution.

摘要（中文）: 在复杂的三维环境中协调无人机编队需要对离散任务规划（决定哪个无人机服务哪个目标以及服务顺序）与连续时间轨迹合成进行有原则的整合，以确保避碰和动力学可行性。本文提出了 IMD-TAPP (集成多无人机任务分配和路径规划)，这是一个端到端框架，它联合处理多目标分配、巡视排序以及适用于在障碍物丰富的空间中运行的四旋翼无人机编队的安全轨迹生成。 IMD-TAPP 首先将工作空间离散化为 3D 导航图，并通过基于图搜索的路径寻找来计算具有避障能力的无人机到目标和目标到目标的行进成本。然后，这些成本被嵌入到 Injected Particle Swarm Optimization (IPSO) 方案中，该方案由多个线性分配引导，以高效地探索耦合的分配/排序替代方案并最小化任务完成时间。最后，通过一个具备迭代验证障碍物清除和无人机间距的生成和优化例程，将生成的航路点巡视转化为时间参数化的最小抖动轨迹，并在安全裕度被违反时触发重新规划。广泛的 MATLAB 仿真在复杂的 3D 场景中表明，IMD-TAPP 始终产生动力学可行、无碰撞的轨迹，同时实现具有竞争力的完成时间。在一个具有代表性的案例研究中，两架无人机服务多个目标，所提出的方法实现了 136 秒的最小任务时间，并在整个执行过程中保持了所需的安全约束。

TLDR: This paper introduces IMD-TAPP, an end-to-end framework for integrated multi-drone task allocation, sequencing, and trajectory generation in complex 3D environments, demonstrating its effectiveness in minimizing mission completion time while maintaining safety constraints through MATLAB simulations.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一个名为IMD-TAPP的端到端框架，用于在复杂的3D环境中集成多无人机任务分配、排序和轨迹生成。MATLAB仿真结果表明，该框架在满足安全约束的同时，能够有效地最小化任务完成时间。

Authors: Yunes Alqudsi, Murat Makaraci

Just Zoom In: Cross-View Geo-Localization via Autoregressive Zooming

Abstract (English): Cross-view geo-localization (CVGL) estimates a camera's location by matching a street-view image to geo-referenced overhead imagery, enabling GPS-denied localization and navigation. Existing methods almost universally formulate CVGL as an image-retrieval problem in a contrastively trained embedding space. This ties performance to large batches and hard negative mining, and it ignores both the geometric structure of maps and the coverage mismatch between street-view and overhead imagery. In particular, salient landmarks visible from the street view can fall outside a fixed satellite crop, making retrieval targets ambiguous and limiting explicit spatial inference over the map. We propose Just Zoom In, an alternative formulation that performs CVGL via autoregressive zooming over a city-scale overhead map. Starting from a coarse satellite view, the model takes a short sequence of zoom-in decisions to select a terminal satellite cell at a target resolution, without contrastive losses or hard negative mining. We further introduce a realistic benchmark with crowd-sourced street views and high-resolution satellite imagery that reflects real capture conditions. On this benchmark, Just Zoom In achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving Recall@1 within 50 m by 5.5% and Recall@1 within 100 m by 9.6% over the strongest contrastive-retrieval baseline. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of sequential coarse-to-fine spatial reasoning for cross-view geo-localization.

摘要（中文）: 跨视角地理定位 (CVGL) 通过将街景图像与具有地理参考的俯视图像进行匹配来估计相机的位置，从而实现无 GPS 定位的本地化和导航。现有的方法几乎普遍地将 CVGL 建模为在对比学习训练的嵌入空间中的图像检索问题。这使得性能受限于大批量和难负样本挖掘，并忽略了地图的几何结构以及街景和俯视图像之间的覆盖范围不匹配。特别地，从街景可见的显著地标可能落在固定的卫星裁剪区域之外，导致检索目标模糊，并限制了在地图上进行明确的空间推理。我们提出了一种替代方案，名为“Just Zoom In”，它通过在城市尺度俯视地图上进行自回归缩放来执行 CVGL。从粗略的卫星视图开始，模型采集一系列短的放大决策，以选择目标分辨率下的终端卫星单元，而无需对比损失或难负样本挖掘。我们进一步引入了一个现实的基准测试，其中包含众包的街景和高分辨率卫星图像，反映了真实的捕获条件。在这个基准测试中，Just Zoom In 实现了最先进的性能，在 50 米范围内的 Recall@1 指标上提高了 5.5%，在 100 米范围内的 Recall@1 指标上提高了 9.6%，超过了最强的对比检索基线。这些结果证明了顺序的粗到精的空间推理对于跨视角地理定位的有效性。

TLDR: The paper introduces "Just Zoom In," a novel cross-view geo-localization method using autoregressive zooming over satellite imagery, achieving state-of-the-art results on a new realistic benchmark by avoiding contrastive learning.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了“Just Zoom In”，一种新颖的跨视角地理定位方法，通过在卫星图像上进行自回归缩放，避免了对比学习，并在一个新的真实基准测试上取得了最先进的结果。

Authors: Yunus Talha Erzurumlu, Jiyong Kwag, Alper Yilmaz

Macroscopic Characteristics of Mixed Traffic Flow with Deep Reinforcement Learning Based Automated and Human-Driven Vehicles

Abstract (English): Automated Vehicle (AV) control in mixed traffic, where AVs coexist with human-driven vehicles, poses significant challenges in balancing safety, efficiency, comfort, fuel efficiency, and compliance with traffic rules while capturing heterogeneous driver behavior. Traditional car-following models, such as the Intelligent Driver Model (IDM), often struggle to generalize across diverse traffic scenarios and typically do not account for fuel efficiency, motivating the use of learning-based approaches. Although Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) has shown strong microscopic performance in car-following conditions, its macroscopic traffic flow characteristics remain underexplored. This study focuses on analyzing the macroscopic traffic flow characteristics and fuel efficiency of DRL-based models in mixed traffic. A Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) algorithm is implemented for AVs' control and trained using the NGSIM highway dataset, enabling realistic interaction with human-driven vehicles. Traffic performance is evaluated using the Fundamental Diagram (FD) under varying driver heterogeneity, heterogeneous time-gap penetration levels, and different shares of RL-controlled vehicles. A macroscopic level comparison of fuel efficiency between the RL-based AV model and the IDM is also conducted. Results show that traffic performance is sensitive to the distribution of safe time gaps and the proportion of RL vehicles. Transitioning from fully human-driven to fully RL-controlled traffic can increase road capacity by approximately 7.52%. Further, RL-based AVs also improve average fuel efficiency by about 28.98% at higher speeds (above 50 km/h), and by 1.86% at lower speeds (below 50 km/h) compared to the IDM. Overall, the DRL framework enhances traffic capacity and fuel efficiency without compromising safety.

摘要（中文）: 在混合交通流中，自动驾驶车辆（AV）与人为驾驶车辆共存，自动驾驶控制面临着在安全、效率、舒适性、燃油效率以及遵守交通规则之间取得平衡的严峻挑战，同时还需要捕捉异质驾驶员行为。诸如智能驾驶员模型（IDM）等传统跟驰模型通常难以推广到各种交通场景，通常也不考虑燃油效率，这促使了基于学习方法的使用。虽然深度强化学习（DRL）在跟驰条件下表现出强大的微观性能，但其宏观交通流特性仍有待探索。本研究侧重于分析基于DRL模型在混合交通流中的宏观交通流特性和燃油效率。采用双延迟深度确定性策略梯度（TD3）算法控制自动驾驶车辆，并使用NGSIM高速公路数据集进行训练，从而实现与人为驾驶车辆的真实交互。使用基本图（FD）评估交通性能，考虑了不同的驾驶员异质性、异质时距渗透率以及不同比例的强化学习控制车辆。同时，还对基于强化学习的自动驾驶车辆模型与IDM之间的宏观层面燃油效率进行了比较。结果表明，交通性能对安全时距的分布和强化学习车辆的比例敏感。从完全人为驾驶交通过渡到完全强化学习控制交通，可将道路通行能力提高约7.52%。此外，与IDM相比，基于强化学习的自动驾驶车辆在高速度（高于50公里/小时）时，平均燃油效率也提高了约28.98%，在低速度（低于50公里/小时）时，平均燃油效率提高了约1.86%。总而言之，该DRL框架在不牺牲安全性的前提下，提高了交通通行能力和燃油效率。

TLDR: This paper explores the macroscopic traffic flow characteristics and fuel efficiency of Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL)-controlled Automated Vehicles (AVs) in mixed traffic, demonstrating improvements in road capacity and fuel efficiency compared to traditional car-following models.

简要说明: 本文探讨了在混合交通中，基于深度强化学习（DRL）控制的自动驾驶车辆（AV）的宏观交通流特性和燃油效率，结果表明与传统的车辆跟随模型相比，道路通行能力和燃油效率均有所提高。

Authors: Pankaj Kumar, Pranamesh Chakraborty, Subrahmanya Swamy Peruru

Large Language Models as Optimization Controllers: Adaptive Continuation for SIMP Topology Optimization

Abstract (English): We present a framework in which a large language model (LLM) acts as an online adaptive controller for SIMP topology optimization, replacing conventional fixed-schedule continuation with real-time, state-conditioned parameter decisions. At every $k$-th iteration, the LLM receives a structured observation$-$current compliance, grayness index, stagnation counter, checkerboard measure, volume fraction, and budget consumption$-$and outputs numerical values for the penalization exponent $p$, projection sharpness $β$, filter radius $r_{\min}$, and move limit $δ$ via a Direct Numeric Control interface. A hard grayness gate prevents premature binarization, and a meta-optimization loop uses a second LLM pass to tune the agent's call frequency and gate threshold across runs. We benchmark the agent against four baselines$-$fixed (no-continuation), standard three-field continuation, an expert heuristic, and a schedule-only ablation$-$on three 2-D problems (cantilever, MBB beam, L-bracket) at $120\!\times\!60$ resolution and two 3-D problems (cantilever, MBB beam) at $40\!\times\!20\!\times\!10$ resolution, all run for 300 iterations. A standardized 40-iteration sharpening tail is applied from the best valid snapshot so that compliance differences reflect only the exploration phase. The LLM agent achieves the lowest final compliance on every benchmark: $-5.7\%$ to $-18.1\%$ relative to the fixed baseline, with all solutions fully binary. The schedule-only ablation underperforms the fixed baseline on two of three problems, confirming that the LLM's real-time intervention$-$not the schedule geometry$-$drives the gain. Code and reproduction scripts will be released upon publication.

摘要（中文）: 我们提出了一个框架，其中大型语言模型（LLM）充当SIMP拓扑优化的在线自适应控制器，以实时、状态条件化的参数决策取代了传统固定方案的延拓方法。在每个第$k$次迭代中，LLM接收结构化观测数据——当前柔顺性、灰度指数、停滞计数器、棋盘格度量、体积分数和预算消耗——并通过直接数值控制接口输出惩罚指数$p$、投影锐度$β$、过滤半径$r_{\min}$和移动极限$δ$的数值。一个硬灰度门阻止过早的二值化，并且一个元优化循环使用第二次LLM传递来调整代理的调用频率和跨运行的门限。我们针对四个基线——固定（无延拓）、标准三场延拓、专家启发式方法和一个仅包含时间表的消融实验——在三个二维问题（悬臂梁、MBB梁、L型支架，$120\!\times\!60$分辨率）和两个三维问题（悬臂梁、MBB梁，$40\!\times\!20\!\times\!10$分辨率）上对该代理进行了基准测试，所有测试均运行300次迭代。从最佳有效快照应用标准化的40次迭代锐化尾部，以便柔顺性差异仅反映探索阶段。 LLM代理在每个基准测试中都实现了最低的最终柔顺性：相对于固定基线，降低了$-5.7\%$至$-18.1\%$，且所有解都是完全二值的。仅包含时间表的消融实验在三个问题中的两个上表现不如固定基线，这证实了LLM的实时干预——而不是时间表的几何形状——驱动了性能提升。代码和复现脚本将在发表时发布。

TLDR: This paper presents a framework using a large language model (LLM) as an online adaptive controller for topology optimization, demonstrating improved performance compared to traditional methods. The LLM dynamically adjusts parameters based on real-time feedback.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种框架，使用大型语言模型（LLM）作为拓扑优化的在线自适应控制器，与传统方法相比，性能有所提高。LLM根据实时反馈动态调整参数。

Authors: Shaoliang Yang, Jun Wang, Yunsheng Wang

Rethinking Failure Attribution in Multi-Agent Systems: A Multi-Perspective Benchmark and Evaluation

Abstract (English): Failure attribution is essential for diagnosing and improving multi-agent systems (MAS), yet existing benchmarks and methods largely assume a single deterministic root cause for each failure. In practice, MAS failures often admit multiple plausible attributions due to complex inter-agent dependencies and ambiguous execution trajectories. We revisit MAS failure attribution from a multi-perspective standpoint and propose multi-perspective failure attribution, a practical paradigm that explicitly accounts for attribution ambiguity. To support this setting, we introduce MP-Bench, the first benchmark designed for multi-perspective failure attribution in MAS, along with a new evaluation protocol tailored to this paradigm. Through extensive experiments, we find that prior conclusions suggesting LLMs struggle with failure attribution are largely driven by limitations in existing benchmark designs. Our results highlight the necessity of multi-perspective benchmarks and evaluation protocols for realistic and reliable MAS debugging.

摘要（中文）: 故障归因对于诊断和改进多智能体系统(MAS)至关重要，然而，现有的基准测试和方法在很大程度上假设每次故障都只有一个确定的根本原因。在实践中，由于复杂的智能体间依赖关系和模糊的执行轨迹，MAS故障通常允许多种合理的归因。我们从多视角角度重新审视MAS故障归因，并提出了多视角故障归因，这是一个实用范例，明确考虑了归因的歧义性。为了支持这种设置，我们引入了MP-Bench，这是第一个专为MAS中的多视角故障归因设计的基准测试，同时提出了针对此范例量身定制的新评估协议。通过大量的实验，我们发现先前认为大型语言模型难以进行故障归因的结论，很大程度上受到现有基准测试设计局限性的驱动。我们的结果强调了多视角基准测试和评估协议对于现实和可靠的MAS调试的必要性。

TLDR: This paper addresses the limitations of existing MAS failure attribution benchmarks by introducing a new multi-perspective benchmark (MP-Bench) and evaluation protocol, demonstrating that LLMs perform better than previously thought when evaluated with a more realistic approach.

简要说明: 本文通过引入新的多视角基准测试 (MP-Bench) 和评估协议，解决了现有 MAS 故障归因基准测试的局限性，表明使用更实际的方法进行评估时，LLM 的性能优于之前的预期。

Authors: Yeonjun In, Mehrab Tanjim, Jayakumar Subramanian, Sungchul Kim, Uttaran Bhattacharya, Wonjoong Kim, Sangwu Park, Somdeb Sarkhel, Chanyoung Park

TIGFlow-GRPO: Trajectory Forecasting via Interaction-Aware Flow Matching and Reward-Driven Optimization

Abstract (English): Human trajectory forecasting is important for intelligent multimedia systems operating in visually complex environments, such as autonomous driving and crowd surveillance. Although Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) has shown strong ability in modeling trajectory distributions from spatio-temporal observations, existing approaches still focus primarily on supervised fitting, which may leave social norms and scene constraints insufficiently reflected in generated trajectories. To address this issue, we propose TIGFlow-GRPO, a two-stage generative framework that aligns flow-based trajectory generation with behavioral rules. In the first stage, we build a CFM-based predictor with a Trajectory-Interaction-Graph (TIG) module to model fine-grained visual-spatial interactions and strengthen context encoding. This stage captures both agent-agent and agent-scene relations more effectively, providing more informative conditional features for subsequent alignment. In the second stage, we perform Flow-GRPO post-training,where deterministic flow rollout is reformulated as stochastic ODE-to-SDE sampling to enable trajectory exploration, and a composite reward combines view-aware social compliance with map-aware physical feasibility. By evaluating trajectories explored through SDE rollout, GRPO progressively steers multimodal predictions toward behaviorally plausible futures. Experiments on the ETH/UCY and SDD datasets show that TIGFlow-GRPO improves forecasting accuracy and long-horizon stability while generating trajectories that are more socially compliant and physically feasible. These results suggest that the proposed framework provides an effective way to connect flow-based trajectory modeling with behavior-aware alignment in dynamic multimedia environments.

摘要（中文）: 人类轨迹预测对于在视觉复杂环境中运行的智能多媒体系统至关重要，例如自动驾驶和人群监控。虽然条件流匹配（CFM）在建模来自时空观测的轨迹分布方面表现出强大的能力，但现有方法仍然主要侧重于监督拟合，这可能导致生成的轨迹未能充分反映社会规范和场景约束。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种名为TIGFlow-GRPO的两阶段生成框架，该框架将基于流的轨迹生成与行为规则对齐。在第一阶段，我们构建一个基于CFM的预测器，其中包含轨迹-交互-图（TIG）模块，以建模细粒度的视觉-空间交互并加强上下文编码。这一阶段更有效地捕捉了主体-主体和主体-场景关系，为后续对齐提供了更具信息性的条件特征。在第二阶段，我们执行Flow-GRPO后训练，其中确定性流展开被重新表述为随机ODE-to-SDE采样，以实现轨迹探索，并且复合奖励结合了视角感知的社会合规性和地图感知的物理可行性。通过评估由SDE展开探索的轨迹，GRPO逐渐将多模态预测引导至行为上合理的未来。在ETH/UCY和SDD数据集上的实验表明，TIGFlow-GRPO提高了预测精度和长时程稳定性，同时生成了更符合社会规范和物理上可行的轨迹。这些结果表明，所提出的框架提供了一种有效的方法，可以将基于流的轨迹建模与动态多媒体环境中行为感知对齐联系起来。

TLDR: The paper introduces TIGFlow-GRPO, a two-stage framework for human trajectory forecasting that combines Conditional Flow Matching with a Trajectory-Interaction-Graph module and reward-driven optimization to improve accuracy, stability, social compliance, and physical feasibility.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了TIGFlow-GRPO，一个用于人类轨迹预测的两阶段框架，它结合了条件流匹配、轨迹交互图模块和奖励驱动的优化，以提高准确性、稳定性、社会合规性和物理可行性。

Authors: Xuepeng Jing, Wenhuan Lu, Hao Meng, Zhizhi Yu, Jianguo Wei

Unleashing Guidance Without Classifiers for Human-Object Interaction Animation

Abstract (English): Generating realistic human-object interaction (HOI) animations remains challenging because it requires jointly modeling dynamic human actions and diverse object geometries. Prior diffusion-based approaches often rely on hand-crafted contact priors or human-imposed kinematic constraints to improve contact quality. We propose LIGHT, a data-driven alternative in which guidance emerges from the denoising pace itself, reducing dependence on manually designed priors. Building on diffusion forcing, we factor the representation into modality-specific components and assign individualized noise levels with asynchronous denoising schedules. In this paradigm, cleaner components guide noisier ones through cross-attention, yielding guidance without auxiliary classifiers. We find that this data-driven guidance is inherently contact-aware, and can be enhanced when training is augmented with a broad spectrum of synthetic object geometries, encouraging invariance of contact semantics to geometric diversity. Extensive experiments show that pace-induced guidance more effectively mirrors the benefits of contact priors than conventional classifier-free guidance, while achieving higher contact fidelity, more realistic HOI generation, and stronger generalization to unseen objects and tasks.

摘要（中文）: 生成逼真的人-物交互（HOI）动画仍然具有挑战性，因为它需要联合建模动态的人类动作和多样化的物体几何形状。 先前基于扩散的方法通常依赖于手工制作的接触先验或人为施加的运动学约束来提高接触质量。 我们提出LIGHT，这是一种数据驱动的替代方案，其中指导来自去噪节奏本身，从而减少对手动设计的先验的依赖。 基于扩散强制，我们将表示分解为模态特定的组件，并使用异步去噪计划为每个组件分配单独的噪声水平。 在这种范式中，更干净的组件通过交叉注意力引导噪声较大的组件，从而在没有辅助分类器的情况下产生指导。 我们发现这种数据驱动的指导本质上是接触感知的，并且当训练通过广泛的合成物体几何形状增强时，可以得到增强，鼓励接触语义对几何多样性的不变性。 大量实验表明，与传统的无分类器指导相比，节奏诱导的指导能够更有效地反映接触先验的优势，同时实现更高的接触保真度、更逼真的人-物交互生成以及对未见物体和任务更强的泛化能力。

TLDR: The paper introduces LIGHT, a diffusion-based method for generating realistic human-object interaction animations by using asynchronous denoising schedules to achieve contact-aware guidance without relying on hand-crafted priors or classifiers, leading to improved contact fidelity and generalization.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为LIGHT的扩散模型，用于生成逼真的人机交互动画。它通过异步去噪计划实现接触感知引导，无需依赖手工设计的先验知识或分类器，从而提高了接触逼真度和泛化能力。

Relevance: (6/10)
