Authors: Ziyin Wang, Sirui Xu, Chuan Guo, Bing Zhou, Jiangshan Gong, Jian Wang, Yu-Xiong Wang, Liang-Yan Gui

GeoHeight-Bench: Towards Height-Aware Multimodal Reasoning in Remote Sensing

Abstract (English): Current Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) in Earth Observation typically neglect the critical "vertical" dimension, limiting their reasoning capabilities in complex remote sensing geometries and disaster scenarios where physical spatial structures often outweigh planar visual textures. To bridge this gap, we introduce a comprehensive evaluation framework dedicated to height-aware remote sensing understanding. First, to overcome the severe scarcity of annotated data, we develop a scalable, VLM-driven data generation pipeline utilizing systematic prompt engineering and metadata extraction. This pipeline constructs two complementary benchmarks: GeoHeight-Bench for relative height analysis, and a more challenging GeoHeight-Bench+ for holistic, terrain-aware reasoning. Furthermore, to validate the necessity of height perception, we propose GeoHeightChat, the first height-aware remote sensing LMM baseline. Serving as a strong proof of concept, our baseline demonstrates that synergizing visual semantics with implicitly injected height geometric features effectively mitigates the "vertical blind spot", successfully unlocking a new paradigm of interactive height reasoning in existing optical models.

摘要（中文）: 地球观测领域当前的大型多模态模型（LMM）通常忽略至关重要的“垂直”维度，限制了其在复杂遥感几何结构和灾害场景中的推理能力，在这些场景中，物理空间结构的重要性往往超过平面视觉纹理。为了弥补这一差距，我们引入了一个全面的评估框架，专门用于感知高度的遥感理解。首先，为了克服标注数据严重稀缺的问题，我们开发了一种可扩展的、VLM 驱动的数据生成流水线，利用系统的提示工程和元数据提取。该流水线构建了两个互补的基准：用于相对高度分析的 GeoHeight-Bench，以及一个更具挑战性的、用于整体地形感知推理的 GeoHeight-Bench+。 此外，为了验证高度感知的必要性，我们提出了 GeoHeightChat，这是第一个感知高度的遥感 LMM 基线。 作为概念验证，我们的基线表明，将视觉语义与隐含注入的高度几何特征相结合，能有效缓解“垂直盲点”，成功开启了现有光学模型中交互式高度推理的新范例。

TLDR: This paper introduces GeoHeight-Bench, a height-aware evaluation framework and baseline (GeoHeightChat) for Large Multimodal Models in remote sensing, addressing the neglect of vertical information in existing models and demonstrating improved reasoning in complex scenarios.

简要说明: 本文介绍了GeoHeight-Bench，这是一个针对遥感领域大型多模态模型的高度感知评估框架和基线(GeoHeightChat)。该框架旨在解决现有模型忽略垂直信息的不足，并展示了模型在复杂场景中更好的推理能力。

Authors: Xuran Hu, Zhitong Xiong, Zhongcheng Hong, Yifang Ban, Xiaoxiang Zhu, Wufan Zhao

Knowledge-Guided Failure Prediction: Detecting When Object Detectors Miss Safety-Critical Objects

Abstract (English): Object detectors deployed in safety-critical environments can fail silently, e.g. missing pedestrians, workers, or other safety-critical objects without emitting any warning. Traditional Out Of Distribution (OOD) detection methods focus on identifying unfamiliar inputs, but do not directly predict functional failures of the detector itself. We introduce Knowledge Guided Failure Prediction (KGFP), a representation-based monitoring framework that treats missed safety-critical detections as anomalies to be detected at runtime. KGFP measures semantic misalignment between internal object detector features and visual foundation model embeddings using a dual-encoder architecture with an angular distance metric. A key property is that when either the detector is operating outside its competence or the visual foundation model itself encounters novel inputs, the two embeddings diverge, producing a high-angle signal that reliably flags unsafe images. We compare our novel KGFS method to baseline OOD detection methods. On COCO person detection, applying KGFP as a selective-prediction gate raises person recall among accepted images from 64.3% to 84.5% at 5% False Positive Rate (FPR), and maintains strong performance across six COCO-O visual domains, outperforming OOD baselines by large margins. Our code, models, and features are published at https://gitlab.cc-asp.fraunhofer.de/iosb_public/KGFP.

摘要（中文）: 在安全攸关环境中部署的目标检测器可能会无声地失效，例如，漏检行人、工人或其他安全攸关目标，且不发出任何警告。传统的分布外 (OOD) 检测方法侧重于识别不熟悉的输入，但不能直接预测检测器自身的功能性故障。我们引入了知识引导的故障预测 (KGFP)，这是一种基于表征的监控框架，将漏检的安全攸关目标视为运行时需要检测的异常。KGFP 使用带有角度距离度量的双编码器架构，测量内部目标检测器特征和视觉基础模型嵌入之间的语义失调。一个关键特性是，当检测器在其能力范围之外运行，或视觉基础模型本身遇到新颖输入时，两个嵌入会发散，产生一个高角度信号，从而可靠地标记不安全图像。我们将我们新颖的 KGFP 方法与基准 OOD 检测方法进行了比较。在 COCO 行人检测中，应用 KGFP 作为选择性预测门可以将接受图像中的行人召回率从 64.3% 提高到 84.5%，且误报率 (FPR) 为 5%，并在六个 COCO-O 视觉领域保持了强大的性能，大幅优于 OOD 基线。我们的代码、模型和特征发布于 https://gitlab.cc-asp.fraunhofer.de/iosb_public/KGFP。

TLDR: This paper introduces Knowledge Guided Failure Prediction (KGFP) to detect when object detectors fail to identify safety-critical objects, using semantic misalignment between object detector features and visual foundation model embeddings. KGFP improves pedestrian detection recall and outperforms OOD baselines.

简要说明: 本文提出了知识引导的失败预测 (KGFP)，用于检测目标检测器在识别安全关键目标时何时失败。 KGFP 通过使用目标检测器特征和视觉基础模型嵌入之间的语义不对齐来工作，并提高了行人检测召回率，优于传统的 OOD 基线。

Authors: Jakob Paul Zimmermann, Gerrit Holzbach, David Lerch

V2U4Real: A Real-world Large-scale Dataset for Vehicle-to-UAV Cooperative Perception

Abstract (English): Modern autonomous vehicle perception systems are often constrained by occlusions, blind spots, and limited sensing range. While existing cooperative perception paradigms, such as Vehicle-to-Vehicle (V2V) and Vehicle-to-Infrastructure (V2I), have demonstrated their effectiveness in mitigating these challenges, they remain limited to ground-level collaboration and cannot fully address large-scale occlusions or long-range perception in complex environments. To advance research in cross-view cooperative perception, we present V2U4Real, the first large-scale real-world multi-modal dataset for Vehicle-to-UAV (V2U) cooperative object perception. V2U4Real is collected by a ground vehicle and a UAV equipped with multi-view LiDARs and RGB cameras. The dataset covers urban streets, university campuses, and rural roads under diverse traffic scenarios, comprising over 56K LiDAR frames, 56K multi-view camera images, and 700K annotated 3D bounding boxes across four classes. To support a wide range of research tasks, we establish benchmarks for single-agent 3D object detection, cooperative 3D object detection, and object tracking. Comprehensive evaluations of several state-of-the-art models demonstrate the effectiveness of V2U cooperation in enhancing perception robustness and long-range awareness. The V2U4Real dataset and codebase is available at https://github.com/VjiaLi/V2U4Real.

摘要（中文）: 现代自动驾驶车辆感知系统通常受到遮挡、盲点和有限感知范围的限制。虽然现有的协同感知范式，例如车-车通信（V2V）和车-路通信（V2I），已经证明了它们在缓解这些挑战方面的有效性，但它们仍然局限于地面层面的协作，无法完全解决复杂环境中的大规模遮挡或远距离感知问题。为了推进跨视角协同感知的研究，我们提出了 V2U4Real，这是首个用于车-无人机通信（V2U）协同目标感知的真实世界大规模多模态数据集。V2U4Real 由一辆地面车辆和一个配备多视角激光雷达和 RGB 相机的无人机采集。该数据集涵盖城市街道、大学校园和乡村道路等多种交通场景，包含超过 5.6 万帧激光雷达数据、5.6 万张多视角相机图像以及跨四个类别的 70 万个标注的 3D 边界框。为了支持广泛的研究任务，我们建立了单智能体 3D 目标检测、协同 3D 目标检测和目标跟踪的基准。对几种最先进模型的综合评估表明，V2U 协作在增强感知鲁棒性和远距离感知能力方面是有效的。V2U4Real 数据集和代码库可在以下网址获取：https://github.com/VjiaLi/V2U4Real。

TLDR: The paper introduces V2U4Real, a large-scale real-world dataset for Vehicle-to-UAV cooperative perception, designed to address limitations of existing cooperative perception systems by leveraging UAV-based observation.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了V2U4Real，一个大规模的真实世界车辆到无人机协同感知数据集，旨在通过利用基于无人机的观测来解决现有协同感知系统的局限性。

Authors: Weijia Li, Haoen Xiang, Tianxu Wang, Shuaibing Wu, Qiming Xia, Cheng Wang, Chenglu Wen

Bilingual Text-to-Motion Generation: A New Benchmark and Baselines

Abstract (English): Text-to-motion generation holds significant potential for cross-linguistic applications, yet it is hindered by the lack of bilingual datasets and the poor cross-lingual semantic understanding of existing language models. To address these gaps, we introduce BiHumanML3D, the first bilingual text-to-motion benchmark, constructed via LLM-assisted annotation and rigorous manual correction. Furthermore, we propose a simple yet effective baseline, Bilingual Motion Diffusion (BiMD), featuring Cross-Lingual Alignment (CLA). CLA explicitly aligns semantic representations across languages, creating a robust conditional space that enables high-quality motion generation from bilingual inputs, including zero-shot code-switching scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BiMD with CLA achieves an FID of 0.045 vs. 0.169 and R@3 of 82.8\% vs. 80.8\%, significantly outperforms monolingual diffusion models and translation baselines on BiHumanML3D, underscoring the critical necessity and reliability of our dataset and the effectiveness of our alignment strategy for cross-lingual motion synthesis. The dataset and code are released at \href{https://wengwanjiang.github.io/BilingualT2M-page}{https://wengwanjiang.github.io/BilingualT2M-page}

摘要（中文）: 文本到动作生成在跨语言应用方面具有巨大的潜力，但缺乏双语数据集和现有语言模型较差的跨语言语义理解阻碍了其发展。为了解决这些问题，我们推出了BiHumanML3D，这是首个双语文本到动作基准，通过LLM辅助标注和严格的人工校正构建而成。此外，我们提出了一个简单而有效的基线模型，即双语动作扩散（BiMD），其特点是跨语言对齐（CLA）。CLA显式地对齐了跨语言的语义表示，创建了一个强大的条件空间，能够从双语输入生成高质量的动作，包括零样本代码切换场景。大量的实验表明，带有CLA的BiMD在BiHumanML3D上实现了0.045的FID（vs. 0.169）和82.8%的R@3（vs. 80.8%），显著优于单语扩散模型和翻译基线，突显了我们数据集的关键必要性和可靠性，以及我们对齐策略在跨语言动作合成方面的有效性。数据集和代码已发布在\href{https://wengwanjiang.github.io/BilingualT2M-page}{https://wengwanjiang.github.io/BilingualT2M-page}。

TLDR: The paper introduces BiHumanML3D, a new bilingual (English and Chinese) text-to-motion dataset, and proposes Bilingual Motion Diffusion (BiMD) with Cross-Lingual Alignment (CLA) for effective cross-lingual motion generation.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了BiHumanML3D，一个新的双语（英语和中文）文本到动作的数据集，并提出了带有跨语言对齐（CLA）的双语运动扩散（BiMD）方法，用于有效的跨语言运动生成。

Authors: Wanjiang Weng, Xiaofeng Tan, Xiangbo Shu, Guo-Sen Xie, Pan Zhou, Hongsong Wang

Label What Matters: Modality-Balanced and Difficulty-Aware Multimodal Active Learning

Abstract (English): Multimodal learning integrates complementary information from different modalities such as image, text, and audio to improve model performance, but its success relies on large-scale labeled data, which is costly to obtain. Active learning (AL) mitigates this challenge by selectively annotating informative samples. In multimodal settings, many approaches implicitly assume that modality importance is stable across rounds and keep selection rules fixed at the fusion stage, which leaves them insensitive to the dynamic nature of multimodal learning, where the relative value of modalities and the difficulty of instances shift as training proceeds. To address this issue, we propose RL-MBA, a reinforcement-learning framework for modality-balanced, difficulty-aware multimodal active learning. RL-MBA models sample selection as a Markov Decision Process, where the policy adapts to modality contributions, uncertainty, and diversity, and the reward encourages accuracy gains and balance. Two key components drive this adaptability: (1) Adaptive Modality Contribution Balancing (AMCB), which dynamically adjusts modality weights via reinforcement feedback, and (2) Evidential Fusion for DifficultyAware Policy Adjustment (EFDA), which estimates sample difficulty via uncertainty-based evidential fusion to prioritize informative samples. Experiments on Food101, KineticsSound, and VGGSound demonstrate that RL-MBA consistently outperforms strong baselines, improving both classification accuracy and modality fairness under limited labeling budgets.

摘要（中文）: 多模态学习整合来自不同模态（如图像、文本和音频）的互补信息以提升模型性能，但其成功依赖于大规模标注数据，而获取这些数据成本高昂。主动学习 (AL) 通过选择性地标注信息量大的样本来缓解这一挑战。在多模态场景中，许多方法隐式地假设模态重要性在各轮迭代中保持稳定，并在融合阶段保持选择规则不变，这使得它们对多模态学习的动态特性不敏感，而模态的相对价值和实例的难度会随着训练的进行而变化。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种基于强化学习的框架 RL-MBA，用于模态平衡、难度感知的多模态主动学习。RL-MBA 将样本选择建模为一个马尔可夫决策过程，其中策略适应模态贡献、不确定性和多样性，并且奖励鼓励准确率提升和平衡。两个关键组成部分驱动了这种适应性：(1) 自适应模态贡献平衡 (AMCB)，它通过强化反馈动态调整模态权重；(2) 用于难度感知策略调整的证据融合 (EFDA)，它通过基于不确定性的证据融合来估计样本难度，从而优先考虑信息量大的样本。在 Food101、KineticsSound 和 VGGSound 上的实验表明，在有限的标注预算下，RL-MBA 始终优于强大的基线方法，从而提高了分类准确率和模态公平性。

TLDR: The paper introduces RL-MBA, a reinforcement learning framework for multimodal active learning that dynamically adjusts modality weights and prioritizes informative samples based on estimated difficulty, outperforming existing methods on several datasets.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了RL-MBA，一个用于多模态主动学习的强化学习框架，它可以动态调整模态权重，并根据估计的难度来优先选择信息量大的样本，在多个数据集上优于现有方法。

Authors: Yuqiao Zeng, Xu Wang, Tengfei Liang, Yiqing Hao, Yi Jin, Hui Yu

GDPO-Listener: Expressive Interactive Head Generation via Auto-Regressive Flow Matching and Group reward-Decoupled Policy Optimization

Abstract (English): Generating realistic 3D head motion for dyadic interactions is a significant challenge in virtual human synthesis. While recent methods achieve impressive results with speaking heads, they frequently suffer from the `Regression-to-the-Mean' problem in listener motions, collapsing into static faces, and lack the parameter space for complex nonverbal motions. In this paper, we propose GDPO-Listener, a novel framework that achieves highly expressive speaking and listening motion generation. First, we introduce an Auto-Regressive Flow Matching architecture enabling stable supervised learning. Second, to overcome kinematic stillness, we apply the Group reward-Decoupled Policy Optimization (GDPO). By isolating reward normalization across distinct FLAME parameter groups, GDPO explicitly incentivizes high variance expressive generations. Finally, we enable explicit semantic text control for customizable responses. Extensive evaluations across the Seamless Interaction and DualTalk datasets demonstrate superior performance compared to existing baselines on long-term kinematic variance, visual expressivity and semantic controllability.

摘要（中文）: 为二元互动生成逼真的3D头部运动是虚拟人合成领域的一项重大挑战。虽然近期的研究在说话头部的运动生成方面取得了显著成果，但它们在听者运动中常常面临“回归均值”的问题，导致静态面部，并且缺乏复杂非语言运动的参数空间。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为GDPO-Listener的新框架，它实现了极具表现力的说话和聆听运动生成。首先，我们引入了一种自回归流匹配架构，从而实现稳定的监督学习。其次，为了克服运动学上的停滞，我们应用了分组奖励解耦策略优化（GDPO）。通过对不同的FLAME参数组进行独立的奖励归一化，GDPO显式地激励高方差的表现力生成。最后，我们实现了显式的语义文本控制，从而实现可定制的响应。在Seamless Interaction和DualTalk数据集上的广泛评估表明，与现有的基线方法相比，我们的方法在长期运动学方差、视觉表现力和语义可控性方面都具有优越的性能。

TLDR: The paper introduces GDPO-Listener, a novel framework for generating expressive 3D head motions for both speaking and listening in dyadic interactions, addressing the 'Regression-to-the-Mean' problem via Auto-Regressive Flow Matching and Group reward-Decoupled Policy Optimization.

简要说明: 该论文提出了GDPO-Listener，一种新颖的框架，用于生成对话互动中富有表现力的3D头部运动，通过自回归流匹配和组奖励解耦策略优化解决“回归均值”问题。

Authors: Zhangyu Jin, Maksim Siniukov, Deuksin Kwon, Ashutosh Chaubey, Mohammad Soleymani

AVControl: Efficient Framework for Training Audio-Visual Controls

Abstract (English): Controlling video and audio generation requires diverse modalities, from depth and pose to camera trajectories and audio transformations, yet existing approaches either train a single monolithic model for a fixed set of controls or introduce costly architectural changes for each new modality. We introduce AVControl, a lightweight, extendable framework built on LTX-2, a joint audio-visual foundation model, where each control modality is trained as a separate LoRA on a parallel canvas that provides the reference signal as additional tokens in the attention layers, requiring no architectural changes beyond the LoRA adapters themselves. We show that simply extending image-based in-context methods to video fails for structural control, and that our parallel canvas approach resolves this. On the VACE Benchmark, we outperform all evaluated baselines on depth- and pose-guided generation, inpainting, and outpainting, and show competitive results on camera control and audio-visual benchmarks. Our framework supports a diverse set of independently trained modalities: spatially-aligned controls such as depth, pose, and edges, camera trajectory with intrinsics, sparse motion control, video editing, and, to our knowledge, the first modular audio-visual controls for a joint generation model. Our method is both compute- and data-efficient: each modality requires only a small dataset and converges within a few hundred to a few thousand training steps, a fraction of the budget of monolithic alternatives. We publicly release our code and trained LoRA checkpoints.

摘要（中文）: 用于控制视频和音频生成需要多种模态，从深度和姿态到相机轨迹和音频变换，然而现有方法要么为固定的控制集训练单个整体模型，要么为每种新模态引入代价高昂的架构变更。我们提出了AVControl，一个轻量级、可扩展的框架，构建于联合视听基础模型LTX-2之上，其中每种控制模态都被训练为一个独立的LoRA，位于一个并行画布上，该画布提供参考信号作为注意力层中的额外token，除了LoRA适配器本身之外，无需任何架构变更。我们表明，简单地将基于图像的上下文学习方法扩展到视频，在结构化控制方面会失效，而我们的并行画布方法解决了这个问题。在VACE基准测试中，我们在深度和姿态引导生成、修复和外推方面优于所有评估的基线，并在相机控制和视听基准测试中表现出具有竞争力的结果。我们的框架支持多种独立训练的模态：空间对齐的控制，例如深度、姿态和边缘，带有内参的相机轨迹，稀疏运动控制，视频编辑，以及据我们所知，首个用于联合生成模型的模块化视听控制。我们的方法兼具计算和数据效率：每种模态仅需要少量数据集，并在几百到几千个训练步骤内收敛，这只是整体替代方案预算的一小部分。我们公开了我们的代码和训练好的LoRA检查点。

TLDR: AVControl is a new framework for training audio-visual controls using LoRA adapters on a parallel canvas built on LTX-2, a joint audio-visual foundation model. It achieves state-of-the-art results on various audio-visual control tasks with better compute and data efficiency.

简要说明: AVControl 是一个用于训练视听控制的新框架，它利用 LTX-2 上的 LoRA 适配器和一个并行画布。它在各种视听控制任务上实现了最先进的结果，并具有更好的计算和数据效率。

Authors: Matan Ben-Yosef, Tavi Halperin, Naomi Ken Korem, Mohammad Salama, Harel Cain, Asaf Joseph, Anthony Chen, Urska Jelercic, Ofir Bibi

Lookalike3D: Seeing Double in 3D

Abstract (English): 3D object understanding and generation methods produce impressive results, yet they often overlook a pervasive source of information in real-world scenes: repeated objects. We introduce the task of lookalike object detection in indoor scenes, which leverages repeated and complementary cues from identical and near-identical object pairs. Given an input scene, the task is to classify pairs of objects as identical, similar or different using multiview images as input. To address this, we present Lookalike3D, a multiview image transformer that effectively distinguishes such object pairs by harnessing strong semantic priors from large image foundation models. To support this task, we collected the 3DTwins dataset, containing 76k manually annotated identical, similar and different pairs of objects based on ScanNet++, and show an improvement of 104% IoU over baselines. We demonstrate how our method improves downstream tasks such as enabling joint 3D object reconstruction and part co-segmentation, turning repeated and lookalike objects into a powerful cue for consistent, high-quality 3D perception. Our code, dataset and models will be made publicly available.

摘要（中文）: 3D物体理解和生成方法已经取得了令人瞩目的成果，但它们通常忽略了现实世界场景中一种普遍存在的信息来源：重复出现的物体。我们提出了室内场景中相似物检测的任务，该任务利用来自相同和近乎相同的物体对的重复和互补线索。给定一个输入场景，该任务旨在使用多视角图像作为输入，将物体对分类为相同、相似或不同。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了Lookalike3D，一种多视角图像Transformer，它通过利用来自大型图像基础模型的强大语义先验来有效地区分这些物体对。为了支持这项任务，我们收集了3DTwins数据集，其中包含基于ScanNet++的7.6万个手动标注的相同、相似和不同的物体对，并展示了相对于基线方法104% IoU的提升。我们证明了我们的方法如何改进下游任务，例如实现联合3D物体重建和部件协同分割，将重复和相似物体转化为一致、高质量3D感知的强大线索。我们的代码、数据集和模型将公开发布。

TLDR: The paper introduces Lookalike3D, a method and dataset (3DTwins) for detecting identical/similar object pairs in 3D scenes using a multiview image transformer, designed to improve 3D object understanding and downstream tasks like reconstruction and part co-segmentation.

Authors: Chandan Yeshwanth, Angela Dai

SEVerA: Verified Synthesis of Self-Evolving Agents

Abstract (English): Recent advances have shown the effectiveness of self-evolving LLM agents on tasks such as program repair and scientific discovery. In this paradigm, a planner LLM synthesizes an agent program that invokes parametric models, including LLMs, which are then tuned per task to improve performance. However, existing self-evolving agent frameworks provide no formal guarantees of safety or correctness. Because such programs are often executed autonomously on unseen inputs, this lack of guarantees raises reliability and security concerns. We formulate agentic code generation as a constrained learning problem, combining hard formal specifications with soft objectives capturing task utility. We introduce Formally Guarded Generative Models (FGGM), which allow the planner LLM to specify a formal output contract for each generative model call using first-order logic. Each FGGM call wraps the underlying model in a rejection sampler with a verified fallback, ensuring every returned output satisfies the contract for any input and parameter setting. Building on FGGM, we present SEVerA (Self-Evolving Verified Agents), a three-stage framework: Search synthesizes candidate parametric programs containing FGGM calls; Verification proves correctness with respect to hard constraints for all parameter values, reducing the problem to unconstrained learning; and Learning applies scalable gradient-based optimization, including GRPO-style fine-tuning, to improve the soft objective while preserving correctness. We evaluate SEVerA on Dafny program verification, symbolic math synthesis, and policy-compliant agentic tool use ($τ^2$-bench). Across tasks, SEVerA achieves zero constraint violations while improving performance over unconstrained and SOTA baselines, showing that formal behavioral constraints not only guarantee correctness but also steer synthesis toward higher-quality agents.

摘要（中文）: 最近的进展表明，自演化的LLM智能体在程序修复和科学发现等任务上表现出高效性。在这种范式中，规划器LLM合成一个智能体程序，该程序调用参数化模型（包括LLM），然后针对每个任务进行调整以提高性能。然而，现有的自演化智能体框架没有提供关于安全性或正确性的正式保证。由于这些程序通常在未知的输入上自主执行，这种缺乏保证引发了可靠性和安全性的担忧。我们将智能体代码生成形式化为一个受约束的学习问题，将硬性形式化规范与捕捉任务效用的软性目标相结合。我们引入了形式化守护生成模型（FGGM），它允许规划器LLM使用一阶逻辑为每个生成模型调用指定一个形式化的输出契约。每个FGGM调用都将底层模型封装在一个带有验证回退的拒绝采样器中，确保每个返回的输出在任何输入和参数设置下都满足契约。基于FGGM，我们提出了SEVerA（自演化验证智能体），这是一个三阶段框架：搜索合成包含FGGM调用的候选参数化程序；验证证明对于所有参数值的硬性约束的正确性，从而将问题简化为无约束学习；学习应用可扩展的基于梯度的优化，包括GRPO风格的微调，以在保持正确性的前提下改进软性目标。我们在Dafny程序验证、符号数学合成和符合策略的智能体工具使用（$τ^2$-bench）上评估了SEVerA。在所有任务中，SEVerA实现了零约束违反，同时提高了优于无约束基线和SOTA基线的性能，表明形式化行为约束不仅保证了正确性，而且引导合成朝着更高质量的智能体发展。

TLDR: The paper introduces SEVerA, a framework for synthesizing self-evolving LLM agents with formal guarantees of safety and correctness, validated on program verification, symbolic math synthesis, and policy-compliant tool use.

简要说明: 本文提出 SEVerA，一个用于合成具有形式化安全性和正确性保证的自进化 LLM 代理的框架，已在程序验证、符号数学合成和符合策略的工具使用方面进行了验证。

Authors: Debangshu Banerjee, Changming Xu, Gagandeep Singh

Learning to Staff: Offline Reinforcement Learning and Fine-Tuned LLMs for Warehouse Staffing Optimization

Abstract (English): We investigate machine learning approaches for optimizing real-time staffing decisions in semi-automated warehouse sortation systems. Operational decision-making can be supported at different levels of abstraction, with different trade-offs. We evaluate two approaches, each in a matching simulation environment. First, we train custom Transformer-based policies using offline reinforcement learning on detailed historical state representations, achieving a 2.4% throughput improvement over historical baselines in learned simulators. In high-volume warehouse operations, improvements of this size translate to significant savings. Second, we explore LLMs operating on abstracted, human-readable state descriptions. These are a natural fit for decisions that warehouse managers make using high-level operational summaries. We systematically compare prompting techniques, automatic prompt optimization, and fine-tuning strategies. While prompting alone proves insufficient, supervised fine-tuning combined with Direct Preference Optimization on simulator-generated preferences achieves performance that matches or slightly exceeds historical baselines in a hand-crafted simulator. Our findings demonstrate that both approaches offer viable paths toward AI-assisted operational decision-making. Offline RL excels with task-specific architectures. LLMs support human-readable inputs and can be combined with an iterative feedback loop that can incorporate manager preferences.

摘要（中文）: 我们研究了使用机器学习方法优化半自动化仓库分拣系统中实时人员配置决策的问题。 运营决策支持可以在不同的抽象层面上进行，并带来不同的权衡。我们评估了两种方法，每种方法都在一个匹配的仿真环境中进行。首先，我们使用离线强化学习在详细的历史状态表示上训练定制的基于Transformer的策略，在学习到的模拟器中实现了比历史基线提高2.4%的吞吐量。 在高吞吐量仓库运营中，这种规模的改进可以转化为显著的节省。其次，我们探索了基于抽象的、人类可读的状态描述运行的大型语言模型（LLM）。这些模型天然适合仓库管理人员使用高层次运营摘要进行的决策。 我们系统地比较了提示技术、自动提示优化和微调策略。虽然单独的提示证明是不够的，但监督微调与模拟器生成的偏好上的直接偏好优化相结合，可以在手工模拟器中达到或略微超过历史基线的性能。我们的研究结果表明，这两种方法都为 AI 辅助运营决策提供了可行的途径。 离线强化学习在特定任务架构中表现出色。 大型语言模型支持人类可读的输入，并且可以与迭代反馈循环相结合，从而可以整合管理人员的偏好。

TLDR: This paper explores offline RL and fine-tuned LLMs for warehouse staffing optimization, finding that offline RL with specialized architectures outperforms historical baselines, while fine-tuned LLMs match baselines and offer human-readable input benefits.

简要说明: 该论文探索了离线强化学习和微调的LLM在仓库人员配置优化中的应用，发现离线强化学习配合专用架构优于历史基线，而微调的LLM与基线持平，并提供了人类可读的输入优势。

Authors: Kalle Kujanpää, Yuying Zhu, Kristina Klinkner, Shervin Malmasi

Characterization of Constraints in Flexible Unknown Environments

Abstract (English): This paper presents an online path planning algorithm for safe autonomous manipulation of a flexibly constrained object in an unknown environment. Methods for real time identification and characterization of perceived flexible constraints and global stiffness are presented. Used in tandem, these methods allow a robot to simultaneously explore, characterize, and manipulate an elastic system safely. Navigation without a-priori knowledge of the system is achieved using constraint exploration based on local force and position information. The perceived constraint stiffness is considered at multiple poses along an object's (system) trajectory. Using stiffness eigenvector information, global stiffness behavior is characterized and identified using an atlas of simple mechanical constraints, such as hinges and planar constraints. Validation of these algorithms is carried out by simulation and experimentally. The ability to recognize several common simple mechanical constraints (such as a flexible hinge) in real time, and to subsequently identify relevant screw parameters is demonstrated. These results suggest the feasibility of simultaneous global constrain/stiffness exploration and safe manipulation of flexibly constrained objects. We believe that this approach will eventually enable safe cooperative manipulation in applications such as organ retraction and manipulation during surgery

摘要（中文）: 本文提出了一种在线路径规划算法，用于在未知环境中安全自主地操纵受柔性约束的对象。论文给出了对感知到的柔性约束和全局刚度进行实时识别和表征的方法。这些方法协同使用，允许机器人同时探索、表征和安全地操纵弹性系统。在没有系统先验知识的情况下，通过基于局部力和位置信息的约束探索来实现导航。在对象（系统）轨迹上的多个姿态处考虑感知到的约束刚度。利用刚度特征向量信息，通过简单机械约束图集（例如铰链和平面约束）来表征和识别全局刚度行为。通过仿真和实验验证了这些算法。实验证明了实时识别几种常见的简单机械约束（如柔性铰链）以及随后识别相关螺旋参数的能力。这些结果表明，同时进行全局约束/刚度探索和安全操纵受柔性约束对象的可能性。我们认为，这种方法最终将能够在诸如手术过程中器官回缩和操作等应用中实现安全的协同操作。

TLDR: The paper presents an online path planning algorithm for safe autonomous manipulation of flexibly constrained objects in unknown environments, enabling simultaneous exploration, characterization, and manipulation using real-time constraint identification and stiffness characterization.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种在线路径规划算法，用于在未知环境中安全自主地操作具有柔性约束的物体。该算法能够通过实时约束识别和刚度表征，同时进行探索、表征和操作。

Authors: Samrat Bhattacharyya, Nabil Simaan

Saranga: MilliWatt Ultrasound for Navigation in Visually Degraded Environments on Palm-Sized Aerial Robots

Abstract (English): Tiny palm-sized aerial robots possess exceptional agility and cost-effectiveness in navigating confined and cluttered environments. However, their limited payload capacity directly constrains the sensing suite on-board the robot, thereby limiting critical navigational tasks in Global Positioning System (GPS)-denied wild scenes. Common methods for obstacle avoidance use cameras and LIght Detection And Ranging (LIDAR), which become ineffective in visually degraded conditions such as low visibility, dust, fog or darkness. Other sensors, such as RAdio Detection And Ranging (RADAR), have high power consumption, making them unsuitable for tiny aerial robots. Inspired by bats, we propose Saranga, a low-power ultrasound-based perception stack that localizes obstacles using a dual sonar array. We present two key solutions to combat the low Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio of $-4.9$ decibels: physical noise reduction and a deep learning based denoising method. Firstly, we present a practical way to block propeller induced ultrasound noise on the weak echoes. The second solution is to train a neural network to utilize the \textcolor{black}{long horizon of ultrasound echoes} for finding signal patterns under high amounts of uncorrelated noise where classical methods were insufficient. We generalize to the real world by using a synthetic data generation pipeline and limited real noise data for training. We enable a palm-sized aerial robot to navigate in visually degraded conditions of dense fog, darkness, and snow in a cluttered environment with thin and transparent obstacles using only on-board sensing and computation. We provide extensive real world results to demonstrate the efficacy of our approach.

摘要（中文）: 微型掌上无人机在导航受限和杂乱环境方面具有卓越的敏捷性和成本效益。然而，其有限的有效载荷能力直接限制了机载传感套件，从而限制了在全球定位系统（GPS）受限的野外场景中的关键导航任务。常用的避障方法使用相机和激光雷达（LIDAR），但在视觉退化条件下，如低能见度、灰尘、雾或黑暗中，这些方法会变得无效。其他传感器，如雷达（RADAR），功耗很高，不适合微型无人机。受蝙蝠的启发，我们提出了Saranga，一种基于超声波的低功耗感知堆栈，它使用双声呐阵列来定位障碍物。我们提出了两种关键解决方案来应对-4.9分贝的低峰值信噪比：物理噪声降低和基于深度学习的去噪方法。首先，我们提出了一种实用的方法来阻挡螺旋桨引起的超声噪声对微弱回声的影响。第二个解决方案是训练一个神经网络，利用超声波回声的长时程来寻找大量不相关噪声下的信号模式，而经典方法无法胜任。我们通过使用合成数据生成管道和有限的真实噪声数据进行训练，推广到现实世界。我们使掌上无人机能够在仅使用机载传感和计算的情况下，在杂乱环境中浓雾、黑暗和降雪等视觉退化条件下，通过稀薄和透明的障碍物进行导航。我们提供了大量的真实世界结果来证明我们方法的有效性。

TLDR: The paper presents Saranga, a low-power ultrasound-based perception stack for enabling navigation in visually degraded environments on palm-sized aerial robots by employing physical noise reduction and deep learning-based denoising.

简要说明: 该论文提出了Saranga，一种低功耗的、基于超声波的感知系统，通过物理降噪和基于深度学习的去噪方法，使掌上无人机能够在视觉退化的环境中导航。

Authors: Manoj Velmurugan, Phillip Brush, Colin Balfour, Richard J. Przybyla, Nitin J. Sanket

Natural-Language Agent Harnesses

Abstract (English): Agent performance increasingly depends on \emph{harness engineering}, yet harness design is usually buried in controller code and runtime-specific conventions, making it hard to transfer, compare, and study as a scientific object. We ask whether the high-level control logic of an agent harness can instead be externalized as a portable executable artifact. We introduce \textbf{Natural-Language Agent Harnesses} (NLAHs), which express harness behavior in editable natural language, and \textbf{Intelligent Harness Runtime} (IHR), a shared runtime that executes these harnesses through explicit contracts, durable artifacts, and lightweight adapters. Across coding and computer-use benchmarks, we conduct controlled evaluations of operational viability, module ablation, and code-to-text harness migration.

摘要（中文）: 智能体的性能越来越依赖于\emph{线路工程设计}，然而线路设计通常隐藏在控制器代码和运行时特定的约定中，这使得它难以作为一种科学对象进行迁移、比较和研究。我们探究是否可以将智能体线路的高级控制逻辑外化为可移植的可执行工件。我们引入了\textbf{自然语言智能体线路} (NLAHs)，它使用可编辑的自然语言表达线路行为，以及\textbf{智能线路运行时} (IHR)，一个通过显式契约、持久工件和轻量级适配器来执行这些线路的共享运行时。在编程和计算机使用基准测试中，我们对可操作性、模块消融和代码到文本线路迁移进行了受控评估。

TLDR: The paper introduces Natural-Language Agent Harnesses (NLAHs) and an Intelligent Harness Runtime (IHR) to externalize and execute agent harness behavior using natural language, demonstrating viability and transferability across coding and computer-use benchmarks.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种自然语言Agent Harness（NLAH）和一个智能Harness运行环境（IHR），该方法可以通过自然语言外部化并执行agent harness行为，并在编码和计算机使用benchmarks中展示了可行性和可移植性。

Authors: Linyue Pan, Lexiao Zou, Shuo Guo, Jingchen Ni, Hai-Tao Zheng

A Gait Foundation Model Predicts Multi-System Health Phenotypes from 3D Skeletal Motion

Abstract (English): Gait is increasingly recognized as a vital sign, yet current approaches treat it as a symptom of specific pathologies rather than a systemic biomarker. We developed a gait foundation model for 3D skeletal motion from 3,414 deeply phenotyped adults, recorded via a depth camera during five motor tasks. Learned embeddings outperformed engineered features, predicting age (Pearson r = 0.69), BMI (r = 0.90), and visceral adipose tissue area (r = 0.82). Embeddings significantly predicted 1,980 of 3,210 phenotypic targets; after adjustment for age, BMI, VAT, and height, gait provided independent gains in all 18 body systems in males and 17 of 18 in females, and improved prediction of clinical diagnoses and medication use. Anatomical ablation revealed that legs dominated metabolic and frailty predictions while torso encoded sleep and lifestyle phenotypes. These findings establish gait as an independent multi-system biosignal, motivating translation to consumer-grade video and its integration as a scalable, passive vital sign.

摘要（中文）: 步态正日益被认为是重要的生命体征，但当前的方法将其视为特定病理的症状，而非全身性生物标志物。我们基于3,414名深度表型成人，通过深度相机记录的五个运动任务中的3D骨骼运动数据，开发了一个步态基础模型。该模型学习到的嵌入特征优于人工设计的特征，能预测年龄（Pearson相关系数r = 0.69）、身体质量指数BMI（r = 0.90）和内脏脂肪组织面积（r = 0.82）。嵌入特征显著预测了3,210个表型目标中的1,980个；在校正年龄、BMI、内脏脂肪组织面积和身高后，步态对男性所有18个身体系统和女性18个身体系统中的17个系统提供了独立的增益，并提高了对临床诊断和药物使用的预测。解剖消融分析显示，腿部在代谢和虚弱预测中占据主导地位，而躯干则编码了睡眠和生活方式表型。这些发现确立了步态作为一种独立的、多系统的生物信号，推动了其向消费级视频的转化，并将其作为一种可扩展的被动生命体征进行整合。

TLDR: This paper introduces a gait foundation model trained on skeletal motion data that effectively predicts a wide range of health phenotypes, demonstrating gait's potential as a comprehensive biosignal. This opens avenues for passive health monitoring using consumer-grade video.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一个步态基础模型，该模型在骨骼运动数据上训练，能够有效预测广泛的健康表型，证明了步态作为综合生物信号的潜力。这为使用消费级视频进行被动健康监测开辟了道路。

Authors: Adam Gabet, Sarah Kohn, Guy Lutsker, Shira Gelman, Anastasia Godneva, Gil Sasson, Arad Zulti, David Krongauz, Rotem Shaulitch, Assaf Rotem, Ohad Doron, Yuval Brodsky, Adina Weinberger, Eran Segal

Decentralized Task Scheduling in Distributed Systems: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Approach

Abstract (English): Efficient task scheduling in large-scale distributed systems presents significant challenges due to dynamic workloads, heterogeneous resources, and competing quality-of-service requirements. Traditional centralized approaches face scalability limitations and single points of failure, while classical heuristics lack adaptability to changing conditions. This paper proposes a decentralized multi-agent deep reinforcement learning (DRL-MADRL) framework for task scheduling in heterogeneous distributed systems. We formulate the problem as a Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (Dec-POMDP) and develop a lightweight actor-critic architecture implemented using only NumPy, enabling deployment on resource-constrained edge devices without heavyweight machine learning frameworks. Using workload characteristics derived from the publicly available Google Cluster Trace dataset, we evaluate our approach on a 100-node heterogeneous system processing 1,000 tasks per episode over 30 experimental runs. Experimental results demonstrate 15.6% improvement in average task completion time (30.8s vs 36.5s for random baseline), 15.2% energy efficiency gain (745.2 kWh vs 878.3 kWh), and 82.3% SLA satisfaction compared to 75.5% for baselines, with all improvements statistically significant (p < 0.001). The lightweight implementation requires only NumPy, Matplotlib, and SciPy. Complete source code and experimental data are provided for full reproducibility at https://github.com/danielbenniah/marl-distributed-scheduling.

摘要（中文）: 大规模分布式系统中高效的任务调度面临着诸多挑战，包括动态的工作负载、异构的资源以及相互竞争的服务质量需求。传统的集中式方法面临着可扩展性限制和单点故障，而经典的启发式方法则缺乏对变化条件的适应性。本文提出了一种用于异构分布式系统中任务调度的去中心化多智能体深度强化学习 (DRL-MADRL) 框架。我们将该问题建模为去中心化部分可观察马尔可夫决策过程 (Dec-POMDP)，并开发了一种轻量级的 Actor-Critic 架构，该架构仅使用 NumPy 实现，从而能够在资源受限的边缘设备上部署，而无需重量级的机器学习框架。我们使用从公开的 Google 集群轨迹数据集导出的工作负载特征，在一个 100 节点异构系统上评估了我们的方法，该系统在 30 次实验中，每次处理 1,000 个任务。实验结果表明，与随机基线相比，平均任务完成时间提高了 15.6%（30.8 秒 vs 36.5 秒），能源效率提高了 15.2%（745.2 kWh vs 878.3 kWh），服务水平协议 (SLA) 满足度为 82.3%，而基线为 75.5%，所有改进均具有统计学意义（p < 0.001）。该轻量级实现仅需要 NumPy、Matplotlib 和 SciPy。完整的源代码和实验数据已在 https://github.com/danielbenniah/marl-distributed-scheduling 上提供，以实现完全的可复现性。

TLDR: This paper presents a decentralized multi-agent deep reinforcement learning (DRL-MADRL) framework for task scheduling in heterogeneous distributed systems, achieving significant improvements in task completion time, energy efficiency, and SLA satisfaction compared to baselines, with a lightweight implementation for edge deployment.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种用于异构分布式系统中任务调度的去中心化多智能体深度强化学习 (DRL-MADRL) 框架，与基线相比，在任务完成时间、能源效率和 SLA 满意度方面取得了显着改进，并且该实现是轻量级的，适合边缘部署。

Authors: Daniel Benniah John

AnyHand: A Large-Scale Synthetic Dataset for RGB(-D) Hand Pose Estimation

Abstract (English): We present AnyHand, a large-scale synthetic dataset designed to advance the state of the art in 3D hand pose estimation from both RGB-only and RGB-D inputs. While recent works with foundation approaches have shown that an increase in the quantity and diversity of training data can markedly improve performance and robustness in hand pose estimation, existing real-world-collected datasets on this task are limited in coverage, and prior synthetic datasets rarely provide occlusions, arm details, and aligned depth together at scale. To address this bottleneck, our AnyHand contains 2.5M single-hand and 4.1M hand-object interaction RGB-D images, with rich geometric annotations. In the RGB-only setting, we show that extending the original training sets of existing baselines with AnyHand yields significant gains on multiple benchmarks (FreiHAND and HO-3D), even when keeping the architecture and training scheme fixed. More impressively, the model trained with AnyHand shows stronger generalization to the out-of-domain HO-Cap dataset, without any fine-tuning. We also contribute a lightweight depth fusion module that can be easily integrated into existing RGB-based models. Trained with AnyHand, the resulting RGB-D model achieves superior performance on the HO-3D benchmark, showing the benefits of depth integration and the effectiveness of our synthetic data.

摘要（中文）: 我们提出了AnyHand，一个大规模的合成数据集，旨在推进基于仅RGB和RGB-D输入的3D手部姿态估计技术水平。 近期基于基础模型的研究表明，训练数据的数量和多样性的增加可以显著提高手部姿态估计的性能和鲁棒性，但现有真实世界采集的数据集在覆盖范围上存在局限性，并且之前的合成数据集很少能大规模地提供遮挡、手臂细节和对齐的深度信息。 为了解决这个瓶颈，我们的AnyHand包含了250万张单手和410万张手部-物体交互的RGB-D图像，并具有丰富的几何标注。 在仅RGB的设置下，我们表明，通过使用AnyHand扩展现有基线的原始训练集，可以在多个基准（FreiHAND和HO-3D）上获得显著提升，即使保持架构和训练方案不变。 更令人印象深刻的是，使用AnyHand训练的模型对域外HO-Cap数据集表现出更强的泛化能力，无需任何微调。 我们还贡献了一个轻量级的深度融合模块，可以轻松地集成到现有的基于RGB的模型中。 使用AnyHand训练后，所得到的RGB-D模型在HO-3D基准上实现了卓越的性能，表明了深度集成的优势和我们合成数据的有效性。

TLDR: The paper introduces AnyHand, a large-scale synthetic RGB-D hand pose estimation dataset (2.5M single-hand and 4.1M hand-object interaction images) which improves performance and generalization on benchmarks when used for training, even for RGB-only models and out-of-domain datasets.

Authors: Chen Si, Yulin Liu, Bo Ai, Jianwen Xie, Rolandos Alexandros Potamias, Chuanxia Zheng, Hao Su

LEMMA: Laplacian pyramids for Efficient Marine SeMAntic Segmentation

Abstract (English): Semantic segmentation in marine environments is crucial for the autonomous navigation of unmanned surface vessels (USVs) and coastal Earth Observation events such as oil spills. However, existing methods, often relying on deep CNNs and transformer-based architectures, face challenges in deployment due to their high computational costs and resource-intensive nature. These limitations hinder the practicality of real-time, low-cost applications in real-world marine settings. To address this, we propose LEMMA, a lightweight semantic segmentation model designed specifically for accurate remote sensing segmentation under resource constraints. The proposed architecture leverages Laplacian Pyramids to enhance edge recognition, a critical component for effective feature extraction in complex marine environments for disaster response, environmental surveillance, and coastal monitoring. By integrating edge information early in the feature extraction process, LEMMA eliminates the need for computationally expensive feature map computations in deeper network layers, drastically reducing model size, complexity and inference time. LEMMA demonstrates state-of-the-art performance across datasets captured from diverse platforms while reducing trainable parameters and computational requirements by up to 71x, GFLOPs by up to 88.5\%, and inference time by up to 84.65\%, as compared to existing models. Experimental results highlight its effectiveness and real-world applicability, including 93.42\% IoU on the Oil Spill dataset and 98.97\% mIoU on Mastr1325.

摘要（中文）: 海洋环境中的语义分割对于无人水面艇（USV）的自主导航以及诸如漏油事件等沿海地球观测任务至关重要。然而，现有的方法，通常依赖于深度卷积神经网络（CNNs）和基于Transformer的架构，由于其高昂的计算成本和资源密集型特性，在部署方面面临挑战。这些限制阻碍了其在现实世界海洋环境中实时、低成本应用的实用性。 为了解决这个问题，我们提出了 LEMMA，一个专为资源约束条件下精确遥感分割而设计的轻量级语义分割模型。所提出的架构利用拉普拉斯金字塔来增强边缘识别能力，这是在复杂海洋环境中进行灾害响应、环境监测和海岸监测时有效特征提取的关键组成部分。通过在特征提取过程的早期集成边缘信息，LEMMA 消除了在更深层网络中进行计算密集型特征图计算的需求，从而大幅降低了模型大小、复杂性和推理时间。与现有模型相比，LEMMA 在从不同平台捕获的数据集上展示了最先进的性能，同时将可训练参数减少高达 71 倍，GFLOPs 减少高达 88.5%，推理时间减少高达 84.65%。实验结果突出了其有效性和现实应用性，包括在漏油数据集上达到 93.42% 的 IoU 和在 Mastr1325 数据集上达到 98.97% 的 mIoU。

TLDR: The paper presents LEMMA, a lightweight semantic segmentation model using Laplacian Pyramids for improved edge recognition in marine environments, achieving state-of-the-art performance with significantly reduced computational costs.

简要说明: 该论文提出了LEMMA，一种轻量级的语义分割模型，它使用拉普拉斯金字塔来提高海洋环境中边缘识别的准确性，并在显著降低计算成本的同时实现了最先进的性能。

Authors: Ishaan Gakhar, Laven Srivastava, Sankarshanaa Sagaram, Aditya Kasliwal, Ujjwal Verma

Hyperspectral Trajectory Image for Multi-Month Trajectory Anomaly Detection

Abstract (English): Trajectory anomaly detection underpins applications from fraud detection to urban mobility analysis. Dense GPS methods preserve fine-grained evidence such as abnormal speeds and short-duration events, but their quadratic cost makes multi-month analysis intractable; consequently, no existing approach detects anomalies over multi-month dense GPS trajectories. The field instead relies on scalable sparse stay-point methods that discard this evidence, forcing separate architectures for each regime and preventing knowledge transfer. We argue this bottleneck is unnecessary: human trajectories, dense or sparse, share a natural two-dimensional cyclic structure along within-day and across-day axes. We therefore propose TITAnD (Trajectory Image Transformer for Anomaly Detection), which reformulates trajectory anomaly detection as a vision problem by representing trajectories as a Hyperspectral Trajectory Image (HTI): a day x time-of-day grid whose channels encode spatial, semantic, temporal, and kinematic information from either modality, unifying both under a single representation. Under this formulation, agent-level detection reduces to image classification and temporal localization to semantic segmentation. To model this representation, we introduce the Cyclic Factorized Transformer (CFT), which factorizes attention along the two temporal axes, encoding the cyclic inductive bias of human routines, while reducing attention cost by orders of magnitude and enabling dense multi-month anomaly detection for the first time. Empirically, TITAnD achieves the best AUC-PR across sparse and dense benchmarks, surpassing vision models like UNet while being 11-75x faster than the Transformer with comparable memory, demonstrating that vision reformulation and structure-aware modeling are jointly essential. Code will be made public soon.

摘要（中文）: 轨迹异常检测是欺诈检测到城市交通分析等应用的基础。 密集GPS方法保留了诸如异常速度和短时事件等细粒度证据，但其二次复杂度使得多月份分析变得难以处理； 因此，目前没有现有方法可以在多月份密集GPS轨迹上检测异常。 该领域转而依赖于可扩展的稀疏停留点方法，该方法丢弃了这些证据，迫使每种模式使用单独的架构，并阻止了知识转移。 我们认为这种瓶颈是不必要的：人类轨迹，无论是密集还是稀疏，都沿着日内和日间轴共享一个自然的二维循环结构。 因此，我们提出了TITAnD（用于异常检测的轨迹图像Transformer），它通过将轨迹表示为高光谱轨迹图像（HTI）将轨迹异常检测重新定义为一个视觉问题：一个天x时间网格，其通道编码来自任一模态的空间、语义、时间和运动学信息，将两者统一在一个单一表示下。 在这种公式下，主体级别检测减少为图像分类，而时间定位减少为语义分割。 为了建模这种表示，我们引入了循环因子分解Transformer（CFT），它沿着两个时间轴分解注意力，编码人类例程的循环归纳偏置，同时将注意力成本降低几个数量级，并首次实现密集的数月异常检测。 实验表明，TITAnD在稀疏和密集基准测试中都实现了最佳的AUC-PR，超过了像UNet这样的视觉模型，同时比具有可比内存的Transformer快11-75倍，这表明视觉重新定义和结构感知建模共同至关重要。 代码即将公开。

TLDR: The paper introduces TITAnD, a method for multi-month trajectory anomaly detection that reformulates the problem as image classification and semantic segmentation using a Hyperspectral Trajectory Image (HTI) representation and a Cyclic Factorized Transformer (CFT). It achieves state-of-the-art results on both sparse and dense trajectory datasets with improved efficiency.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了TITAnD，一种用于多月轨迹异常检测的方法，它通过使用高光谱轨迹图像 (HTI) 表示和循环分解 Transformer (CFT) 将问题重新定义为图像分类和语义分割。该方法在稀疏和密集轨迹数据集上均取得了最先进的结果，并且效率更高。

Authors: Md Awsafur Rahman, Chandrakanth Gudavalli, Hardik Prajapati, B. S. Manjunath

AG-EgoPose: Leveraging Action-Guided Motion and Kinematic Joint Encoding for Egocentric 3D Pose Estimation

Abstract (English): Egocentric 3D human pose estimation remains challenging due to severe perspective distortion, limited body visibility, and complex camera motion inherent in first-person viewpoints. Existing methods typically rely on single-frame analysis or limited temporal fusion, which fails to effectively leverage the rich motion context available in egocentric videos. We introduce AG-EgoPose, a novel dual-stream framework that integrates short- and long-range motion context with fine-grained spatial cues for robust pose estimation from fisheye camera input. Our framework features two parallel streams: A spatial stream uses a weight-sharing ResNet-18 encoder-decoder to generate 2D joint heatmaps and corresponding joint-specific spatial feature tokens. Simultaneously, a temporal stream uses a ResNet-50 backbone to extract visual features, which are then processed by an action recognition backbone to capture the motion dynamics. These complementary representations are fused and refined in a transformer decoder with learnable joint tokens, which allows for the joint-level integration of spatial and temporal evidence while maintaining anatomical constraints. Experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that AG-EgoPose achieves state-of-the-art performance in both quantitative and qualitative metrics. Code is available at: https://github.com/Mushfiq5647/AG-EgoPose.

摘要（中文）: 由于第一人称视角固有的严重透视畸变、有限的身体可见性和复杂的相机运动，以自我为中心的3D人体姿态估计仍然具有挑战性。现有方法通常依赖于单帧分析或有限的时间融合，无法有效地利用以自我为中心的视频中丰富的运动上下文。我们引入了AG-EgoPose，一种新颖的双流框架，该框架集成了短程和长程运动上下文以及精细的空间线索，从而可以从鱼眼相机输入中进行鲁棒的姿态估计。我们的框架具有两个并行流：空间流使用权重共享的ResNet-18编码器-解码器来生成2D关节热图和相应的关节特定空间特征令牌。同时，时间流使用ResNet-50骨干网络来提取视觉特征，然后通过动作识别骨干网络进行处理，以捕获运动动态。这些互补的表示在具有可学习关节令牌的Transformer解码器中进行融合和细化，从而允许关节级别的空间和时间证据集成，同时保持解剖学约束。在真实世界数据集上的实验表明，AG-EgoPose在定量和定性指标方面均取得了最先进的性能。 代码可在以下位置获得：https://github.com/Mushfiq5647/AG-EgoPose。

TLDR: The paper introduces AG-EgoPose, a dual-stream framework for egocentric 3D pose estimation that fuses spatial and temporal information using action recognition and transformers, achieving state-of-the-art results.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了AG-EgoPose，这是一个双流框架，用于以自我为中心的3D姿态估计，它使用动作识别和transformers融合空间和时间信息，从而实现了最先进的结果。

Authors: Md Mushfiqur Azam, John Quarles, Kevin Desai

EgoXtreme: A Dataset for Robust Object Pose Estimation in Egocentric Views under Extreme Conditions

Abstract (English): Smart glass is emerging as an useful device since it provides plenty of insights under hands-busy, eyes-on-task situations. To understand the context of the wearer, 6D object pose estimation in egocentric view is becoming essential. However, existing 6D object pose estimation benchmarks fail to capture the challenges of real-world egocentric applications, which are often dominated by severe motion blur, dynamic illumination, and visual obstructions. This discrepancy creates a significant gap between controlled lab data and chaotic real-world application. To bridge this gap, we introduce EgoXtreme, a new large-scale 6D pose estimation dataset captured entirely from an egocentric perspective. EgoXtreme features three challenging scenarios - industrial maintenance, sports, and emergency rescue - designed to introduce severe perceptual ambiguities through extreme lighting, heavy motion blur, and smoke. Evaluations of state-of-the-art generalizable pose estimators on EgoXtreme indicate that their generalization fails to hold in extreme conditions, especially under low light. We further demonstrate that simply applying image restoration (e.g., deblurring) offers no positive improvement for extreme conditions. While performance gain has appeared in tracking-based approach, implying using temporal information in fast-motion scenarios is meaningful. We conclude that EgoXtreme is an essential resource for developing and evaluating the next generation of pose estimation models robust enough for real-world egocentric vision. The dataset and code are available at https://taegyoun88.github.io/EgoXtreme/

摘要（中文）: 智能眼镜正逐渐成为一种有用的设备，因为它在双手繁忙、眼睛专注于任务的情况下提供了丰富的洞察力。为了理解佩戴者的情境，以自我中心视角进行6D物体姿态估计变得至关重要。然而，现有的6D物体姿态估计基准测试无法捕捉到真实世界自我中心应用的挑战，这些应用通常受到严重的运动模糊、动态光照和视觉遮挡的影响。这种差异在受控的实验室数据和混乱的真实世界应用之间造成了巨大的差距。为了弥合这一差距，我们引入了一个新的大规模6D姿态估计数据集EgoXtreme，该数据集完全以自我中心视角捕获。EgoXtreme 以三个具有挑战性的场景为特色——工业维护、运动和紧急救援——旨在通过极端光照、严重的运动模糊和烟雾来引入严重的感知歧义。对EgoXtreme上最先进的通用姿态估计器的评估表明，它们的泛化能力在极端条件下失效，尤其是在低光照下。我们进一步证明，仅应用图像恢复（例如，去模糊）并不能对极端条件产生积极的改进。虽然基于跟踪方法出现了性能提升，这意味着在快速运动场景中使用时间信息是有意义的。我们得出结论，EgoXtreme是开发和评估下一代姿态估计模型的重要资源，该模型应足够鲁棒，以适应真实世界的自我中心视觉。数据集和代码可在https://taegyoun88.github.io/EgoXtreme/ 获取。

TLDR: The paper introduces EgoXtreme, a new egocentric 6D object pose estimation dataset focusing on extreme conditions like motion blur, low light, and obstructions, highlighting the limitations of current pose estimation models in such scenarios.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了EgoXtreme，一个新的以自我为中心的6D物体姿态估计数据集，专注于极端条件，如运动模糊、低光照和遮挡，突出了当前姿态估计模型在这些场景中的局限性。

Authors: Taegyoon Yoon, Yegyu Han, Seojin Ji, Jaewoo Park, Sojeong Kim, Taein Kwon, Hyung-Sin Kim

GaussFusion: Improving 3D Reconstruction in the Wild with A Geometry-Informed Video Generator

Abstract (English): We present GaussFusion, a novel approach for improving 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) reconstructions in the wild through geometry-informed video generation. GaussFusion mitigates common 3DGS artifacts, including floaters, flickering, and blur caused by camera pose errors, incomplete coverage, and noisy geometry initialization. Unlike prior RGB-based approaches limited to a single reconstruction pipeline, our method introduces a geometry-informed video-to-video generator that refines 3DGS renderings across both optimization-based and feed-forward methods. Given an existing reconstruction, we render a Gaussian primitive video buffer encoding depth, normals, opacity, and covariance, which the generator refines to produce temporally coherent, artifact-free frames. We further introduce an artifact synthesis pipeline that simulates diverse degradation patterns, ensuring robustness and generalization. GaussFusion achieves state-of-the-art performance on novel-view synthesis benchmarks, and an efficient variant runs in real time at 21 FPS while maintaining similar performance, enabling interactive 3D applications.

摘要（中文）: 我们提出了GaussFusion，一种通过几何信息指导视频生成来改进野外环境下3D高斯溅射 (3DGS) 重建的新方法。GaussFusion 减轻了常见的3DGS伪影，包括由相机姿态误差、不完整覆盖和噪声几何初始化引起的漂浮物、闪烁和模糊。与之前局限于单一重建流程的基于RGB的方法不同，我们的方法引入了一个几何信息指导的视频到视频生成器，可以跨基于优化的方法和前馈方法来优化3DGS渲染结果。给定一个现有的重建结果，我们渲染一个高斯基元视频缓冲，该缓冲编码深度、法线、不透明度和协方差，生成器对其进行优化以产生时间上连贯且无伪影的帧。我们进一步引入了一个伪影合成流程，该流程模拟各种退化模式，从而确保鲁棒性和泛化能力。GaussFusion 在新视角合成基准测试中实现了最先进的性能，并且一个高效变体可以以 21 FPS 的实时速度运行，同时保持相似的性能，从而实现交互式 3D 应用。

TLDR: The paper introduces GaussFusion, a geometry-informed video generator that refines 3D Gaussian splatting reconstructions to mitigate artifacts like floaters and flickering, achieving state-of-the-art performance and real-time capability.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为GaussFusion的方法，它是一个几何信息驱动的视频生成器，用于优化3D高斯溅射重建，以减少如漂浮物和闪烁等伪影，实现了最先进的性能和实时能力。

Authors: Liyuan Zhu, Manjunath Narayana, Michal Stary, Will Hutchcroft, Gordon Wetzstein, Iro Armeni

MoRGS: Efficient Per-Gaussian Motion Reasoning for Streamable Dynamic 3D Scenes

Abstract (English): Online reconstruction of dynamic scenes aims to learn from streaming multi-view inputs under low-latency constraints. The fast training and real-time rendering capabilities of 3D Gaussian Splatting have made on-the-fly reconstruction practically feasible, enabling online 4D reconstruction. However, existing online approaches, despite their efficiency and visual quality, fail to learn per-Gaussian motion that reflects true scene dynamics. Without explicit motion cues, appearance and motion are optimized solely under photometric loss, causing per-Gaussian motion to chase pixel residuals rather than true 3D motion. To address this, we propose MoRGS, an efficient online per-Gaussian motion reasoning framework that explicitly models per-Gaussian motion to improve 4D reconstruction quality. Specifically, we leverage optical flow on a sparse set of key views as lightweight motion cues that regularize per-Gaussian motion beyond photometric supervision. To compensate for the sparsity of flow supervision, we learn a per-Gaussian motion offset field that reconciles discrepancies between projected 3D motion and observed flow across views and time. In addition, we introduce a per-Gaussian motion confidence that separates dynamic from static Gaussians and weights Gaussian attribute residual updates, thereby suppressing redundant motion in static regions for better temporal consistency and accelerating the modeling of large motions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MoRGS achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction quality and motion fidelity among online methods, while maintaining streamable performance.

摘要（中文）: 动态场景的在线重建旨在从低延迟约束下的流式多视角输入中进行学习。3D高斯溅射的快速训练和实时渲染能力使得实时重建在实践上可行，从而实现了在线4D重建。然而，现有的在线方法虽然具有高效性和视觉质量，但未能学习反映真实场景动态的逐高斯运动。在没有明确运动线索的情况下，外观和运动仅在光度损失下进行优化，导致逐高斯运动追逐像素残差，而非真实的3D运动。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了MoRGS，一个高效的在线逐高斯运动推理框架，它显式地建模逐高斯运动以提高4D重建质量。具体来说，我们利用稀疏关键帧视图上的光流作为轻量级的运动线索，对超出光度监督的逐高斯运动进行正则化。为了弥补光流监督的稀疏性，我们学习了一个逐高斯运动偏移场，以协调跨视图和时间投影的3D运动与观测到的光流之间的差异。此外，我们引入了一个逐高斯运动置信度，用于区分动态高斯和静态高斯，并加权高斯属性残差更新，从而抑制静态区域的冗余运动，以获得更好的时间一致性，并加速大型运动的建模。大量的实验表明，MoRGS在在线方法中实现了最先进的重建质量和运动保真度，同时保持了可流式传输的性能。

TLDR: The paper introduces MoRGS, an efficient online per-Gaussian motion reasoning framework for dynamic 3D scene reconstruction, leveraging optical flow and motion confidence to improve reconstruction quality and motion fidelity.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为MoRGS的高效在线高斯运动推理框架，用于动态3D场景重建，利用光流和运动置信度来提高重建质量和运动保真度。

Authors: Wonjoon Lee, Sungmin Woo, Donghyeong Kim, Jungho Lee, Sangheon Park, Sangyoun Lee

Distributed Real-Time Vehicle Control for Emergency Vehicle Transit: A Scalable Cooperative Method

Abstract (English): Rapid transit of emergency vehicles is critical for saving lives and reducing property loss but often relies on surrounding ordinary vehicles to cooperatively adjust their driving behaviors. It is important to ensure rapid transit of emergency vehicles while minimizing the impact on ordinary vehicles. Centralized mathematical solver and reinforcement learning are the state-of-the-art methods. The former obtains optimal solutions but is only practical for small-scale scenarios. The latter implicitly learns through extensive centralized training but the trained model exhibits limited scalability to different traffic conditions. Hence, existing methods suffer from two fundamental limitations: high computational cost and lack of scalability. To overcome above limitations, this work proposes a scalable distributed vehicle control method, where vehicles adjust their driving behaviors in a distributed manner online using only local instead of global information. We proved that the proposed distributed method using only local information is approximately equivalent to the one using global information, which enables vehicles to evaluate their candidate states and make approximately optimal decisions in real time without pre-training and with natural adaptability to varying traffic conditions. Then, a distributed conflict resolution mechanism is further proposed to guarantee vehicles' safety by avoiding their decision conflicts, which eliminates the single-point-of-failure risk of centralized methods and provides deterministic safety guarantees that learned methods cannot offer. Compared with existing methods, simulation experiments based on real-world traffic datasets demonstrate that the proposed method achieves faster decision-making, less impact on ordinary vehicles, and maintains much stronger scalability across different traffic densities and road configurations.

摘要（中文）: 紧急车辆的快速通行对于拯救生命和减少财产损失至关重要，但这通常依赖于周围普通车辆协同调整其驾驶行为。确保紧急车辆快速通行的同时，尽量减少对普通车辆的影响非常重要。集中式数学求解器和强化学习是目前最先进的方法。前者可以获得最优解，但仅适用于小规模场景。后者通过大量的集中式训练进行隐式学习，但训练后的模型对于不同交通状况的可扩展性有限。因此，现有方法存在两个根本性的局限性：高计算成本和缺乏可扩展性。为了克服上述局限性，本文提出了一种可扩展的分布式车辆控制方法，车辆仅使用局部信息而非全局信息，以分布式的在线方式调整其驾驶行为。我们证明了所提出的仅使用局部信息的分布式方法与使用全局信息的方法近似等价，这使车辆能够评估其候选状态，并在没有预训练的情况下实时做出近似最优决策，并能自然地适应不同的交通状况。此外，我们进一步提出了一种分布式冲突解决机制，通过避免车辆决策冲突来保证车辆安全，从而消除了集中式方法的单点故障风险，并提供了学习方法无法提供的确定性安全保障。基于真实交通数据集的仿真实验表明，与现有方法相比，所提出的方法实现了更快的决策速度，对普通车辆的影响更小，并在不同的交通密度和道路配置下保持了更强的可扩展性。

TLDR: The paper proposes a scalable, distributed vehicle control method for emergency vehicle transit that utilizes local information for real-time decision-making, offering improvements in speed, impact on ordinary vehicles, and scalability compared to centralized approaches.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种可扩展的分布式车辆控制方法，用于紧急车辆通行。该方法利用本地信息进行实时决策，与集中式方法相比，在速度、对普通车辆的影响和可扩展性方面都有所提高。

Authors: WenXi Wang, JunQi Zhang

Towards Video Anomaly Detection from Event Streams: A Baseline and Benchmark Datasets

Abstract (English): Event-based vision, characterized by low redundancy, focus on dynamic motion, and inherent privacy-preserving properties, naturally fits the demands of video anomaly detection (VAD). However, the absence of dedicated event-stream anomaly detection datasets and effective modeling strategies has significantly hindered progress in this field. In this work, we take the first major step toward establishing event-based VAD as a unified research direction. We first construct multiple event-stream based benchmarks for video anomaly detection, featuring synchronized event and RGB recordings. Leveraging the unique properties of events, we then propose an EVent-centric spatiotemporal Video Anomaly Detection framework, namely EWAD, with three key innovations: an event density aware dynamic sampling strategy to select temporally informative segments; a density-modulated temporal modeling approach that captures contextual relations from sparse event streams; and an RGB-to-event knowledge distillation mechanism to enhance event-based representations under weak supervision. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate that our EWAD achieves significant improvements over existing approaches, highlighting the potential and effectiveness of event-driven modeling for video anomaly detection. The benchmark datasets will be made publicly available.

摘要（中文）: 基于事件的视觉，以其低冗余度、对动态运动的关注和固有的隐私保护特性为特征，天然地契合了视频异常检测（VAD）的需求。然而，缺乏专门的事件流异常检测数据集和有效的建模策略，极大地阻碍了该领域的发展。 在这项工作中，我们迈出了构建基于事件的VAD作为一个统一研究方向的第一个重要步骤。我们首先构建了多个基于事件流的视频异常检测基准数据集，其特点是同步的事件和RGB记录。 借助事件的独特属性，我们随后提出了一个以事件为中心的时空视频异常检测框架，即EWAD，它具有三个关键创新：一种事件密度感知动态采样策略，用于选择时间上信息量丰富的片段；一种密度调节的时间建模方法，用于捕捉来自稀疏事件流的上下文关系；以及一种RGB到事件的知识蒸馏机制，以增强弱监督下的基于事件的表征。 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的EWAD相比现有方法取得了显著的改进，突出了事件驱动建模在视频异常检测中的潜力和有效性。 基准数据集将公开提供。

TLDR: This paper introduces several event-stream based benchmark datasets for video anomaly detection and proposes an event-centric spatiotemporal anomaly detection framework (EWAD) that outperforms existing methods.

简要说明: 本文介绍了几个基于事件流的视频异常检测基准数据集，并提出了一个以事件为中心的时空异常检测框架 (EWAD)，该框架优于现有方法。

Authors: Peng Wu, Yuting Yan, Guansong Pang, Yujia Sun, Qingsen Yan, Peng Wang, Yanning Zhang

DRoPS: Dynamic 3D Reconstruction of Pre-Scanned Objects

Abstract (English): Dynamic scene reconstruction from casual videos has seen recent remarkable progress. Numerous approaches have attempted to overcome the ill-posedness of the task by distilling priors from 2D foundational models and by imposing hand-crafted regularization on the optimized motion. However, these methods struggle to reconstruct scenes from extreme novel viewpoints, especially when highly articulated motions are present. In this paper, we present DRoPS, a novel approach that leverages a static pre-scan of the dynamic object as an explicit geometric and appearance prior. While existing state-of-the-art methods fail to fully exploit the pre-scan, DRoPS leverages our novel setup to effectively constrain the solution space and ensure geometrical consistency throughout the sequence. The core of our novelty is twofold: first, we establish a grid-structured and surface-aligned model by organizing Gaussian primitives into pixel grids anchored to the object surface. Second, by leveraging the grid structure of our primitives, we parameterize motion using a CNN conditioned on those grids, injecting strong implicit regularization and correlating the motion of nearby points. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms the current state of the art in rendering quality and 3D tracking accuracy.

摘要（中文）: 从随意的视频中进行动态场景重建最近取得了显著进展。许多方法试图通过从二维基础模型中提取先验知识以及对优化后的运动施加手工设计的正则化来克服该任务的不适定性。然而，这些方法在从极端新视角重建场景时表现不佳，尤其是在存在高度铰接运动时。在本文中，我们提出了一种新颖的方法DRoPS，该方法利用动态对象的静态预扫描作为显式的几何和外观先验。虽然现有的最先进方法未能充分利用预扫描，但DRoPS利用我们新颖的设置来有效地约束解空间，并确保整个序列中的几何一致性。我们的创新核心体现在两个方面：首先，我们通过将高斯基元组织成锚定在物体表面的像素网格，建立一个网格结构化且表面对齐的模型。其次，通过利用基元的网格结构，我们使用以这些网格为条件的 CNN 来参数化运动，从而注入强大的隐式正则化并关联附近点的运动。大量的实验表明，我们的方法在渲染质量和三维跟踪精度方面显著优于当前最先进的方法。

TLDR: DRoPS presents a novel approach for dynamic 3D reconstruction of pre-scanned objects from video, using a grid-structured Gaussian primitive model and a CNN-based motion parameterization to improve accuracy and consistency, particularly from novel viewpoints.

简要说明: DRoPS提出了一种新的方法，用于从视频中动态三维重建先前扫描过的物体。该方法使用网格结构的高斯基元模型和基于CNN的运动参数化，从而提高准确性和一致性，尤其是在新视点下。

Authors: Narek Tumanyan, Samuel Rota Bulò, Denis Rozumny, Lorenzo Porzi, Adam Harley, Tali Dekel, Peter Kontschieder, Jonathon Luiten

OpenCap Monocular: 3D Human Kinematics and Musculoskeletal Dynamics from a Single Smartphone Video

Abstract (English): Quantifying human movement (kinematics) and musculoskeletal forces (kinetics) at scale, such as estimating quadriceps force during a sit-to-stand movement, could transform prediction, treatment, and monitoring of mobility-related conditions. However, quantifying kinematics and kinetics traditionally requires costly, time-intensive analysis in specialized laboratories, limiting clinical translation. Scalable, accurate tools for biomechanical assessment are needed. We introduce OpenCap Monocular, an algorithm that estimates 3D skeletal kinematics and kinetics from a single smartphone video. The method refines 3D human pose estimates from a monocular pose estimation model (WHAM) via optimization, computes kinematics of a biomechanically constrained skeletal model, and estimates kinetics via physics-based simulation and machine learning. We validated OpenCap Monocular against marker-based motion capture and force plate data for walking, squatting, and sit-to-stand tasks. OpenCap Monocular achieved low kinematic error (4.8° mean absolute error for rotational degrees of freedom; 3.4 cm for pelvis translations), outperforming a regression-only computer vision baseline by 48% in rotational accuracy (p = 0.036) and 69% in translational accuracy (p < 0.001). OpenCap Monocular also estimated ground reaction forces during walking with accuracy comparable to, or better than, our prior two-camera OpenCap system. We demonstrate that the algorithm estimates important kinetic outcomes with clinically meaningful accuracy in applications related to frailty and knee osteoarthritis, including estimating knee extension moment during sit-to-stand transitions and knee adduction moment during walking. OpenCap Monocular is deployed via a smartphone app, web app, and secure cloud computing (https://opencap.ai), enabling free, accessible single-smartphone biomechanical assessments.

摘要（中文）: 大规模量化人体运动（运动学）和肌肉骨骼力（动力学），例如在站坐动作中估计股四头肌力，可以转变对活动相关疾病的预测、治疗和监测。 然而，量化运动学和动力学通常需要在专业实验室中进行昂贵且耗时的分析，限制了临床转化。因此，需要可扩展且准确的生物力学评估工具。我们介绍了一种名为 OpenCap Monocular 的算法，该算法可以从单个智能手机视频中估计 3D 骨骼运动学和动力学。该方法通过优化，改进了来自单目姿态估计模型 (WHAM) 的 3D 人体姿态估计，计算了生物力学约束骨骼模型的运动学，并通过基于物理的模拟和机器学习估计动力学。我们通过基于标记点的动作捕捉和测力板数据，对行走、下蹲和站坐任务验证了 OpenCap Monocular 的有效性。 OpenCap Monocular 实现了较低的运动学误差（旋转自由度的平均绝对误差为 4.8°；骨盆平移为 3.4 厘米），其旋转精度比仅使用回归的计算机视觉基线提高了 48%（p = 0.036），平移精度提高了 69%（p < 0.001）。 OpenCap Monocular 还能以与我们之前的双相机 OpenCap 系统相当或更好的精度估计行走过程中的地面反作用力。我们证明了该算法在与虚弱和膝骨关节炎相关的应用中，能够以具有临床意义的准确性估计重要的动力学结果，包括估计站坐转换期间的膝关节伸展力矩和行走过程中的膝关节内收力矩。 OpenCap Monocular 通过智能手机应用程序、Web 应用程序和安全的云计算 (https://opencap.ai) 部署，从而实现免费、便捷的单智能手机生物力学评估。

TLDR: This paper introduces OpenCap Monocular, a system that estimates 3D human kinematics and kinetics from a single smartphone video, enabling scalable and accessible biomechanical assessments.

简要说明: 这篇论文介绍了OpenCap Monocular，一个通过单部智能手机视频估算3D人体运动学和动力学的系统，从而实现可拓展且易于获取的生物力学评估。

Authors: Selim Gilon, Emily Y. Miller, Scott D. Uhlrich

Accurate Point Measurement in 3DGS -- A New Alternative to Traditional Stereoscopic-View Based Measurements

Abstract (English): 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has revolutionized real-time rendering with its state-of-the-art novel view synthesis, but its utility for accurate geometric measurement remains underutilized. Compared to multi-view stereo (MVS) point clouds or meshes, 3DGS rendered views present superior visual quality and completeness. However, current point measurement methods still rely on demanding stereoscopic workstations or direct picking on often-incomplete and inaccurate 3D meshes. As a novel view synthesizer, 3DGS renders exact source views and smoothly interpolates in-between views. This allows users to intuitively pick congruent points across different views while operating 3DGS models. By triangulating these congruent points, one can precisely generate 3D point measurements. This approach mimics traditional stereoscopic measurement but is significantly less demanding: it requires neither a stereo workstation nor specialized operator stereoscopic capability. Furthermore, it enables multi-view intersection (more than two views) for higher measurement accuracy. We implemented a web-based application to demonstrate this proof-of-concept (PoC). Using several UAV aerial datasets, we show this PoC allows users to successfully perform highly accurate point measurements, achieving accuracy matching or exceeding traditional stereoscopic methods on standard hardware. Specifically, our approach significantly outperforms direct mesh-based measurements. Quantitatively, our method achieves RMSEs in the 1-2 cm range on well-defined points. More critically, on challenging thin structures where mesh-based RMSE was 0.062 m, our method achieved 0.037 m. On sharp corners poorly reconstructed in the mesh, our method successfully measured all points with a 0.013 m RMSE, whereas the mesh method failed entirely. Code is available at: https://github.com/GDAOSU/3dgs_measurement_tool.

摘要（中文）: 3D高斯溅射(3DGS)以其最先进的新视角合成技术彻底改变了实时渲染，但其在精确几何测量方面的效用仍未得到充分利用。与多视图立体(MVS)点云或网格相比，3DGS渲染的视图呈现出卓越的视觉质量和完整性。然而，目前的点测量方法仍然依赖于要求苛刻的立体工作站或在通常不完整且不准确的3D网格上直接拾取。作为一种新视角合成器，3DGS渲染精确的源视图，并在视图之间平滑插值。这使得用户能够在操作3DGS模型时直观地跨不同视图拾取一致的点。通过三角化这些一致的点，可以精确地生成3D点测量值。该方法模仿了传统的立体测量，但要求显著降低：它既不需要立体工作站，也不需要专门的操作员立体能力。此外，它还支持多视图交汇（超过两个视图）以获得更高的测量精度。我们实现了一个基于Web的应用程序来演示这个概念验证(PoC)。通过使用几个无人机航摄数据集，我们展示了该PoC允许用户成功地执行高精度点测量，在标准硬件上实现与传统立体方法相匹配或超过传统立体方法的精度。具体而言，我们的方法显著优于基于网格的直接测量。在定量方面，我们的方法在定义良好的点上实现了1-2厘米范围内的RMSE。更重要的是，在基于网格的RMSE为0.062米的具有挑战性的薄结构上，我们的方法实现了0.037米的RMSE。在网格中重建不良的尖锐角上，我们的方法成功测量了所有点，RMSE为0.013米，而网格方法完全失败。代码可在以下网址获取：https://github.com/GDAOSU/3dgs_measurement_tool。

TLDR: This paper introduces a new method for accurate 3D point measurement using 3D Gaussian Splatting, outperforming traditional stereoscopic methods and mesh-based measurements, especially on challenging geometries. They provide a web-based tool and validate it on UAV aerial datasets.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种使用3D高斯溅射进行精确3D点测量的新方法，优于传统的立体视觉方法和基于网格的测量，尤其是在具有挑战性的几何形状上。他们提供了一个基于Web的工具，并在无人机航空数据集上进行了验证。

Authors: Deyan Deng, Rongjun Qin

Ultra-fast Traffic Nowcasting and Control via Differentiable Agent-based Simulation

Abstract (English): Traffic digital twins, which inform policymakers of effective interventions based on large-scale, high-fidelity computational models calibrated to real-world traffic, hold promise for addressing societal challenges in our rapidly urbanizing world. However, conventional fine-grained traffic simulations are non-differentiable and typically rely on inefficient gradient-free optimization, making calibration for real-world applications computationally infeasible. Here we present a differentiable agent-based traffic simulator that enables ultra-fast model calibration, traffic nowcasting, and control on large-scale networks. We develop several differentiable computing techniques for simulating individual vehicle movements, including stochastic decision-making and inter-agent interactions, while ensuring that entire simulation trajectories remain end-to-end differentiable for efficient gradient-based optimization. On the large-scale Chicago road network, with over 10,000 calibration parameters, our model simulates more than one million vehicles at 173 times real-time speed. This ultra-fast simulation, together with efficient gradient-based optimization, enables us to complete model calibration using the previous 30 minutes of traffic data in 455 s, provide a one-hour-ahead traffic nowcast in 21 s, and solve the resulting traffic control problem in 728 s. This yields a full calibration--nowcast--control loop in under 20 minutes, leaving about 40 minutes of lead time for implementing interventions. Our work thus provides a practical computational basis for realizing traffic digital twins.

摘要（中文）: 交通数字孪生基于大规模、高保真计算模型，该模型经过对真实世界交通数据的校准，能为政策制定者提供有效的干预措施信息，有望应对快速城市化世界中的社会挑战。然而，传统的细粒度交通仿真不可微，并且通常依赖于低效的无梯度优化，使得真实世界应用的校准在计算上不可行。本文提出了一种可微的基于Agent的交通仿真器，可在大型网络上实现超快速的模型校准、交通现状预测和控制。我们开发了几种可微计算技术来模拟单个车辆的移动，包括随机决策和Agent间的交互，同时确保整个仿真轨迹保持端到端的可微性，从而实现高效的基于梯度的优化。在拥有超过10,000个校准参数的大型芝加哥路网上，我们的模型以173倍的实时速度模拟超过一百万辆车辆。这种超快速的仿真，结合高效的基于梯度的优化，使我们能够在455秒内使用前30分钟的交通数据完成模型校准，在21秒内提供一小时前方的交通现状预测，并在728秒内解决由此产生的交通控制问题。这使得完整的校准-现状预测-控制闭环能在不到20分钟内完成，为干预措施的实施留下大约40分钟的提前时间。因此，我们的工作为实现交通数字孪生提供了一个切实可行的计算基础。

TLDR: The paper presents a differentiable agent-based traffic simulator for ultra-fast traffic nowcasting and control, demonstrating real-time performance on the Chicago road network.

Authors: Fumiyasu Makinoshima, Yuya Yamaguchi, Eigo Segawa, Koichiro Niinuma, Sean Qian

Accurate Surface and Reflectance Modelling from 3D Radar Data with Neural Radiance Fields

Abstract (English): Robust scene representation is essential for autonomous systems to safely operate in challenging low-visibility environments. Radar has a clear advantage over cameras and lidars in these conditions due to its resilience to environmental factors such as fog, smoke, or dust. However, radar data is inherently sparse and noisy, making reliable 3D surface reconstruction challenging. To address these challenges, we propose a neural implicit approach for 3D mapping from radar point clouds, which jointly models scene geometry and view-dependent radar intensities. Our method leverages a memory-efficient hybrid feature encoding to learn a continuous Signed Distance Field (SDF) for surface reconstruction, while also capturing radar-specific reflective properties. We show that our approach produces smoother, more accurate 3D surface reconstructions compared to existing lidar-based reconstruction methods applied to radar data, and can reconstruct view-dependent radar intensities. We also show that in general, as input point clouds get sparser, neural implicit representations render more faithful surfaces, compared to traditional explicit SDFs and meshing techniques.

摘要（中文）: 稳健的场景表示对于自主系统在具有挑战性的低能见度环境中安全运行至关重要。雷达在这种情况下比相机和激光雷达具有明显的优势，因为它能够抵抗雾、烟或灰尘等环境因素的影响。然而，雷达数据本质上是稀疏且嘈杂的，这使得可靠的3D表面重建具有挑战性。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了一种基于神经隐式方法的雷达点云3D建图方法，该方法联合建模了场景几何和视角相关的雷达强度。我们的方法利用一种节省内存的混合特征编码来学习一个连续的符号距离场（SDF）以进行表面重建，同时捕获雷达特有的反射特性。我们表明，与应用于雷达数据的现有基于激光雷达的重建方法相比，我们的方法可以生成更平滑、更准确的3D表面重建，并且可以重建视角相关的雷达强度。我们还表明，一般来说，随着输入点云变得更加稀疏，与传统的显式SDF和网格划分技术相比，神经隐式表示可以呈现更真实的表面。

TLDR: This paper introduces a neural implicit approach for 3D mapping from sparse and noisy radar point clouds, jointly modeling scene geometry and view-dependent radar intensities using a memory-efficient hybrid feature encoding. It demonstrates superior surface reconstruction compared to lidar-based methods when applied to radar data, especially with sparse data.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种神经隐式方法，用于从稀疏且嘈杂的雷达点云中进行3D地图重建，利用内存高效的混合特征编码来联合建模场景几何和视角相关的雷达强度。结果表明，与应用于雷达数据的基于激光雷达的方法相比，该方法能够获得更好的表面重建效果，尤其是在数据稀疏的情况下。

Authors: Judith Treffler, Vladimír Kubelka, Henrik Andreasson, Martin Magnusson

Bayesian Learning-Enhanced Navigation with Deep Smoothing for Inertial-Aided Navigation

Abstract (English): Accurate post-processing navigation is essential for applications such as survey and mapping, where the full measurement history can be exploited to refine past state estimates. Fixed-interval smoothing algorithms represent the theoretically optimal solution under Gaussian assumptions. However, loosely coupled INS/GNSS systems fundamentally inherit the systematic position bias of raw GNSS measurements, leaving a persistent accuracy gap that model-based smoothers cannot resolve. To address this limitation, we propose BLENDS, which integrates Bayesian learning with deep smoothing to enhance navigation performance. BLENDS is a a data-driven post-processing framework that augments the classical two-filter smoother with a transformer-based neural network. It learns to modify the filter covariance matrices and apply an additive correction to the smoothed error-state directly within the Bayesian framework. A novel Bayesian-consistent loss jointly supervises the smoothed mean and covariance, enforcing minimum-variance estimates while maintaining statistical consistency. BLENDS is evaluated on two real-world datasets spanning a mobile robot and a quadrotor. Across all unseen test trajectories, BLENDS achieves horizontal position improvements of up to 63% over the baseline forward EKF.

摘要（中文）: 精确的后处理导航对于诸如测绘等应用至关重要，这些应用可以利用完整的测量历史来优化过去的状态估计。固定区间平滑算法在高斯假设下代表着理论上最优的解决方案。然而，松耦合INS/GNSS系统从根本上继承了原始GNSS测量的系统性位置偏差，从而留下了一个基于模型的平滑器无法解决的持续精度差距。为了解决这一局限性，我们提出了BLENDS，它将贝叶斯学习与深度平滑相结合，以提高导航性能。BLENDS是一个数据驱动的后处理框架，它利用基于Transformer的神经网络来增强传统的双滤波器平滑器。它学习修改滤波器的协方差矩阵，并在贝叶斯框架内直接对平滑误差状态应用附加校正。一种新颖的贝叶斯一致性损失函数联合监督平滑后的均值和协方差，在保持统计一致性的同时，强制执行最小方差估计。BLENDS在横跨移动机器人和四旋翼飞行器的两个真实世界数据集上进行了评估。在所有未见过的测试轨迹上，BLENDS相对于基线前向EKF实现了高达63%的水平位置精度提升。

TLDR: The paper introduces BLENDS, a Bayesian learning-enhanced deep smoothing framework for inertial-aided navigation that uses a transformer-based neural network to improve post-processing accuracy by learning to modify filter covariance matrices and applying corrections to smoothed error states, achieving significant position improvements over a baseline EKF.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了BLENDS，一个基于贝叶斯学习的深度平滑框架，用于惯性辅助导航。它使用基于Transformer的神经网络，通过学习修改滤波器协方差矩阵和对平滑误差状态进行校正来提高后处理精度，相对于基线EKF实现了显著的位置改进。

Authors: Nadav Cohen, Itzik Klein

A Minimum-Energy Control Approach for Redundant Mobile Manipulators in Physical Human-Robot Interaction Applications

Abstract (English): Research on mobile manipulation systems that physically interact with humans has expanded rapidly in recent years, opening the way to tasks which could not be performed using fixed-base manipulators. Within this context, developing suitable control methodologies is essential since mobile manipulators introduce additional degrees of freedom, making the design of control approaches more challenging and more prone to performance optimization. This paper proposes a control approach for a mobile manipulator, composed of a mobile base equipped with a robotic arm mounted on the top, with the objective of minimizing the overall kinetic energy stored in the whole-body mobile manipulator in physical human-robot interaction applications. The approach is experimentally tested with reference to a peg-in-hole task, and the results demonstrate that the proposed approach reduces the overall kinetic energy stored in the whole-body robotic system and improves the system performance compared with the benchmark method.

摘要（中文）: 近年来，对与人类进行物理交互的移动操作系统的研究迅速发展，为使用固定基座机械臂无法完成的任务开辟了道路。在此背景下，开发合适的控制方法至关重要，因为移动操作系统引入了额外的自由度，使得控制方法的设计更具挑战性，也更易于进行性能优化。本文提出了一种用于移动操作机器人的控制方法，该机器人由一个移动底座和一个安装在其顶部的机械臂组成，目的是在物理人机交互应用中最小化整个移动操作机器人中存储的总体动能。该方法通过一个销钉插入孔的任务进行了实验测试，结果表明，与基准方法相比，所提出的方法能够降低整个机器人系统中存储的总体动能，并提高系统性能。

TLDR: This paper presents a control approach for mobile manipulators interacting with humans, aiming to minimize kinetic energy during tasks like peg-in-hole. Experimental results suggest improved system performance compared to benchmark methods.

Authors: Davide Tebaldi, Niccolò Paradisi, Fabio Pini, Luigi Biagiotti

Towards automatic smoke detector inspection: Recognition of the smoke detectors in industrial facilities and preparation for future drone integration

Abstract (English): Fire safety consists of a complex pipeline, and it is a very important topic of concern. One of its frontal parts are the smoke detectors, which are supposed to provide an alarm prior to a massive fire appears. As they are often difficult to reach due to high ceilings or problematic locations, an automatic inspection system would be very beneficial as it could allow faster revisions, prevent workers from dangerous work in heights, and make the whole process cheaper. In this study, we present the smoke detector recognition part of the automatic inspection system, which could easily be integrated to the drone system. As part of our research, we compare two popular convolutional-based object detectors YOLOv11 and SSD widely used on embedded devices together with the state-of-the-art transformer-based RT-DETRv2 with the backbones of different sizes. Due to a complicated way of collecting a sufficient amount of data for training in the real-world environment, we also compare several training strategies using the real and semi-synthetic data together with various augmentation methods. To achieve a robust testing, all models were evaluated on two test datasets with an expected and difficult appearance of the smoke detectors including motion blur, small resolution, or not complete objects. The best performing detector is the YOLOv11n, which reaches the average mAP@0.5 score of 0.884. Our code, pretrained models and dataset are publicly available.

摘要（中文）: 防火安全涉及一个复杂的流程，是一个非常重要的关注议题。其中一个前端环节是烟雾探测器，其作用是在大规模火灾发生前发出警报。由于烟雾探测器通常因高天花板或位置不便而难以触及，因此自动检测系统将非常有利，它可以加快检测速度，避免工人从事危险的高空作业，并降低整个过程的成本。在本研究中，我们提出了自动检测系统中的烟雾探测器识别部分，该部分可以很容易地集成到无人机系统中。作为我们研究的一部分，我们比较了两种流行的基于卷积的对象检测器YOLOv11和SSD，它们广泛应用于嵌入式设备，以及具有不同尺寸骨干网络的最新基于Transformer的RT-DETRv2。由于在真实环境中收集足够的训练数据比较复杂，我们还比较了几种使用真实和半合成数据以及各种增强方法的训练策略。为了实现稳健的测试，所有模型都在两个测试数据集上进行了评估，这些数据集包含烟雾探测器的预期和困难外观，包括运动模糊、小分辨率或不完整对象。表现最佳的检测器是 YOLOv11n，其平均 mAP@0.5 得分为0.884。我们的代码、预训练模型和数据集均已公开。

TLDR: This paper presents an automatic smoke detector recognition system for industrial facilities using YOLOv11, SSD, and RT-DETRv2, comparing different training strategies with real and semi-synthetic data to achieve robust detection under challenging conditions. The best performing detector is YOLOv11n reaching 0.884 mAP@0.5 and the dataset, code and pretrained models are publicly available.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种用于工业设施的自动烟雾探测器识别系统，使用YOLOv11、SSD和RT-DETRv2，比较了使用真实和半合成数据的不同训练策略，以在具有挑战性的条件下实现鲁棒的检测。性能最佳的检测器是YOLOv11n，其mAP@0.5得分为0.884，并且数据集、代码和预训练模型已公开。
