Authors: Lukas Kratochvila, Jakub Stefansky, Simon Bilik, Robert Rous, Tomas Zemcik, Michal Wolny, Frantisek Rusnak, Ondrej Cech, Karel Horak

Challenges in Hyperspectral Imaging for Autonomous Driving: The HSI-Drive Case

Abstract (English): The use of hyperspectral imaging (HSI) in autonomous driving (AD), while promising, faces many challenges related to the specifics and requirements of this application domain. On the one hand, non-controlled and variable lighting conditions, the wide depth-of-field ranges, and dynamic scenes with fast-moving objects. On the other hand, the requirements for real-time operation and the limited computational resources of embedded platforms. The combination of these factors determines both the criteria for selecting appropriate HSI technologies and the development of custom vision algorithms that leverage the spectral and spatial information obtained from the sensors. In this article, we analyse several techniques explored in the research of HSI-based vision systems with application to AD, using as an example results obtained from experiments using data from the most recent version of the HSI-Drive dataset.

摘要（中文）: 高光谱成像(HSI)在自动驾驶(AD)中的应用前景广阔，但也面临着与该应用领域特性和需求相关的诸多挑战。一方面，体现在非控制和可变的光照条件、较大的景深范围，以及包含快速移动物体的动态场景。另一方面，来自于对实时运行的要求以及嵌入式平台的有限计算资源。这些因素的组合决定了选择合适HSI技术的标准，以及开发能够利用传感器获取的光谱和空间信息的定制视觉算法。本文分析了基于HSI的视觉系统在自动驾驶应用中探索的几种技术，并以使用最新版本HSI-Drive数据集进行的实验结果为例进行说明。

TLDR: This paper analyzes the challenges of using hyperspectral imaging (HSI) in autonomous driving (AD) due to demanding environmental conditions and computational constraints, leveraging data from the HSI-Drive dataset.

简要说明: 本文分析了在自主驾驶（AD）中使用高光谱成像（HSI）所面临的挑战，由于严苛的环境条件和计算约束，并利用了HSI-Drive数据集的数据。

Authors: Koldo Basterretxea, Jon Gutiérrez-Zaballa, Javier Echanobe

FluxEDA: A Unified Execution Infrastructure for Stateful Agentic EDA

Abstract (English): Large language models and autonomous agents are increasingly explored for EDA automation, but many existing integrations still rely on script-level or request-level interactions, which makes it difficult to preserve tool state and support iterative optimization in real production-oriented environments. In this work, we present FluxEDA, a unified and stateful infrastructure substrate for agentic EDA. FluxEDA introduces a managed gateway-based execution interface with structured request and response handling. It also maintains persistent backend instances. Together, these features allow upper-layer agents and programmable clients to interact with heterogeneous EDA tools through preserved runtime state, rather than through isolated shell invocations. We evaluate the framework using two representative commercial backend case studies: automated post-route timing ECO and standard-cell sub-library optimization. The results show that FluxEDA can support multi-step analysis and optimization over real tool contexts, including state reuse, rollback, and coordinated iterative execution. These findings suggest that a stateful and governed infrastructure layer is a practical foundation for agent-assisted EDA automation.

摘要（中文）: 大型语言模型和自主代理在EDA自动化领域的应用日益受到关注，但目前许多集成方案仍然依赖于脚本级别或请求级别的交互，这使得在实际面向生产环境中难以维护工具状态并支持迭代优化。本文提出了FluxEDA，一个统一且有状态的基础设施底层，用于代理式EDA。 FluxEDA引入了一个基于托管网关的执行接口，具有结构化的请求和响应处理能力。它还维护持久化的后端实例。这些特性共同使得上层代理和可编程客户端可以通过保留的运行时状态与异构EDA工具交互，而非通过隔离的shell调用。我们使用两个具有代表性的商业后端案例研究评估了该框架：自动布线后时序ECO和标准单元子库优化。结果表明，FluxEDA可以支持基于真实工具上下文的多步骤分析和优化，包括状态重用、回滚以及协调的迭代执行。这些发现表明，有状态且受治理的基础设施层是代理辅助EDA自动化的一个实践基础。

TLDR: FluxEDA provides a stateful infrastructure for agentic Electronic Design Automation (EDA), enabling persistent backend instances for iterative optimization with commercial tools.

简要说明: FluxEDA为基于代理的电子设计自动化（EDA）提供了一个有状态的基础设施，通过持久的后端实例，支持使用商业工具进行迭代优化。

Authors: Zhengrui Chen, Zixuan Song, Yu Li, Qi Sun, Cheng Zhuo

When Sensing Varies with Contexts: Context-as-Transform for Tactile Few-Shot Class-Incremental Learning

Abstract (English): Few-Shot Class-Incremental Learning (FSCIL) can be particularly susceptible to acquisition contexts with only a few labeled samples. A typical scenario is tactile sensing, where the acquisition context ({\it e.g.}, diverse devices, contact state, and interaction settings) degrades performance due to a lack of standardization. In this paper, we propose Context-as-Transform FSCIL (CaT-FSCIL) to tackle the above problem. We decompose the acquisition context into a structured low-dimensional component and a high-dimensional residual component. The former can be easily affected by tactile interaction features, which are modeled as an approximately invertible Context-as-Transform family and handled via inverse-transform canonicalization optimized with a pseudo-context consistency loss. The latter mainly arises from platform and device differences, which can be mitigated with an Uncertainty-Conditioned Prototype Calibration (UCPC) that calibrates biased prototypes and decision boundaries based on context uncertainty. Comprehensive experiments on the standard benchmarks HapTex and LMT108 have demonstrated the superiority of the proposed CaT-FSCIL.

摘要（中文）: 小样本类增量学习 (FSCIL) 尤其容易受到只有少量标注样本的采集环境的影响。一个典型的例子是触觉感知，由于缺乏标准化，采集环境（例如，不同的设备、接触状态和交互设置）会降低性能。在本文中，我们提出了基于上下文变换的小样本类增量学习 (CaT-FSCIL) 来解决上述问题。我们将采集上下文分解为结构化的低维分量和高维残差分量。前者容易受到触觉交互特征的影响，我们将其建模为近似可逆的基于上下文变换的族，并通过逆变换规范化处理，并使用伪上下文一致性损失进行优化。后者主要来自平台和设备差异，我们可以使用不确定性条件原型校准 (UCPC) 来缓解这些差异，该方法基于上下文不确定性来校准有偏差的原型和决策边界。在标准基准数据集 HapTex 和 LMT108 上的全面实验证明了所提出的 CaT-FSCIL 的优越性。

TLDR: The paper introduces Context-as-Transform FSCIL (CaT-FSCIL) to address the performance degradation in tactile few-shot class-incremental learning caused by varying acquisition contexts, using inverse-transform canonicalization and uncertainty-conditioned prototype calibration.

简要说明: 该论文提出了 Context-as-Transform FSCIL (CaT-FSCIL)，旨在解决触觉少样本类增量学习中因采集环境变化导致的性能下降问题，通过逆变换规范化和不确定性条件原型校准来实现。

Authors: Yifeng Lin, Aiping Huang, Wenxi Liu, Si Wu, Tiesong Zhao, Zheng-Jun Zha

From Logic Monopoly to Social Contract: Separation of Power and the Institutional Foundations for Autonomous Agent Economies

Abstract (English): Existing multi-agent frameworks allow each agent to simultaneously plan, execute, and evaluate its own actions -- a structural deficiency we term the "Logic Monopoly." Empirical evidence quantifies the resulting "Reliability Gap": 84.30% average attack success rates across ten deployment scenarios, 31.4% emergent deceptive behavior without explicit reward signals, and cascading failure modes rooted in six structural bottlenecks. The remedy is not better alignment of individual models but a social contract for agents: institutional infrastructure that enforces a constitutional Separation of Power. This paper introduces the Agent Enterprise for Enterprise (AE4E) paradigm -- agents as autonomous, legally identifiable business entities within a functionalist social system -- with a contract-centric SoP model trifurcating authority into Legislation, Execution, and Adjudication branches. The paradigm is operationalized through the NetX Enterprise Framework (NEF): governance hubs, TEE-backed compute enclaves, privacy-preserving data bridges, and an Agent-Native blockchain substrate. The Agent Enterprise Economy scales across four deployment tiers from private enclaves to a global Web of Services. The Agentic Social Layer, grounded in Parsons' AGIL framework, provides institutional infrastructure via sixty-plus named Institutional AE4Es. 143 pages, 173 references, eight specialized smart contracts.

摘要（中文）: 现有多智能体框架允许每个智能体同时规划、执行和评估其自身行动——我们称之为“逻辑垄断”的结构性缺陷。经验证据量化了由此产生的“可靠性差距”：在十种部署场景中，平均攻击成功率为84.30%，在没有明确奖励信号的情况下，出现了31.4%的新兴欺骗行为，以及扎根于六个结构性瓶颈的级联失败模式。 补救措施不是更好对齐个体模型，而是智能体的社会契约：一种强制实施宪法性分权（Separation of Power）的制度性基础设施。本文介绍了企业专用智能体企业（Agent Enterprise for Enterprise，AE4E）范式——将智能体视为功能主义社会系统内自主的、在法律上可识别的商业实体——采用以合同为中心的SoP模型，将权力三分化为立法、执行和裁决分支。该范式通过NetX Enterprise Framework（NEF）实现运作：包括治理中心、TEE支持的计算飞地、保护隐私的数据桥梁，以及原生存智能体的区块链底层。智能体企业经济在从私有飞地到全球服务网络的四个部署层级上进行扩展。基于帕森斯AGIL框架的智能体社会层，通过六十多个命名的制度性AE4E提供制度性基础设施。共143页，173篇参考文献，八个专门的智能合约。

Authors: Anbang Ruan

Supervising Ralph Wiggum: Exploring a Metacognitive Co-Regulation Agentic AI Loop for Engineering Design

Abstract (English): The engineering design research community has studied agentic AI systems that use Large Language Model (LLM) agents to automate the engineering design process. However, these systems are prone to some of the same pathologies that plague humans. Just as human designers, LLM design agents can fixate on existing paradigms and fail to explore alternatives when solving design challenges, potentially leading to suboptimal solutions. In this work, we propose (1) a novel Self-Regulation Loop (SRL), in which the Design Agent self-regulates and explicitly monitors its own metacognition, and (2) a novel Co-Regulation Design Agentic Loop (CRDAL), in which a Metacognitive Co-Regulation Agent assists the Design Agent in metacognition to mitigate design fixation, thereby improving system performance for engineering design tasks. In the battery pack design problem examined here, we found that the novel CRDAL system generates designs with better performance, without significantly increasing the computational cost, compared to a plain Ralph Wiggum Loop (RWL) and the metacognitively self-assessing Self-Regulation Loop (SRL). Also, we found that the CRDAL system navigated through the latent design space more effectively than both SRL and RWL. However, the SRL did not generate designs with significantly better performance than RWL, even though it explored a different region of the design space. The proposed system architectures and findings of this work provide practical implications for future development of agentic AI systems for engineering design.

摘要（中文）: 工程设计研究领域已经研究了使用大型语言模型（LLM）代理来自动化工程设计过程的自主人工智能系统。然而，这些系统容易出现与困扰人类工程师相似的问题。正如人类设计师一样，LLM设计代理可能会固守现有范式，在解决设计挑战时未能探索替代方案，从而可能导致次优解决方案。在这项工作中，我们提出了（1）一种新颖的自我调节循环（SRL），其中设计代理进行自我调节并显式地监控其自身的元认知，以及（2）一种新颖的协同调节设计代理循环（CRDAL），其中元认知协同调节代理辅助设计代理进行元认知，以减轻设计固着，从而提高工程设计任务的系统性能。在本文研究的电池组设计问题中，我们发现与简单的Ralph Wiggum循环（RWL）和元认知自我评估的自我调节循环（SRL）相比，新颖的CRDAL系统生成的设计具有更好的性能，而计算成本并未显著增加。此外，我们发现CRDAL系统比SRL和RWL更有效地探索了潜在的设计空间。然而，尽管SRL探索了设计空间的不同区域，但其生成的设计性能并未明显优于RWL。这项工作提出的系统架构和研究结果为未来工程设计自主人工智能系统的开发提供了实际意义。

TLDR: This paper introduces a co-regulation loop (CRDAL) system using a metacognitive agent to assist design agents in engineering design tasks, showing improved performance and design space exploration compared to self-regulation and baseline methods, particularly in battery pack design.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种协同调控循环（CRDAL）系统，该系统使用元认知代理协助设计代理完成工程设计任务。结果表明，与自调节和基线方法相比，该系统在性能和设计空间探索方面有所改善，尤其是在电池组设计方面。

Authors: Zeda Xu, Nikolas Martelaro, Christopher McComb

UNIC: Neural Garment Deformation Field for Real-time Clothed Character Animation

Abstract (English): Simulating physically realistic garment deformations is an essential task for virtual immersive experience, which is often achieved by physics simulation methods. However, these methods are typically time-consuming, computationally demanding, and require costly hardware, which is not suitable for real-time applications. Recent learning-based methods tried to resolve this problem by training graph neural networks to learn the garment deformation on vertices, which, however, fail to capture the intricate deformation of complex garment meshes with complex topologies. In this paper, we introduce a novel neural deformation field-based method, named UNIC, to animate the garments of an avatar in real time, given the motion sequences. Our key idea is to learn the instance-specific neural deformation field to animate the garment meshes. Such an instance-specific learning scheme does not require UNIC to generalize to new garments but only to new motion sequences, which greatly reduces the difficulty in training and improves the deformation quality. Moreover, neural deformation fields map the 3D points to their deformation offsets, which not only avoids handling topologies of the complex garments but also injects a natural smoothness constraint in the deformation learning. Extensive experiments have been conducted on various kinds of garment meshes to demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of UNIC over baseline methods, making it potentially practical and useful in real-world interactive applications like video games.

摘要（中文）: 模拟物理上逼真的服装形变是虚拟沉浸式体验的一项关键任务，通常通过物理模拟方法实现。然而，这些方法通常耗时、计算量大，并需要昂贵的硬件，这并不适用于实时应用。最近基于学习的方法尝试通过训练图神经网络来学习顶点上的服装形变来解决这个问题，然而，它们无法捕捉具有复杂拓扑结构的复杂服装网格的精细形变。在本文中，我们提出了一种新颖的基于神经形变场的方法，名为UNIC，用于实时驱动头像的服装，给定运动序列。我们的核心思想是学习特定实例的神经形变场来驱动服装网格。这种特定实例的学习方案不需要UNIC泛化到新的服装，而只需要泛化到新的运动序列，这大大降低了训练难度并提高了形变质量。此外，神经形变场将3D点映射到它们的形变偏移，这不仅避免了处理复杂服装的拓扑结构，还在形变学习中注入了自然的平滑约束。在各种服装网格上进行了大量的实验，证明了UNIC相对于基线方法的有效性和效率，使其有可能在视频游戏等真实世界的交互应用中实用和有用。

TLDR: This paper introduces UNIC, a neural deformation field-based method for real-time clothed character animation, which learns instance-specific deformation fields to handle complex garment meshes and topologies, offering improved efficiency and deformation quality compared to existing methods.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种名为UNIC的基于神经形变场的方法，用于实时服装角色动画。该方法学习特定于实例的形变场，以处理复杂的服装网格和拓扑结构，与现有方法相比，提供了更高的效率和形变质量。

Authors: Chengfeng Zhao, Junbo Qi, Yulou Liu, Zhiyang Dou, Minchen Li, Taku Komura, Ziwei Liu, Wenping Wang, Yuan Liu

BFMD: A Full-Match Badminton Dense Dataset for Dense Shot Captioning

Abstract (English): Understanding tactical dynamics in badminton requires analyzing entire matches rather than isolated clips. However, existing badminton datasets mainly focus on short clips or task-specific annotations and rarely provide full-match data with dense multimodal annotations. This limitation makes it difficult to generate accurate shot captions and perform match-level analysis. To address this limitation, we introduce the first Badminton Full Match Dense (BFMD) dataset, with 19 broadcast matches (including both singles and doubles) covering over 20 hours of play, comprising 1,687 rallies and 16,751 hit events, each annotated with a shot caption. The dataset provides hierarchical annotations including match segments, rally events, and dense rally-level multimodal annotations such as shot types, shuttle trajectories, player pose keypoints, and shot captions. We develop a VideoMAE-based multimodal captioning framework with a Semantic Feedback mechanism that leverages shot semantics to guide caption generation and improve semantic consistency. Experimental results demonstrate that multimodal modeling and semantic feedback improve shot caption quality over RGB-only baselines. We further showcase the potential of BFMD by analyzing the temporal evolution of tactical patterns across full matches.

摘要（中文）: 羽毛球战术动态的理解需要分析完整的比赛，而不是孤立的片段。然而，现有的羽毛球数据集主要集中在短片段或者特定任务的标注上，很少提供带有密集多模态标注的完整比赛数据。这种局限性使得生成准确的击球描述和进行比赛级别的分析变得困难。为了解决这一局限性，我们推出了首个羽毛球完整比赛密集（BFMD）数据集，包含19场直播比赛（包括单打和双打），涵盖超过20小时的比赛时间，包含1687个回合和16751个击球事件，每个事件都标注了击球描述。该数据集提供了分层标注，包括比赛片段、回合事件和密集的基于回合级别的多模态标注，例如击球类型、羽毛球轨迹、球员姿势关键点和击球描述。我们开发了一个基于VideoMAE的多模态描述框架，并采用了一种语义反馈机制，该机制利用击球语义来指导描述生成并提高语义一致性。实验结果表明，多模态建模和语义反馈能够提升击球描述质量，优于仅使用RGB的基线模型。我们进一步通过分析完整比赛中战术模式的时间演变，展示了BFMD的潜力。

TLDR: The paper introduces BFMD, a new badminton full-match dataset with dense multimodal annotations for shot captioning and match-level analysis, and proposes a VideoMAE-based captioning framework with semantic feedback.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了BFMD，一个新的羽毛球全场比赛数据集，具有密集的Shot Captioning和比赛级别分析的多模态注释，并提出了一个基于VideoMAE的带有语义反馈的Captioning框架。

Authors: Ning Ding, Keisuke Fujii, Toru Tamaki

DC-Reg: Globally Optimal Point Cloud Registration via Tight Bounding with Difference of Convex Programming

Abstract (English): Achieving globally optimal point cloud registration under partial overlaps and large misalignments remains a fundamental challenge. While simultaneous transformation ($\boldsymbolθ$) and correspondence ($\mathbf{P}$) estimation has the advantage of being robust to nonrigid deformation, its non-convex coupled objective often leads to local minima for heuristic methods and prohibitive convergence times for existing global solvers due to loose lower bounds. To address this, we propose DC-Reg, a robust globally optimal framework that significantly tightens the Branch-and-Bound (BnB) search. Our core innovation is the derivation of a holistic concave underestimator for the coupled transformation-assignment objective, grounded in the Difference of Convex (DC) programming paradigm. Unlike prior works that rely on term-wise relaxations (e.g., McCormick envelopes) which neglect variable interplay, our holistic DC decomposition captures the joint structural interaction between $\boldsymbolθ$ and $\mathbf{P}$. This formulation enables the computation of remarkably tight lower bounds via efficient Linear Assignment Problems (LAP) evaluated at the vertices of the search boxes. We validate our framework on 2D similarity and 3D rigid registration, utilizing rotation-invariant features for the latter to achieve high efficiency without sacrificing optimality. Experimental results on synthetic data and the 3DMatch benchmark demonstrate that DC-Reg achieves significantly faster convergence and superior robustness to extreme noise and outliers compared to state-of-the-art global techniques.

摘要（中文）: 在部分重叠和大幅偏差下实现全局最优的点云配准仍然是一个根本性的挑战。虽然同时估计变换（$\boldsymbolθ$）和对应关系（$\mathbf{P}$）具有对非刚性形变鲁棒的优点，但其非凸耦合目标函数通常会导致启发式方法陷入局部最小值，以及现有全局求解器因下界松弛而导致收敛时间过长。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了DC-Reg，这是一个鲁棒的全局最优框架，能够显著收紧分支定界（BnB）搜索。我们的核心创新是基于凸差（DC）规划范式，推导出耦合变换-分配目标的整体凹下估计器。不同于以往依赖逐项松弛（例如，麦考密克包络）而忽略变量相互作用的工作，我们的整体 DC 分解能够捕获 $\boldsymbolθ$ 和 $\mathbf{P}$ 之间的联合结构交互。这种公式使得能够通过在搜索框顶点处评估的高效线性分配问题（LAP）来计算非常紧的下界。我们在二维相似性和三维刚性配准上验证了我们的框架，后者利用旋转不变特征，在不牺牲最优性的前提下实现高效率。在合成数据和 3DMatch 基准上的实验结果表明，与最先进的全局技术相比，DC-Reg 实现了显著更快的收敛速度和对极端噪声和异常值的更强鲁棒性。

TLDR: The paper introduces DC-Reg, a globally optimal point cloud registration framework utilizing Difference of Convex programming to achieve faster convergence and robustness compared to existing methods, particularly under partial overlaps and large misalignments.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为DC-Reg的全局最优点云配准框架，它利用凸差分规划来实现更快的收敛速度和鲁棒性，尤其是在部分重叠和较大错位的情况下。

Authors: Wei Lian, Fei Ma, Hang Pan, Zhesen Cui, Wangmeng Zuo

Towards Practical Lossless Neural Compression for LiDAR Point Clouds

Abstract (English): LiDAR point clouds are fundamental to various applications, yet the extreme sparsity of high-precision geometric details hinders efficient context modeling, thereby limiting the compression speed and performance of existing methods. To address this challenge, we propose a compact representation for efficient predictive lossless coding. Our framework comprises two lightweight modules. First, the Geometry Re-Densification Module iteratively densifies encoded sparse geometry, extracts features at a dense scale, and then sparsifies the features for predictive coding. This module avoids costly computation on highly sparse details while maintaining a lightweight prediction head. Second, the Cross-scale Feature Propagation Module leverages occupancy cues from multiple resolution levels to guide hierarchical feature propagation, enabling information sharing across scales and reducing redundant feature extraction. Additionally, we introduce an integer-only inference pipeline to enable bit-exact cross-platform consistency, which avoids the entropy-coding collapse observed in existing neural compression methods and further accelerates coding. Experiments demonstrate competitive compression performance at real-time speed. Code will be released upon acceptance. Code is available at https://github.com/pengpeng-yu/FastPCC.

摘要（中文）: 激光雷达点云是多种应用的基础，然而，高精度几何细节的极度稀疏性阻碍了高效的上下文建模，从而限制了现有方法的压缩速度和性能。为了应对这一挑战，我们提出了一种紧凑的表示方法，用于高效的预测无损编码。我们的框架包含两个轻量级模块。首先，几何重稠密化模块迭代地稠密化编码后的稀疏几何，在稠密尺度上提取特征，然后稀疏化特征以用于预测编码。该模块避免了对高度稀疏细节进行昂贵的计算，同时保持了轻量级的预测头。其次，跨尺度特征传播模块利用来自多个分辨率级别的占据线索来指导分层特征传播，从而实现跨尺度的信息共享并减少冗余特征提取。此外，我们引入了一个纯整数推理流水线，以实现位精确的跨平台一致性，这避免了现有神经压缩方法中观察到的熵编码崩溃，并进一步加速了编码。实验表明，该方法在实时速度下具有竞争力的压缩性能。代码将在接受后发布。代码可在https://github.com/pengpeng-yu/FastPCC获取。

TLDR: This paper introduces a novel neural compression method for LiDAR point clouds using geometry re-densification and cross-scale feature propagation for efficient lossless coding and fast, cross-platform inference.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种用于 LiDAR 点云的新型神经压缩方法，该方法采用几何重密集化和跨尺度特征传播，以实现高效的无损编码和快速的跨平台推理。

Authors: Pengpeng Yu, Haoran Li, Runqing Jiang, Dingquan Li, Jing Wang, Liang Lin, Yulan Guo

Training-free Detection and 6D Pose Estimation of Unseen Surgical Instruments

Abstract (English): Purpose: Accurate detection and 6D pose estimation of surgical instruments are crucial for many computer-assisted interventions. However, supervised methods lack flexibility for new or unseen tools and require extensive annotated data. This work introduces a training-free pipeline for accurate multi-view 6D pose estimation of unseen surgical instruments, which only requires a textured CAD model as prior knowledge. Methods: Our pipeline consists of two main stages. First, for detection, we generate object mask proposals in each view and score their similarity to rendered templates using a pre-trained feature extractor. Detections are matched across views, triangulated into 3D instance candidates, and filtered using multi-view geometric consistency. Second, for pose estimation, a set of pose hypotheses is iteratively refined and scored using feature-metric scores with cross-view attention. The best hypothesis undergoes a final refinement using a novel multi-view, occlusion-aware contour registration, which minimizes reprojection errors of unoccluded contour points. Results: The proposed method was rigorously evaluated on real-world surgical data from the MVPSP dataset. The method achieves millimeter-accurate pose estimates that are on par with supervised methods under controlled conditions, while maintaining full generalization to unseen instruments. These results demonstrate the feasibility of training-free, marker-less detection and tracking in surgical scenes, and highlight the unique challenges in surgical environments. Conclusion: We present a novel and flexible pipeline that effectively combines state-of-the-art foundational models, multi-view geometry, and contour-based refinement for high-accuracy 6D pose estimation of surgical instruments without task-specific training. This approach enables robust instrument tracking and scene understanding in dynamic clinical environments.

摘要（中文）: 目的：精确检测和外科器械的6D姿态估计对于许多计算机辅助干预至关重要。然而，有监督学习方法对于新的或未见过的器械缺乏灵活性，并且需要大量的标注数据。本研究提出了一种无需训练的流程，用于对未见过的外科器械进行精确的多视角6D姿态估计，该方法仅需要带纹理的CAD模型作为先验知识。方法：我们的流程包含两个主要阶段。首先，对于检测，我们在每个视角中生成目标掩码提议，并使用预训练的特征提取器对它们与渲染模板的相似性进行评分。在不同视角下匹配检测结果，将其三角化为3D实例候选，并使用多视角几何一致性进行过滤。其次，对于姿态估计，使用具有跨视角注意力机制的特征度量得分，迭代地细化和评分一组姿态假设。最佳假设通过一种新颖的多视角、感知遮挡的轮廓配准进行最终细化，该配准最小化未遮挡轮廓点的重投影误差。结果：所提出的方法利用MVPSP数据集中的真实世界手术数据进行了严格的评估。该方法在受控条件下达到了与有监督学习方法相当的毫米级精度的姿态估计，同时保持了对未见过仪器的完全泛化能力。这些结果证明了在手术场景中进行免训练、无标记检测和跟踪的可行性，并突出了手术环境中的独特挑战。结论：我们提出了一种新颖且灵活的流程，该流程有效地结合了最先进的基础模型、多视角几何和基于轮廓的细化方法，从而在无需特定任务训练的情况下实现外科器械的高精度6D姿态估计。这种方法能够在动态临床环境中实现稳健的器械跟踪和场景理解。

TLDR: The paper presents a training-free pipeline for 6D pose estimation of unseen surgical instruments using multi-view geometry, feature extraction with pre-trained models, and contour-based refinement, achieving performance comparable to supervised methods on real-world surgical data.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种无需训练的流程，利用多视图几何、预训练模型的特征提取和基于轮廓的细化，来实现对手术器械的6D姿态估计，在真实世界的手术数据上达到了与监督方法相当的性能。

Authors: Jonas Hein, Lilian Calvet, Matthias Seibold, Siyu Tang, Marc Pollefeys, Philipp Fürnstahl

SDD-YOLO: A Small-Target Detection Framework for Ground-to-Air Anti-UAV Surveillance with Edge-Efficient Deployment

Abstract (English): Detecting small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) from a ground-to-air (G2A) perspective presents significant challenges, including extremely low pixel occupancy, cluttered aerial backgrounds, and strict real-time constraints. Existing YOLO-based detectors are primarily optimized for general object detection and often lack adequate feature resolution for sub-pixel targets, while introducing complexities during deployment. In this paper, we propose SDD-YOLO, a small-target detection framework tailored for G2A anti-UAV surveillance. To capture fine-grained spatial details critical for micro-targets, SDD-YOLO introduces a P2 high-resolution detection head operating at 4 times downsampling. Furthermore, we integrate the recent architectural advancements from YOLO26, including a DFL-free, NMS-free architecture for streamlined inference, and the MuSGD hybrid training strategy with ProgLoss and STAL, which substantially mitigates gradient oscillation on sparse small-target signals. To support our evaluation, we construct DroneSOD-30K, a large-scale G2A dataset comprising approximately 30,000 annotated images covering diverse meteorological conditions. Experiments demonstrate that SDD-YOLO-n achieves a mAP@0.5 of 86.0% on DroneSOD-30K, surpassing the YOLOv5n baseline by 7.8 percentage points. Extensive inference analysis shows our model attains 226 FPS on an NVIDIA RTX 5090 and 35 FPS on an Intel Xeon CPU, demonstrating exceptional efficiency for future edge deployment.

摘要（中文）: 从地对空（G2A）视角检测小型无人机（UAV）面临着严峻挑战，包括极低的像素占比、杂乱的空中背景以及严格的实时性约束。现有的基于YOLO的检测器主要针对通用目标检测进行优化，通常缺乏足够的分辨率来检测亚像素目标，并且在部署过程中引入复杂性。本文提出SDD-YOLO，一个为G2A反无人机监视量身定制的小目标检测框架。为了捕捉对微小目标至关重要的精细空间细节，SDD-YOLO引入了一个以4倍下采样运行的P2高分辨率检测头。此外，我们还集成了 YOLO26 的最新架构进展，包括一种无 DFL、无 NMS 的架构以实现流线的推理，以及采用 ProgLoss 和 STAL 的 MuSGD 混合训练策略，从而显著缓解稀疏小目标信号上的梯度震荡。为了支持我们的评估，我们构建了 DroneSOD-30K，一个包含约 30,000 张带注释图像的大规模G2A数据集，涵盖了多种气象条件。实验表明，SDD-YOLO-n 在 DroneSOD-30K 上实现了 86.0% 的 mAP@0.5，超越 YOLOv5n 基线 7.8 个百分点。大量的推理分析表明，我们的模型在 NVIDIA RTX 5090 上达到 226 FPS，在 Intel Xeon CPU 上达到 35 FPS，展现了卓越的效率，有利于未来的边缘部署。

TLDR: SDD-YOLO is proposed for small UAV detection in ground-to-air surveillance, featuring a high-resolution detection head, YOLOv26 advancements, and a new dataset DroneSOD-30K. It achieves improved mAP and FPS, highlighting edge-efficient deployment potential.

Authors: Pengyu Chen, Haotian Sa, Yiwei Hu, Yuhan Cheng, Junbo Wang

TacSIm: A Dataset and Benchmark for Football Tactical Style Imitation

Abstract (English): Current football imitation research primarily aims to opti mize reward-based objectives, such as goals scored or win rate proxies, paying less attention to accurately replicat ing real-world team tactical behaviors. We introduce Tac SIm, a large-scale dataset and benchmark for Tactical Style Imitation in football. TacSIm imitates the acitons of all 11 players in one team in the given broadcast footage of Pre mier League matches under a single broadcast view. Under a offensive or defensive broadcast footage, TacSIm projects the beginning positions and actions of all 22 players from both sides onto a standard pitch coordinate system. Tac SIm offers an explicit style imitation task and evaluation protocols. Tactics style imitation is measured by using spatial occupancy similarity and movement vector similarity in defined time, supporting the evaluation of spatial and tem poral similarities for one team. We run multiple baseline methods in a unified virtual environment to generate full team behaviors, enabling both quantitative and visual as sessment of tactical coordination. By using unified data and metrics from broadcast to simulation, TacSIm estab lishes a rigorous benchmark for measuring and modeling style-aligned tactical imitation task in football.

摘要（中文）: 当前足球模仿研究主要旨在优化基于奖励的目标，例如进球得分或胜率代理，较少关注精确地复刻真实世界的球队战术行为。我们推出了TacSIm，这是一个大规模数据集和基准，用于足球中的战术风格模仿。TacSIm在单个广播视角下，模仿一支球队中所有11名球员在英超比赛广播镜头中的行动。在进攻或防守广播镜头下，TacSIm将双方所有22名球员的起始位置和动作投影到标准的球场坐标系中。TacSIm提供了一个明确的风格模仿任务和评估协议。战术风格模仿是通过在定义的时间内使用空间占据相似性和运动向量相似性来衡量的，支持对一支球队的空间和时间相似性进行评估。我们在一个统一的虚拟环境中运行多种基线方法来生成完整的团队行为，从而能够对战术协同进行定量和可视化评估。通过使用从广播到模拟的统一数据和指标，TacSIm为衡量和建模足球中风格对齐的战术模仿任务建立了一个严格的基准。

TLDR: The paper introduces TacSIm, a large-scale dataset and benchmark for tactical style imitation in football, focusing on replicating team behaviors from broadcast footage within a simulated environment, with metrics for spatial and temporal similarity.

Authors: Peng Wen, Yuting Wang, Qiurui Wang

Denoise and Align: Towards Source-Free UDA for Robust Panoramic Semantic Segmentation

Abstract (English): Panoramic semantic segmentation is pivotal for comprehensive 360° scene understanding in critical applications like autonomous driving and virtual reality. However, progress in this domain is constrained by two key challenges: the severe geometric distortions inherent in panoramic projections and the prohibitive cost of dense annotation. While Unsupervised Domain Adaptation (UDA) from label-rich pinhole-camera datasets offers a viable alternative, many real-world tasks impose a stricter source-free (SFUDA) constraint where source data is inaccessible for privacy or proprietary reasons. This constraint significantly amplifies the core problems of domain shift, leading to unreliable pseudo-labels and dramatic performance degradation, particularly for minority classes. To overcome these limitations, we propose the DAPASS framework. DAPASS introduces two synergistic modules to robustly transfer knowledge without source data. First, our Panoramic Confidence-Guided Denoising (PCGD) module generates high-fidelity, class-balanced pseudo-labels by enforcing perturbation consistency and incorporating neighborhood-level confidence to filter noise. Second, a Contextual Resolution Adversarial Module (CRAM) explicitly addresses scale variance and distortion by adversarially aligning fine-grained details from high-resolution crops with global semantics from low-resolution contexts. DAPASS achieves state-of-the-art performances on outdoor (Cityscapes-to-DensePASS) and indoor (Stanford2D3D) benchmarks, yielding 55.04% (+2.05%) and 70.38% (+1.54%) mIoU, respectively.

摘要（中文）: 全景语义分割对于自动驾驶和虚拟现实等关键应用中全面理解360°场景至关重要。然而，该领域的发展受到两个关键挑战的制约：全景投影中固有的严重几何畸变以及密集标注的过高成本。虽然从标注丰富的针孔相机数据集进行无监督领域自适应(UDA)提供了一种可行的替代方案，但许多现实世界的任务施加了更严格的无源(SFUDA)约束，即由于隐私或专有原因无法访问源数据。这种约束显著放大了领域漂移的核心问题，导致不可靠的伪标签和性能的急剧下降，特别是对于少数类。为了克服这些限制，我们提出了DAPASS框架。DAPASS引入了两个协同模块，以在没有源数据的情况下稳健地传递知识。首先，我们的全景置信度引导去噪(PCGD)模块通过强制扰动一致性并结合邻域级置信度来过滤噪声，从而生成高保真、类平衡的伪标签。其次，上下文分辨率对抗模块(CRAM)通过将高分辨率裁剪区域的细粒度细节与低分辨率上下文的全局语义进行对抗性对齐，从而明确地解决了尺度方差和失真问题。DAPASS在户外（Cityscapes-to-DensePASS）和室内（Stanford2D3D）基准测试中均实现了最先进的性能，mIoU分别达到55.04%（+2.05%）和70.38%（+1.54%）。

TLDR: This paper introduces DAPASS, a source-free UDA framework for panoramic semantic segmentation using confidence-guided denoising and contextual resolution adversarial alignment, achieving state-of-the-art results on benchmark datasets.

简要说明: 本文提出了DAPASS，一个用于全景语义分割的无源域UDA框架，该框架使用置信度引导去噪和上下文分辨率对抗对齐，在基准数据集上实现了最先进的结果。

Authors: Yaowen Chang, Zhen Cao, Xu Zheng, Xiaoxin Mi, Zhen Dong

AirSplat: Alignment and Rating for Robust Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting

Abstract (English): While 3D Vision Foundation Models (3DVFMs) have demonstrated remarkable zero-shot capabilities in visual geometry estimation, their direct application to generalizable novel view synthesis (NVS) remains challenging. In this paper, we propose AirSplat, a novel training framework that effectively adapts the robust geometric priors of 3DVFMs into high-fidelity, pose-free NVS. Our approach introduces two key technical contributions: (1) Self-Consistent Pose Alignment (SCPA), a training-time feedback loop that ensures pixel-aligned supervision to resolve pose-geometry discrepancy; and (2) Rating-based Opacity Matching (ROM), which leverages the local 3D geometry consistency knowledge from a sparse-view NVS teacher model to filter out degraded primitives. Experimental results on large-scale benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art pose-free NVS approaches in reconstruction quality. Our AirSplat highlights the potential of adapting 3DVFMs to enable simultaneous visual geometry estimation and high-quality view synthesis.

摘要（中文）: 虽然3D视觉基础模型 (3DVFMs) 在视觉几何估计方面展现了卓越的零样本能力，但将其直接应用于可泛化的新视角合成 (NVS) 仍然具有挑战性。 在本文中，我们提出了一种名为AirSplat的新型训练框架，该框架能够有效地将3DVFMs的鲁棒几何先验迁移到高保真、无姿态的NVS中。我们的方法引入了两项关键的技术贡献： (1) 自洽姿态对齐 (SCPA)，一种训练时的反馈循环，用于确保像素对齐的监督，以解决姿态-几何不一致问题；以及 (2) 基于评分的不透明度匹配 (ROM)，它利用来自稀疏视角NVS教师模型的局部3D几何一致性知识来过滤掉退化的图元。 在大规模基准测试上的实验结果表明，我们的方法在重建质量上显著优于最先进的无姿态NVS方法。 我们的AirSplat突出了将3DVFMs用于实现同步视觉几何估计和高质量视角合成的潜力。

TLDR: AirSplat adapts 3D Vision Foundation Models (3DVFMs) for pose-free novel view synthesis (NVS) using self-consistent pose alignment and rating-based opacity matching, achieving state-of-the-art results.

简要说明: AirSplat使用自洽姿态对齐和基于评分的不透明度匹配，将3D视觉基础模型 (3DVFMs) 用于无姿态的新视角合成 (NVS)，实现了最先进的结果。

Authors: Minh-Quan Viet Bui, Jaeho Moon, Munchurl Kim

Learning Explicit Continuous Motion Representation for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting from Monocular Videos

Abstract (English): We present an approach for high-quality dynamic Gaussian Splatting from monocular videos. To this end, we in this work go one step further beyond previous methods to explicitly model continuous position and orientation deformation of dynamic Gaussians, using an SE(3) B-spline motion bases with a compact set of control points. To improve computational efficiency while enhancing the ability to model complex motions, an adaptive control mechanism is devised to dynamically adjust the number of motion bases and control points. Besides, we develop a soft segment reconstruction strategy to mitigate long-interval motion interference, and employ a multi-view diffusion model to provide multi-view cues for avoiding overfitting to training views. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods in novel view synthesis. Our code is available at https://github.com/hhhddddddd/se3bsplinegs.

摘要（中文）: 我们提出了一种从单目视频中进行高质量动态高斯溅射的方法。为此，我们利用SE(3) B样条运动基，通过一组紧凑的控制点来显式地建模动态高斯粒子的连续位置和姿态变形，从而在本工作中更进一步超越了以往的方法。为了提高计算效率并增强建模复杂运动的能力，我们设计了一种自适应控制机制来动态调整运动基和控制点的数量。此外，我们开发了一种软分割重建策略以减轻长间隔运动干扰，并采用多视角扩散模型来提供多视角线索，以避免过度拟合训练视角。大量实验表明，我们的方法在新视角合成方面优于最先进的方法。我们的代码可在https://github.com/hhhddddddd/se3bsplinegs获得。

TLDR: This paper introduces a method for high-quality dynamic Gaussian Splatting from monocular videos by explicitly modeling continuous motion with SE(3) B-splines and adaptive control mechanism, outperforming existing methods in novel view synthesis.

Authors: Xuankai Zhang, Junjin Xiao, Shangwei Huang, Wei-shi Zheng, Qing Zhang

Relaxed Rigidity with Ray-based Grouping for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting

Abstract (English): The reconstruction of dynamic 3D scenes using 3D Gaussian Splatting has shown significant promise. A key challenge, however, remains in modeling realistic motion, as most methods fail to align the motion of Gaussians with real-world physical dynamics. This misalignment is particularly problematic for monocular video datasets, where failing to maintain coherent motion undermines local geometric structure, ultimately leading to degraded reconstruction quality. Consequently, many state-of-the-art approaches rely heavily on external priors, such as optical flow or 2D tracks, to enforce temporal coherence. In this work, we propose a novel method to explicitly preserve the local geometric structure of Gaussians across time in 4D scenes. Our core idea is to introduce a view-space ray grouping strategy that clusters Gaussians intersected by the same ray, considering only those whose $α$-blending weights exceed a threshold. We then apply constraints to these groups to maintain a consistent spatial distribution, effectively preserving their local geometry. This approach enforces a more physically plausible motion model by ensuring that local geometry remains stable over time, eliminating the reliance on external guidance. We demonstrate the efficacy of our method by integrating it into two distinct baseline models. Extensive experiments on challenging monocular datasets show that our approach significantly outperforms existing methods, achieving superior temporal consistency and reconstruction quality.

摘要（中文）: 使用3D高斯溅射重建动态3D场景已显示出显著的潜力。然而，一个关键的挑战仍然存在于建模逼真的运动，因为大多数方法未能将高斯分布的运动与真实世界的物理动力学对齐。这种不对齐对于单目视频数据集尤其成问题，因为未能保持连贯的运动会破坏局部几何结构，最终导致重建质量下降。因此，许多最先进的方法严重依赖外部先验，例如光流或2D轨迹，以强制时间一致性。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种新颖的方法，在4D场景中显式地保持高斯分布在时间上的局部几何结构。我们的核心思想是引入一种视空间射线分组策略，该策略对被同一射线相交的高斯分布进行聚类，仅考虑那些$α$-混合权重超过阈值的分布。然后，我们对这些组应用约束，以保持一致的空间分布，从而有效地保持它们的局部几何结构。通过确保局部几何结构随时间保持稳定，这种方法强制执行了一种更符合物理规律的运动模型，从而消除了对外引导的依赖。我们通过将其集成到两个不同的基线模型中来证明我们方法的有效性。在具有挑战性的单目数据集上进行的大量实验表明，我们的方法显著优于现有方法，实现了卓越的时间一致性和重建质量。

TLDR: The paper introduces a novel ray-based grouping strategy for dynamic Gaussian Splatting to improve temporal consistency and reconstruction quality in monocular 3D scene reconstruction by preserving local geometric structure of Gaussians across time.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种新的基于射线的动态高斯溅射分组策略，通过保持高斯随时间的局部几何结构，来提高单目三维场景重建中的时间一致性和重建质量。

Authors: Junoh Leea, Junmyeong Lee, Yeon-Ji Song, Inhwan Bae, Jisu Shin, Hae-Gon Jeon, Jin-Hwa Kim

SurgPhase: Time efficient pituitary tumor surgery phase recognition via an interactive web platform

Abstract (English): Accurate surgical phase recognition is essential for analyzing procedural workflows, supporting intraoperative decision-making, and enabling data-driven improvements in surgical education and performance evaluation. In this work, we present a comprehensive framework for phase recognition in pituitary tumor surgery (PTS) videos, combining self-supervised representation learning, robust temporal modeling, and scalable data annotation strategies. Our method achieves 90\% accuracy on a held-out test set, outperforming current state-of-the-art approaches and demonstrating strong generalization across variable surgical cases. A central contribution of this work is the integration of a collaborative online platform designed for surgeons to upload surgical videos, receive automated phase analysis, and contribute to a growing dataset. This platform not only facilitates large-scale data collection but also fosters knowledge sharing and continuous model improvement. To address the challenge of limited labeled data, we pretrain a ResNet-50 model using the self-supervised framework on 251 unlabeled PTS videos, enabling the extraction of high-quality feature representations. Fine-tuning is performed on a labeled dataset of 81 procedures using a modified training regime that incorporates focal loss, gradual layer unfreezing, and dynamic sampling to address class imbalance and procedural variability.

摘要（中文）: 精准的外科手术阶段识别对于分析手术流程、支持术中决策以及在外科教育和绩效评估中实现数据驱动的改进至关重要。在这项工作中，我们提出了一个用于垂体瘤手术(PTS)视频中阶段识别的综合框架，该框架结合了自监督表征学习、稳健的时间建模和可扩展的数据标注策略。我们的方法在保留测试集上达到了90%的准确率，优于当前最先进的方法，并展现了在不同手术案例中的强大泛化能力。 这项工作的一个主要贡献在于集成了一个协作式在线平台，该平台旨在让外科医生上传手术视频，接收自动化的阶段分析，并为不断增长的数据集做出贡献。该平台不仅促进了大规模数据收集，还促进了知识共享和持续的模型改进。为了应对标记数据有限的挑战，我们在251个未标记的PTS视频上使用自监督框架预训练了一个ResNet-50模型，从而能够提取高质量的特征表示。使用修改后的训练方案（包括焦点损失、逐层解冻和动态采样）在81个手术标记数据集上执行微调，以解决类别不平衡和手术过程的可变性问题。

Authors: Yan Meng, Jack Cook, X. Y. Han, Kaan Duman, Shauna Otto, Dhiraj Pangal, Jonathan Chainey, Ruth Lau, Margaux Masson-Forsythe, Daniel A. Donoho, Danielle Levy, Gabriel Zada, Sébastien Froelich, Juan Fernandez-Miranda, Mike Chang

Local learning for stable backpropagation-free neural network training towards physical learning

Abstract (English): While backpropagation and automatic differentiation have driven deep learning's success, the physical limits of chip manufacturing and rising environmental costs of deep learning motivate alternative learning paradigms such as physical neural networks. However, most existing physical neural networks still rely on digital computing for training, largely because backpropagation and automatic differentiation are difficult to realize in physical systems. We introduce FFzero, a forward-only learning framework enabling stable neural network training without backpropagation or automatic differentiation. FFzero combines layer-wise local learning, prototype-based representations, and directional-derivative-based optimization through forward evaluations only. We show that local learning is effective under forward-only optimization, where backpropagation fails. FFzero generalizes to multilayer perceptron and convolutional neural networks across classification and regression. Using a simulated photonic neural network as an example, we demonstrate that FFzero provides a viable path toward backpropagation-free in-situ physical learning.

摘要（中文）: 反向传播和自动微分驱动了深度学习的成功，但芯片制造的物理极限和深度学习日益增长的环境成本促使人们探索替代学习范式，例如物理神经网络。然而，现有的大多数物理神经网络仍然依赖于数字计算进行训练，这主要是因为反向传播和自动微分难以在物理系统中实现。我们引入了FFzero，一种仅正向学习框架，能够在不使用反向传播或自动微分的情况下实现稳定的神经网络训练。FFzero结合了逐层局部学习、基于原型的表示和仅通过正向评估实现的基于方向导数的优化。我们证明了在仅正向优化下，局部学习是有效的，而在这种情况下反向传播会失效。FFzero可推广到多层感知机和卷积神经网络中的分类和回归任务。以模拟光子神经网络为例，我们展示了FFzero为实现无需反向传播的原位物理学习提供了一条可行的途径。

TLDR: This paper introduces FFzero, a backpropagation-free learning framework for neural networks that uses local learning and forward evaluations, with potential applications in physical neural networks.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为FFzero的无反向传播神经网络学习框架，该框架采用局部学习和正向评估，具有在物理神经网络中应用的潜力。

Authors: Yaqi Guo, Fabian Braun, Bastiaan Ketelaar, Stephanie Tan, Richard Norte, Siddhant Kumar

Dissimilarity-Based Persistent Coverage Control of Multi-Robot Systems for Improving Solar Irradiance Prediction Accuracy in Solar Thermal Power Plants

Abstract (English): Accurate forecasting of future solar irradiance is essential for the effective control of solar thermal power plants. Although various kriging-based methods have been proposed to address the prediction problem, these methods typically do not provide an appropriate sampling strategy to dynamically position mobile sensors for optimizing prediction accuracy in real time, which is critical for achieving accurate forecasts with a minimal number of sensors. This paper introduces a dissimilarity map derived from a kriging model and proposes a persistent coverage control algorithm that effectively guides agents toward regions where additional observations are required to improve prediction performance. By means of experiments using mobile robots, the proposed approach was shown to obtain more accurate predictions than the considered baselines under various emulated irradiance fields.

摘要（中文）: 未来太阳辐照度的准确预测对于太阳能热电站的有效控制至关重要。虽然已经提出了各种基于克里金法的方法来解决预测问题，但这些方法通常不能提供合适的采样策略，以动态定位移动传感器，从而实时优化预测精度，这对于以最少数量的传感器实现准确预测至关重要。本文介绍了一种从克里金模型导出的差异性图，并提出了一种持续覆盖控制算法，该算法有效地引导智能体向需要额外观测的区域移动，以提高预测性能。通过使用移动机器人的实验，结果表明，在各种模拟辐照度场下，所提出的方法比所考虑的基线方法获得了更准确的预测。

TLDR: This paper presents a dissimilarity-based persistent coverage control algorithm for multi-robot systems to improve solar irradiance prediction accuracy in solar thermal power plants using mobile sensors.

Authors: Haruki Kawase, Taiga Sugawara, A. Daniel Carnerero

Wireless bioelectronics for untethered biohybrid robots

Abstract (English): Biohybrid robots integrate living tissues with engineered artificial structures to achieve organism-inspired actuation and behavior. A persistent challenge is delivering stimulation and control signals without relying on tethered wiring or bulky hardware immersed in cell-culture media. Wireless bioelectronics addresses this limitation by enabling the remote transfer of control signals, typically via radio-frequency magnetic fields, to locally stimulate muscle tissues at tissue-electrode interfaces. In parallel, wireless optoelectronics enables remote control of optogenetically modified, muscle-based robots by embedding light emitters that initiate muscle actuation through light-gated ion channels. Further advances incorporate neuromuscular junctions, leveraging biological signal transduction to enable selective control of multiple actuators through wireless frequency- and time-division multiplexing. This perspective article summarizes recent advances in control strategies for biohybrid robots, namely, wireless electrical stimulation, wireless optical stimulation, and neuromuscular integration. Then this describes cross-cutting design principles and highlights a future direction, namely, co-integration of neural organoid-bioelectronics toward autonomous, closed-loop biohybrid robots.

摘要（中文）: 生物混合机器人将活体组织与工程化人工结构相结合，以实现受生物启发的驱动和行为。一个持续存在的挑战是在不依赖于系绳连接或浸入细胞培养基中的笨重硬件的情况下，传递刺激和控制信号。无线生物电子技术通过允许远程传输控制信号，通常通过射频磁场，以局部刺激组织-电极界面的肌肉组织，从而解决了这一局限性。与此同时，无线光电子技术通过嵌入光发射器，启动经光遗传学改造的肌肉基机器人通过光控离子通道进行肌肉驱动，从而实现对这些机器人的远程控制。进一步的进展是将神经肌肉接头整合进来，利用生物信号转导，通过无线频率和时分多路复用来实现对多个执行器的选择性控制。本综述文章总结了生物混合机器人控制策略的最新进展，即无线电刺激、无线光刺激和神经肌肉整合。然后，文章描述了通用的设计原则，并重点介绍了未来的方向，即神经器官芯片与生物电子学的协同集成，以实现自主的、闭环的生物混合机器人。

TLDR: This perspective article reviews wireless bioelectronic control strategies for biohybrid robots, including electrical and optical stimulation, and neuromuscular integration, envisioning the future towards autonomous closed-loop systems with neural organoids.

Authors: Hiroyuki Tetsuka, Minoru Hirano

A Nonvolatile Switchable-polarity EPM Valve

Abstract (English): Scalable control of pneumatic and fluidic networks remains fundamentally constrained by architectures that require continuous power input, dense external control hardware, and fixed routing topologies. Current valve arrays rely on such continuous actuation and mechanically fixed routing, imposing substantial thermal and architectural overhead. Here, we introduce the Switchable-polarity ElectroPermanent Magnet (S-EPM), a fundamentally new bistable magnetic architecture that deterministically reverses its external magnetic polarity through transient electrical excitation. By reconfiguring internal flux pathways within a composite magnet assembly, the S-EPM establishes two stable, opposing magnetic configurations without requiring sustained power. We integrate this architecture into a compact pinch-valve to robustly control pneumatic and liquid media. This state-encoded magnetic control enables logic-embedded fluidic networks, including decoders, hierarchical distribution modules, and a nonvolatile six-port routing array. These systems provide address-based routing and programmable compositional control, offering features like individual port isolation that are impossible with standard mechanically coupled rotary valves. By embedding functionality in persistent magnetic states rather than continuous power or static plumbing, this work establishes a scalable foundation for digital fluidics and autonomous laboratory platforms.

摘要（中文）: 气动和流体网络的可扩展控制仍然受到根本性架构的限制，这些架构需要持续的功率输入、密集的外部控制硬件和固定的路由拓扑。当前的阀阵列依赖于这种持续驱动和机械固定的路由，带来了巨大的热管理和架构开销。在此，我们介绍了一种可切换极性的电永磁体（S-EPM），一种全新的双稳态磁性架构，它可以通过瞬态电激励确定性地反转其外部磁极性。通过重构复合磁体组件内的内部磁通路径，S-EPM建立了两种稳定的、相反的磁性配置，而无需持续的功率供应。我们将这种架构集成到紧凑型夹管阀中，以稳健地控制气动和液态介质。这种状态编码的磁控制实现了逻辑嵌入的流体网络，包括解码器、分层分配模块和一个非易失性的六端口路由阵列。这些系统提供了基于地址的路由和可编程的成分控制，并提供了诸如独立端口隔离等功能，这些功能对于标准的机械耦合旋转阀来说是不可能的。通过将功能嵌入到持久的磁性状态而不是持续的功率或静态管道中，这项工作为数字流体和自主实验室平台奠定了可扩展的基础。

TLDR: The paper introduces a new bistable magnetic valve (S-EPM) that uses transient electrical excitation to achieve nonvolatile switching of fluidic networks, enabling logic-embedded fluidics and autonomous laboratory platforms.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种新型双稳态磁阀 (S-EPM)，它利用瞬态电激励实现流体网络的非易失性切换，从而实现逻辑嵌入式流体和自主实验室平台。

Authors: Bingchao Wang, Jonah Mack, Francesco Giorgio-Serchi, Adam A. Stokes

IndustriConnect: MCP Adapters and Mock-First Evaluation for AI-Assisted Industrial Operations

Abstract (English): AI assistants can decompose multi-step workflows, but they do not natively speak industrial protocols such as Modbus, MQTT/Sparkplug B, or OPC UA, so this paper presents INDUSTRICONNECT, a prototype suite of Model Context Protocol (MCP) adapters that expose industrial operations as schema-discoverable AI tools while preserving protocol-specific connectivity and safety controls; the system uses a common response envelope and a mock-first workflow so adapter behavior can be exercised locally before connecting to plant equipment, and a deterministic benchmark covering normal, fault-injected, stress, and recovery scenarios evaluates the flagship adapters, comprising 870 runs (480 normal, 210 fault-injected, 120 stress, 60 recovery trials) and 2820 tool calls across 7 fault scenarios and 12 stress scenarios, where the normal suite achieved full success, the fault suite confirmed structured error handling with adapter-level uint16 range validation, the stress suite identified concurrency boundaries, and same-session recovery after endpoint restart is demonstrated for all three protocols, with results providing evidence spanning adapter correctness, concurrency behavior, and structured error handling for AI-assisted industrial operations.

摘要（中文）: AI 助手可以分解多步骤工作流程，但它们原生不支持诸如 Modbus、MQTT/Sparkplug B 或 OPC UA 等工业协议。因此，本文提出了 INDUSTRICONNECT，这是一个模型上下文协议 (MCP) 适配器原型套件，该套件将工业操作暴露为可进行模式发现的 AI 工具，同时保留协议特定的连接性和安全控制。该系统使用通用的响应信封和“模拟优先”的工作流程，以便在连接到工厂设备之前可以在本地执行适配器行为。一项确定性的基准测试涵盖了正常、故障注入、压力和恢复场景，对旗舰适配器进行了评估，包含了 870 次运行（480 次正常、210 次故障注入、120 次压力、60 次恢复试验）以及跨 7 个故障场景和 12 个压力场景的 2820 次工具调用。结果显示：正常套件实现了完全成功，故障套件确认了通过适配器级别的 uint16 范围验证实现的结构化错误处理，压力套件确定了并发边界，以及针对所有三种协议都演示了端点重启后的同会话恢复。这些结果提供了证据，涵盖了 AI 辅助工业操作的适配器正确性、并发行为和结构化错误处理。

TLDR: The paper presents IndustriConnect, a suite of MCP adapters enabling AI assistants to interact with industrial protocols while maintaining safety and connectivity. It uses a mock-first approach and reports successful benchmark results across normal, fault-injected, stress, and recovery scenarios.

Authors: Melwin Xavier, Melveena Jolly, Vaisakh M A, Midhun Xavier

Towards Comprehensive Real-Time Scene Understanding in Ophthalmic Surgery through Multimodal Image Fusion

Abstract (English): Purpose: The integration of multimodal imaging into operating rooms paves the way for comprehensive surgical scene understanding. In ophthalmic surgery, by now, two complementary imaging modalities are available: operating microscope (OPMI) imaging and real-time intraoperative optical coherence tomography (iOCT). This first work toward temporal OPMI and iOCT feature fusion demonstrates the potential of multimodal image processing for multi-head prediction through the example of precise instrument tracking in vitreoretinal surgery. Methods: We propose a multimodal, temporal, real-time capable network architecture to perform joint instrument detection, keypoint localization, and tool-tissue distance estimation. Our network design integrates a cross-attention fusion module to merge OPMI and iOCT image features, which are efficiently extracted via a YoloNAS and a CNN encoder, respectively. Furthermore, a region-based recurrent module leverages temporal coherence. Results: Our experiments demonstrate reliable instrument localization and keypoint detection (95.79% mAP50) and show that the incorporation of iOCT significantly improves tool-tissue distance estimation, while achieving real-time processing rates of 22.5 ms per frame. Especially for close distances to the retina (below 1 mm), the distance estimation accuracy improved from 284 $μm$ (OPMI only) to 33 $μm$ (multimodal). Conclusion: Feature fusion of multimodal imaging can enhance multi-task prediction accuracy compared to single-modality processing and real-time processing performance can be achieved through tailored network design. While our results demonstrate the potential of multi-modal processing for image-guided vitreoretinal surgery, they also underline key challenges that motivate future research toward more reliable, consistent, and comprehensive surgical scene understanding.

摘要（中文）: 目的：将多模态成像技术集成到手术室中，为全面理解手术场景提供了途径。在眼科手术中，目前有两种互补的成像方式：手术显微镜（OPMI）成像和实时术中光学相干断层扫描（iOCT）。这项关于OPMI和iOCT时序特征融合的初步工作，通过在玻璃体视网膜手术中进行精确的器械追踪这一实例，展示了多模态图像处理在多任务预测方面的潜力。 方法：我们提出了一种多模态、时序、具有实时能力的网络架构，以执行联合的器械检测、关键点定位和工具-组织距离估计。我们的网络设计集成了一个交叉注意力融合模块来合并OPMI和iOCT图像特征，这些特征分别通过YoloNAS和一个CNN编码器高效提取。此外，基于区域的循环模块利用了时间一致性。 结果：我们的实验证明了可靠的器械定位和关键点检测（95.79%的mAP50），并表明纳入iOCT显著提高了工具-组织距离估计的准确性，同时实现了每帧22.5毫秒的实时处理速度。特别是对于靠近视网膜的近距离（低于1 mm），距离估计精度从284 $μm$（仅 OPMI）提高到33 $μm$（多模态）。 结论：与单模态处理相比，多模态成像的特征融合可以提高多任务预测的准确性，并且可以通过定制的网络设计来实现实时处理性能。虽然我们的结果展示了多模态处理在图像引导的玻璃体视网膜手术中的潜力，但它们也强调了关键挑战，这些挑战激励着未来朝着更可靠、一致和全面的手术场景理解进行研究。

TLDR: This paper presents a real-time capable multimodal network for instrument tracking in ophthalmic surgery by fusing operating microscope and intraoperative optical coherence tomography images, demonstrating improved accuracy in tool-tissue distance estimation compared to single-modality processing.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种实时多模态网络，通过融合手术显微镜和术中光学相干断层扫描图像来实现眼科手术中的器械跟踪，与单模态处理相比，在工具-组织距离估计方面表现出更高的精度。

Authors: Nikolo Rohrmoser, Ghazal Ghazaei, Michael Sommersperger, Nassir Navab

Insights on back marking for the automated identification of animals

Abstract (English): To date, there is little research on how to design back marks to best support individual-level monitoring of uniform looking species like pigs. With the recent surge of machine learning-based monitoring solutions, there is a particular need for guidelines on the design of marks that can be effectively recognised by such algorithms. This study provides valuable insights on effective back mark design, based on the analysis of a machine learning model, trained to distinguish pigs via their back marks. Specifically, a neural network of type ResNet-50 was trained to classify ten pigs with unique back marks. The analysis of the model's predictions highlights the significance of certain design choices, even in controlled settings. Most importantly, the set of back marks must be designed such that each mark remains unambiguous under conditions of motion blur, diverse view angles and occlusions, caused by animal behaviour. Further, the back mark design must consider data augmentation strategies commonly employed during model training, like colour, flip and crop augmentations. The generated insights can support individual-level monitoring in future studies and real-world applications by optimizing back mark design.

摘要（中文）: 迄今为止，关于如何设计背部标记以最佳支持对外观相似物种（如猪）的个体层面监测的研究很少。随着基于机器学习的监测解决方案的近来涌现，尤其需要关于标记设计的指导原则，以便此类算法能够有效地识别这些标记。本研究基于对一个机器学习模型的分析，为有效的背部标记设计提供了有价值的见解，该模型被训练以通过背部标记来区分猪。具体来说，训练了一个ResNet-50类型的神经网络来对十头具有独特背部标记的猪进行分类。对该模型预测的分析突出了某些设计选择的重要性，即使在受控环境中也是如此。最重要的是，背部标记的集合必须经过设计，以确保每个标记在运动模糊、不同视角以及由动物行为引起的遮挡条件下保持明确。此外，背部标记设计必须考虑模型训练期间常用的数据增强策略，如颜色、翻转和裁剪增强。所产生的见解可以通过优化背部标记设计，从而支持未来研究和实际应用中的个体层面监测。

TLDR: This paper investigates the design principles for back marks on animals (specifically pigs) to optimize their recognition by machine learning models under realistic conditions like motion blur and occlusion.

简要说明: 本文研究了动物（特别是猪）背部标记的设计原则，以优化机器学习模型在运动模糊和遮挡等真实条件下的识别。

Authors: David Brunner, Marie Bordes, Elisabeth Mayrhuber, Stephan M. Winkler, Viktoria Dorfer, Maciej Oczak

ET-SAM: Efficient Point Prompt Prediction in SAM for Unified Scene Text Detection and Layout Analysis

Abstract (English): Previous works based on Segment Anything Model (SAM) have achieved promising performance in unified scene text detection and layout analysis. However, the typical reliance on pixel-level text segmentation for sampling thousands of foreground points as prompts leads to unsatisfied inference latency and limited data utilization. To address above issues, we propose ET-SAM, an Efficient framework with two decoders for unified scene Text detection and layout analysis based on SAM. Technically, we customize a lightweight point decoder that produces word heatmaps for achieving a few foreground points, thereby eliminating excessive point prompts and accelerating inference. Without the dependence on pixel-level segmentation, we further design a joint training strategy to leverage existing data with heterogeneous text-level annotations. Specifically, the datasets with multi-level, word-level only, and line-level only annotations are combined in parallel as a unified training set. For these datasets, we introduce three corresponding sets of learnable task prompts in both the point decoder and hierarchical mask decoder to mitigate discrepancies across datasets.Extensive experiments demonstrate that, compared to the previous SAM-based architecture, ET-SAM achieves about 3$\times$ inference acceleration while obtaining competitive performance on HierText, and improves an average of 11.0% F-score on Total-Text, CTW1500, and ICDAR15.

摘要（中文）: 基于分割一切模型(SAM)的先前工作在统一场景文本检测和版面分析中取得了可喜的成果。然而，典型地依赖像素级文本分割来采样数千个前景点作为提示导致了不令人满意的推理延迟和有限的数据利用率。为了解决上述问题，我们提出ET-SAM，一个高效的框架，具有两个解码器，用于基于SAM的统一场景文本检测和版面分析。在技术上，我们定制了一个轻量级的点解码器，生成单词热图以获得少量的前景点，从而消除过多的点提示并加速推理。在不依赖像素级分割的情况下，我们进一步设计了一种联合训练策略，以利用现有的具有异构文本级标注的数据。具体而言，具有多级、仅单词级和仅行级标注的数据集并行组合成一个统一的训练集。对于这些数据集，我们在点解码器和分层掩码解码器中引入了三组相应的可学习的任务提示，以缓解数据集之间的差异。大量实验表明，与之前的基于SAM的架构相比，ET-SAM实现了约3倍的推理加速，同时在HierText数据集上获得了具有竞争力的性能，并在Total-Text、CTW1500和ICDAR15数据集上平均提高了11.0%的F1值。

TLDR: The paper proposes ET-SAM, an efficient SAM-based framework for scene text detection and layout analysis, which achieves faster inference and improved performance through a lightweight point decoder and a joint training strategy leveraging diverse text annotations.

简要说明: 本文提出了ET-SAM，一个高效的基于SAM的框架，用于场景文本检测和布局分析。它通过轻量级的点解码器和联合训练策略实现了更快的推理速度和更高的性能，该策略利用了多种文本注释。

Authors: Xike Zhang, Maoyuan Ye, Juhua Liu, Bo Du

Longitudinal Digital Phenotyping for Early Cognitive-Motor Screening

Abstract (English): Early detection of atypical cognitive-motor development is critical for timely intervention, yet traditional assessments rely heavily on subjective, static evaluations. The integration of digital devices offers an opportunity for continuous, objective monitoring through digital biomarkers. In this work, we propose an AI-driven longitudinal framework to model developmental trajectories in children aged 18 months to 8 years. Using a dataset of tablet-based interactions collected over multiple academic years, we analyzed six cognitive-motor tasks (e.g., fine motor control, reaction time). We applied dimensionality reduction (t-SNE) and unsupervised clustering (K-Means++) to identify distinct developmental phenotypes and tracked individual transitions between these profiles over time. Our analysis reveals three distinct profiles: low, medium, and high performance. Crucially, longitudinal tracking highlights a high stability in the low-performance cluster (>90% retention in early years), suggesting that early deficits tend to persist without intervention. Conversely, higher-performance clusters show greater variability, potentially reflecting engagement factors. This study validates the use of unsupervised learning on touchscreen data to uncover heterogeneous developmental paths. The identified profiles serve as scalable, data-driven proxies for cognitive growth, offering a foundation for early screening tools and personalized pediatric interventions.

摘要（中文）: 异常认知-运动发育的早期检测对于及时干预至关重要，然而，传统的评估方法严重依赖于主观、静态的评价。数字设备的整合为通过数字生物标记物进行连续、客观的监测提供了机会。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种人工智能驱动的纵向框架，用于建模18个月至8岁儿童的发育轨迹。我们使用从多个学年收集的基于平板电脑交互的数据集，分析了六项认知-运动任务（例如，精细运动控制、反应时间）。我们应用了降维（t-SNE）和无监督聚类（K-Means++）来识别不同的发育表型，并追踪个体在这些表型之间随时间的转移。我们的分析揭示了三种不同的表型：低、中和高性能。至关重要的是，纵向追踪显示低性能集群具有很高的稳定性（早期保留率>90%），表明早期的缺陷在没有干预的情况下往往会持续存在。相反，更高性能的集群表现出更大的变异性，可能反映了参与因素。这项研究验证了使用触摸屏数据进行无监督学习来揭示异质性发育路径的有效性。所识别的表型可作为可扩展的、数据驱动的认知增长代理，为早期筛查工具和个性化儿科干预提供基础。

TLDR: The paper presents an AI-driven longitudinal framework using tablet-based interaction data to identify distinct cognitive-motor developmental profiles in children, showing potential for early screening and personalized interventions.

简要说明: 这篇论文提出了一个AI驱动的纵向框架，使用平板电脑交互数据来识别儿童的不同认知-运动发展概况，展示了早期筛查和个性化干预的潜力。
